METRICS_SCHEMA_VERSION = "label-system-operational-metrics-v1"
BACKUP_STATUS_FORMAT = "label-system-backup-status-v1"
RESTORE_STATUS_FORMAT = "label-system-restore-status-v1"
WORKER_LOOP_STALE_SECONDS = 60
QUEUE_AGE_ALERT_SECONDS = 900
RUNTIME_DEAD_LETTER_ALERT_SECONDS = 15 * 60
# 日备份定时器 24 小时一次，另有 RandomizedDelaySec=10min、flock 最长等 1 小时和
# 备份本身的耗时；恰好 24 小时判过期会在每天正常备份前误报。与异地副本的
# max_age_seconds（93600 = 26 小时）对齐。
BACKUP_AGE_ALERT_SECONDS = 26 * 60 * 60
MYSQL_CONNECTION_ALERT_RATIO = 0.8
MYSQL_REDO_CHECKPOINT_ALERT_RATIO = 0.8
DISK_ALERT_RATIO = 0.85
DISK_CRITICAL_RATIO = 0.95
_OUTBOX_STATUSES = frozenset(
    {"queued", "leased", "delivered", "dead_lettered", "canceled"}
)


def _utc(value: datetime) -> datetime:
    return value if value.tzinfo is not None else value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def _age_seconds(value: datetime | None, current: datetime) -> int:
    if value is None:
        return 0
    return max(0, int((_utc(current) - _utc(value)).total_seconds()))


def _worker_metrics(
    db: Session,
    *,
    expected_workers: int,
    current: datetime,
) -> dict[str, int]:
    snapshot = automation_worker_snapshot(db, now=current)
    active_workers = [
        item
        for item in snapshot["workers"]
        if isinstance(item, Mapping)
        and item.get("active") is True
        and item.get("capacity_eligible", True) is True
    ]
    # A synchronous evaluation worker ticks its loop only between jobs, and one
    # job can hold a provider wait for many minutes (2026-09-17: prompt steps
    # averaged 56 minutes on the sync worker). While it is marked "working" and
    # still owns a live step lease, the loop is busy, not stalled: that is the
    # same live-lease evidence the snapshot accepts for optimization and
    # projection workers. An expired lease or any other status stays stale.
    working_lease_owners = set(
        db.scalars(
            select(ProductionStepAttempt.lease_owner).where(
                ProductionStepAttempt.lease_owner.is_not(None),
                ProductionStepAttempt.status.in_(("leased", "running")),
                ProductionStepAttempt.lease_expires_at.is_not(None),
                ProductionStepAttempt.lease_expires_at > current,
            )
        ).all()
    )
    evaluation_stale_ids = {
        str(item.get("worker_id"))
        for item in active_workers
        if (
            item.get("last_tick_at") is None
            or _age_seconds(item.get("last_tick_at"), current)
            > WORKER_LOOP_STALE_SECONDS
        )
        and not (
            str(item.get("last_status") or "") == "working"
            and str(item.get("worker_id")) in working_lease_owners
        )
    }
    process_active_workers = [
        item
        for item in snapshot["workers"]
        if isinstance(item, Mapping) and item.get("active") is True
    ]
    auxiliary_stale_ids = {
        str(item.get("worker_id"))
        for item in process_active_workers
        if item.get("auxiliary_eligible") is True
        and item.get("auxiliary_active") is not True
    }
    optimization_stale_ids = {
        str(item.get("worker_id"))
        for item in process_active_workers
        if item.get("optimization_eligible") is True
        and item.get("optimization_active") is not True
    }
    projection_stale_ids = {
        str(item.get("worker_id"))
        for item in process_active_workers
        if item.get("projection_eligible") is True
        and item.get("projection_active") is not True
    }
    loop_stale = len(
        evaluation_stale_ids
        | auxiliary_stale_ids
        | optimization_stale_ids
        | projection_stale_ids
    )
    active = int(
        snapshot.get("active_evaluation_worker_count")
        if "active_evaluation_worker_count" in snapshot
        else snapshot.get("active_worker_count") or 0
    )
    active_auxiliary = int(
        snapshot.get("active_auxiliary_worker_count") or 0
    )
    active_optimization = int(
        snapshot.get("active_optimization_worker_count") or 0
    )
    active_projection = int(
        snapshot.get("active_projection_worker_count") or 0
    )
    expected = max(1, int(expected_workers))
    return {
        "active": active,
        "expected": expected,
        "missing": max(0, expected - active),
        "active_auxiliary": active_auxiliary,
        "missing_auxiliary": max(0, 1 - active_auxiliary),
        "active_optimization": active_optimization,
        "missing_optimization": max(0, 1 - active_optimization),
        "active_projection": active_projection,
        "missing_projection": max(0, 1 - active_projection),
        "loop_stale": loop_stale,
        "loop_stale_auxiliary": len(auxiliary_stale_ids),
        "loop_stale_optimization": len(optimization_stale_ids),
        "loop_stale_projection": len(projection_stale_ids),
        "loop_stale_after_seconds": WORKER_LOOP_STALE_SECONDS,
    }


def _queue_and_outbox_metrics(
    db: Session,
    *,
    current: datetime,
) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, int]]:
    capacity = runtime_capacity_snapshot(db, now=current)
    recent_dead_letters = {
        str(queue_class): int(count)
        for queue_class, count in db.execute(
            select(
                RuntimeDispatchItem.queue_class,
                func.count(RuntimeDispatchItem.id),
            )
            .where(
                RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "dead_lettered",
                RuntimeDispatchItem.dead_lettered_at.is_not(None),
                RuntimeDispatchItem.dead_lettered_at
                >= current
                - timedelta(seconds=RUNTIME_DEAD_LETTER_ALERT_SECONDS),
            )
            .group_by(RuntimeDispatchItem.queue_class)
        ).all()
    }
    # Retry routing moves baseline jobs into recovery; retain their origin for alerts.
    # Only a verified baseline source is exempted. Missing/other origins fail closed.
    def _baseline_recovery(*, recent_only: bool) -> int:
        query = (
            select(func.count(RuntimeDispatchItem.id))
            .join(
                ProductionStepAttempt,
                ProductionStepAttempt.id == RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id,
            )
            .join(ProductionRun, ProductionRun.id == ProductionStepAttempt.run_id)
            .where(
                RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "dead_lettered",
                RuntimeDispatchItem.queue_class == "recovery",
                ProductionRun.source_type == "baseline_regression_item",
            )
        )
        if recent_only:
            query = query.where(
                RuntimeDispatchItem.dead_lettered_at.is_not(None),
                RuntimeDispatchItem.dead_lettered_at
                >= current - timedelta(seconds=RUNTIME_DEAD_LETTER_ALERT_SECONDS),
            )
        return int(db.scalar(query) or 0)

    baseline_recovery_recent = _baseline_recovery(recent_only=True)
    queues = [
        {
            "queue_class": str(item["queue_class"]),
            "queued": int(item["queued"]),
            "leased": int(item["leased"]),
            "dead_lettered": int(item["dead_letters"]),
            "regression_dead_lettered": int(item.get("regression_dead_letters") or 0),
            "dead_lettered_last_15m": recent_dead_letters.get(
                str(item["queue_class"]),
                0,
            ),
            "oldest_queued_age_seconds": int(
                item["oldest_queued_age_seconds"]
            ),
        }
        for item in capacity["queues"]
    ]
    for queue in queues:
        # recovery 队列里基线回归的死信从 09-18 起已整体不计入 dead_lettered（见
        # runtime_capacity.REGRESSION_SOURCE_TYPE，单列在 regression_dead_lettered）。
        # 这里不能再减一次：否则每有一条回归死信就会抵掉一条真实生产死信，积压告警失灵。
        validation_count = (
            queue["dead_lettered"]
            if queue["queue_class"] == "validation"
            else 0
        )
        queue["validation_dead_lettered"] = validation_count
        queue["blocking_dead_lettered"] = max(
            0, queue["dead_lettered"] - validation_count
        )
        # 发布门只看最近 15 分钟新产生的阻塞死信；历史积压另计（见 evaluate_operational_alerts）。
        recent_validation = (
            queue["dead_lettered_last_15m"]
            if queue["queue_class"] == "validation"
            else min(queue["dead_lettered_last_15m"], baseline_recovery_recent)
            if queue["queue_class"] == "recovery"
            else 0
        )
        queue["blocking_dead_lettered_last_15m"] = max(
            0, queue["dead_lettered_last_15m"] - recent_validation
        )
    outbox = capacity["outbox"]
    unknown = int(
        db.scalar(
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
            .where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.status.not_in(_OUTBOX_STATUSES))
        )
        or 0
    )
    queued = int(outbox["queued"])
    leased = int(outbox["leased"])
    return queues, {
        "queued": queued,
        "leased": leased,
        "pending": queued + leased,
        "dead_lettered": int(outbox["dead_letters"]),
        "unknown": unknown,
        "oldest_queued_age_seconds": int(
            outbox["oldest_queued_age_seconds"]
        ),
    }


def _provider_metrics(db: Session, *, current: datetime) -> dict[str, Any]:
    recent = current - timedelta(minutes=15)
    confirmed_slots = int(
        db.scalar(
            select(func.sum(ModelQuotaPool.max_concurrency)).where(
                ModelQuotaPool.enabled.is_(True)
            )
        )
        or 0
    )
    active_leases = int(
        db.scalar(
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(ModelQuotaLease)
            .where(
                ModelQuotaLease.status == "active",
                ModelQuotaLease.expires_at > current,
            )
        )
        or 0
    )
    return {
        "http_429_last_15m": int(
            db.scalar(
                select(func.count())
                .select_from(WorkflowModelInvocation)
                .where(
                    WorkflowModelInvocation.upstream_status_code == 429,
                    WorkflowModelInvocation.created_at >= recent,
                )
            )
            or 0
        ),
        "cooldown_pools": int(
            db.scalar(
                select(func.count())
                .select_from(ModelQuotaPool)
                .where(
                    ModelQuotaPool.enabled.is_(True),
                    ModelQuotaPool.cooldown_until.is_not(None),
                    ModelQuotaPool.cooldown_until > current,
                )
            )
            or 0
        ),
        "timeouts_last_15m": int(
            db.scalar(
                select(func.count())
                .select_from(WorkflowModelInvocation)
                .where(
                    WorkflowModelInvocation.error_code.in_(
                        ("timeout", "model_provider_timeout")
                    ),
                    WorkflowModelInvocation.created_at >= recent,
                )
            )
            or 0
        ),
        "confirmed_slots": confirmed_slots,
        "active_leases": active_leases,
        "slot_utilization_ratio": (
            round(active_leases / confirmed_slots, 6)
            if confirmed_slots > 0
            else None
        ),
        "expired_active_leases": int(
            db.scalar(
                select(func.count())
                .select_from(ModelQuotaLease)
                .where(
                    ModelQuotaLease.status == "active",
                    ModelQuotaLease.expires_at <= current,
                )
            )
            or 0
        ),
    }


def _business_date(
    policy: HumanReviewQuotaPolicy | None,
    current: datetime,
) -> date:
    zone_name = policy.timezone_name if policy is not None else "Asia/Shanghai"
    try:
        zone = ZoneInfo(zone_name)
    except ZoneInfoNotFoundError:
        zone = ZoneInfo("Asia/Shanghai")
    return _utc(current).astimezone(zone).date()


def _human_review_metrics(db: Session, *, current: datetime) -> dict[str, Any]:
    policy = db.scalar(
        select(HumanReviewQuotaPolicy).where(
            HumanReviewQuotaPolicy.status == "active",
            HumanReviewQuotaPolicy.active_slot == "global",
        )
    )
    business_date = _business_date(policy, current)
    batch = db.scalar(
        select(HumanReviewDailyBatch).where(
            HumanReviewDailyBatch.business_date == business_date
        )
    )
    capacity = int(
        batch.capacity
        if batch is not None
        else policy.total_capacity
        if policy is not None
        else 0
    )
    if batch is None:
        return {
            "business_date": business_date.isoformat(),
            "capacity": capacity,
            "selected": 0,
            "reviewed": 0,
            "pending": 0,
            "overflow": 0,
            "severe_overflow": 0,
            "status": "not_generated",
        }
    reviewed = int(
        db.scalar(
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(HumanReviewDailyBatchItem)
            .where(
                HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.batch_id == batch.id,
                HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.selection_state == "selected_reviewed",
            )
        )
        or 0
    )
    pending = int(
        db.scalar(
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(HumanReviewDailyBatchItem)
            .where(
                HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.batch_id == batch.id,
                HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.selection_state == "selected_pending",
            )
        )
        or 0
    )
    severe = int(
        db.scalar(
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(HumanReviewOverflowFact)
            .where(
                HumanReviewOverflowFact.batch_id == batch.id,
                HumanReviewOverflowFact.severity == "severe",
            )
        )
        or 0
    )
    return {
        "business_date": business_date.isoformat(),
        "capacity": capacity,
        "selected": int(batch.selected_count),
        "reviewed": reviewed,
        "pending": pending,
        "overflow": int(batch.overflow_count),
        "severe_overflow": severe,
        "status": str(batch.status),
    }


def _database_metrics(db: Session) -> dict[str, Any]:
    dialect = db.get_bind().dialect.name
    result: dict[str, Any] = {
        "dialect": dialect,
        "available": True,
        "threads_connected": None,
        "max_connections": None,
        "connection_utilization_ratio": None,
        "innodb_log_waits_total": None,
        "innodb_buffer_pool_wait_free_total": None,
        "innodb_redo_log_written_bytes_total": None,
        "innodb_redo_log_checkpoint_age_bytes": None,
        "innodb_redo_log_capacity_bytes": None,
        "innodb_redo_log_checkpoint_utilization_ratio": None,
        "innodb_buffer_pool_size_bytes": None,
    }
    if dialect != "mysql":
        return result
    try:
        statuses = {
            str(name): int(value)
            for name, value in db.execute(
                text(
                    "SHOW GLOBAL STATUS WHERE Variable_name IN ("
                    "'Threads_connected',"
                    "'Innodb_log_waits',"
                    "'Innodb_buffer_pool_wait_free',"
                    "'Innodb_os_log_written',"
                    "'Innodb_redo_log_checkpoint_lsn',"
                    "'Innodb_redo_log_current_lsn')"
                )
            ).all()
        }
        maximum, redo_capacity, buffer_pool_size = (
            int(value)
            for value in db.execute(
                text(
                    "SELECT @@GLOBAL.max_connections, "
                    "@@GLOBAL.innodb_redo_log_capacity, "
                    "@@GLOBAL.innodb_buffer_pool_size"
                )
            ).one()
        )
        threads = statuses["Threads_connected"]
        checkpoint_age = max(
            0,
            statuses["Innodb_redo_log_current_lsn"]
            - statuses["Innodb_redo_log_checkpoint_lsn"],
        )
    except (SQLAlchemyError, IndexError, KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
        result["available"] = False
        return result
    result.update(
        {
            "threads_connected": threads,
            "max_connections": maximum,
            "connection_utilization_ratio": round(threads / maximum, 6)
            if maximum > 0
            else None,
            "innodb_log_waits_total": statuses["Innodb_log_waits"],
            "innodb_buffer_pool_wait_free_total": statuses[
                "Innodb_buffer_pool_wait_free"
            ],
            "innodb_redo_log_written_bytes_total": statuses[
                "Innodb_os_log_written"
            ],
            "innodb_redo_log_checkpoint_age_bytes": checkpoint_age,
            "innodb_redo_log_capacity_bytes": redo_capacity,
            "innodb_redo_log_checkpoint_utilization_ratio": round(
                checkpoint_age / redo_capacity,
                6,
            )
            if redo_capacity > 0
            else None,
            "innodb_buffer_pool_size_bytes": buffer_pool_size,
        }
    )
    return result


def _storage_metrics(data_root: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        usage = shutil.disk_usage(data_root)
    except OSError:
        return {
            "available": False,
            "total_bytes": None,
            "free_bytes": None,
            "used_ratio": None,
        }
    return {
        "available": True,
        "total_bytes": int(usage.total),
        "free_bytes": int(usage.free),
        "used_ratio": round(usage.used / usage.total, 6)
        if usage.total > 0
        else None,
    }


def _backup_metrics(
    backup_status_file: Path | None,
    *,
    current: datetime,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if backup_status_file is None:
        return {"configured": False, "available": False, "age_seconds": None}
    path = backup_status_file.expanduser().absolute()
    if path.is_symlink() or not path.is_file():
        return {"configured": True, "available": False, "age_seconds": None}
    try:
        raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        if len(raw.encode("utf-8")) > 4096:
            raise ValueError("backup status is too large")
        value = json.loads(raw)
        if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
            raise ValueError("backup status is not an object")
        if value.get("format") != BACKUP_STATUS_FORMAT:
            raise ValueError("backup status format is invalid")
        source_schema = value.get("source_schema")
        manifest_hash = value.get("manifest_sha256")
        completed_at = value.get("completed_at")
        if (
            not isinstance(source_schema, str)
            or re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,63}", source_schema)
            is None
            or not isinstance(manifest_hash, str)
            or re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", manifest_hash) is None
            or not isinstance(completed_at, str)
        ):
            raise ValueError("backup status fields are invalid")
        completed = datetime.fromisoformat(completed_at.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
        if completed.tzinfo is None or _utc(completed) > _utc(current) + timedelta(minutes=5):
            raise ValueError("backup completion time is invalid")
    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError):
        return {"configured": True, "available": False, "age_seconds": None}
    return {
        "configured": True,
        "available": True,
        "age_seconds": _age_seconds(completed, current),
    }


def _restore_metrics(
    restore_status_file: Path | None,
    *,
    current: datetime,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    unavailable = {
        "configured": restore_status_file is not None,
        "available": False,
        "age_seconds": None,
        "restored_tables": None,
        "restore_seconds": None,
    }
    if restore_status_file is None:
        return unavailable
    path = restore_status_file.expanduser().absolute()
    if path.is_symlink() or not path.is_file():
        return unavailable
    try:
        raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        if len(raw.encode("utf-8")) > 4096:
            raise ValueError("restore status is too large")
        value = json.loads(raw)
        if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
            raise ValueError("restore status is not an object")
        completed_at = value.get("completed_at")
        manifest_hash = value.get("manifest_sha256")
        source_schema = value.get("source_schema")
        target_schema = value.get("target_schema")
        restored_tables = int(value.get("restored_tables") or 0)
        restore_seconds = float(value.get("restore_seconds") or 0)
        if (
            value.get("format") != RESTORE_STATUS_FORMAT
            or value.get("completed") is not True
            or not isinstance(completed_at, str)
            or not isinstance(manifest_hash, str)
            or re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", manifest_hash) is None
            or not isinstance(source_schema, str)
            or re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,63}", source_schema)
            is None
            or not isinstance(target_schema, str)
            or re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,63}", target_schema)
            is None
            or restored_tables < 1
            or restore_seconds < 0
        ):
            raise ValueError("restore status fields are invalid")
        completed = datetime.fromisoformat(completed_at.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
        if (
            completed.tzinfo is None
            or _utc(completed) > _utc(current) + timedelta(minutes=5)
        ):
            raise ValueError("restore completion time is invalid")
    except (
        OSError,
        UnicodeDecodeError,
        json.JSONDecodeError,
        TypeError,
        ValueError,
    ):
        return unavailable
    return {
        "configured": True,
        "available": True,
        "age_seconds": _age_seconds(completed, current),
        "restored_tables": restored_tables,
        "restore_seconds": restore_seconds,
    }


def _recent_blocking_dead_letters(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> int:
    """最近 15 分钟内新产生、且不属于验证/回归来源的死信数。

    新快照直接带 ``blocking_dead_lettered_last_15m``；老快照只有按队列的 15 分钟计数，
    这时 validation 队列按 0 算、其余队列按全部近期死信算（保守，宁可多拦）。
    """
    # 已被处理（重放/放弃）的死信不再是 dead_lettered，当前总数就是上界。
    ceiling = max(0, int(item.get("dead_lettered") or 0))
    explicit = item.get("blocking_dead_lettered_last_15m")
    if explicit is not None:
        return min(ceiling, max(0, int(explicit or 0)))
    recent = int(item.get("dead_lettered_last_15m") or 0)
    return 0 if item.get("queue_class") == "validation" else min(ceiling, recent)


def _dead_letter_counts(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[int, int]:
    total = int(item.get("dead_lettered") or 0)
    # Older snapshots have no provenance counters; preserve conservative routing.
    validation = int(
        item.get(
            "validation_dead_lettered",
            total if item.get("queue_class") == "validation" else 0,
        )
        or 0
    )
    validation = max(0, min(total, validation))
    return total - validation, validation


def evaluate_operational_alerts(
    snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    alerts: dict[str, str] = {}

    def add(code: str, severity: str) -> None:
        previous = alerts.get(code)
        if previous != "critical":
            alerts[code] = severity

    workers = snapshot.get("workers", {})
    if int(workers.get("missing") or 0) > 0:
        add("worker_missing", "critical")
    if int(workers.get("loop_stale") or 0) > 0:
        add("worker_loop_stale", "critical")
    if int(workers.get("missing_auxiliary") or 0) > 0:
        add("auxiliary_worker_missing", "critical")
    if int(workers.get("missing_optimization") or 0) > 0:
        add("optimization_worker_missing", "critical")
    if int(workers.get("missing_projection") or 0) > 0:
        add("projection_worker_missing", "critical")

    queues = snapshot.get("queues", [])

    # Physical queue counters remain intact. Provenance-aware counts distinguish
    # regression evidence from production failures, including retry routing.
    # 2026-09-16 老大裁决改口径：critical 只看最近 15 分钟新产生的阻塞死信——发布门要回答的是
    # "现在是不是在坏"，不是"历史上有没有坏过"。此前 recovery 队列 9 月 7–15 日实验期留下的
    # 35 条死信让之后每一轮发布都在 verify-operations 被拦下。历史积压仍可见，降为 warning。
    if any(_recent_blocking_dead_letters(item) > 0 for item in queues):
        add("runtime_dead_letter", "critical")
    elif any(_dead_letter_counts(item)[0] > 0 for item in queues):
        add("runtime_dead_letter_backlog", "warning")
    if any(_dead_letter_counts(item)[1] > 0 for item in queues):
        add("validation_dead_letter", "warning")
    if any(
        int(item.get("oldest_queued_age_seconds") or 0)
        >= QUEUE_AGE_ALERT_SECONDS
        for item in queues
    ):
        add("queue_oldest_age_high", "warning")

    outbox = snapshot.get("outbox", {})
    if int(outbox.get("dead_lettered") or 0) > 0:
        add("outbox_dead_letter", "critical")
    if int(outbox.get("unknown") or 0) > 0:
        add("outbox_unknown_status", "critical")
    if int(outbox.get("oldest_queued_age_seconds") or 0) >= QUEUE_AGE_ALERT_SECONDS:
        add("outbox_oldest_age_high", "critical")

    provider = snapshot.get("provider", {})
    if int(provider.get("http_429_last_15m") or 0) > 0:
        add("provider_429", "warning")
    if int(provider.get("timeouts_last_15m") or 0) > 0:
        add("provider_timeout", "warning")
    if int(provider.get("cooldown_pools") or 0) > 0:
        add("provider_cooldown", "warning")
    if int(provider.get("expired_active_leases") or 0) > 0:
        add("provider_lease_expired", "critical")
    slot_ratio = provider.get("slot_utilization_ratio")
    if slot_ratio is not None and float(slot_ratio) >= 1:
        add("provider_pool_exhausted", "warning")

    review = snapshot.get("human_review", {})
    capacity = int(review.get("capacity") or 0)
    selected = int(review.get("selected") or 0)
    if capacity <= 0:
        add("human_review_quota_missing", "critical")
    elif selected >= capacity:
        add(
            "human_review_capacity_exhausted",
            "critical" if selected > capacity else "warning",
        )
    if int(review.get("severe_overflow") or 0) > 0:
        add("human_review_severe_overflow", "critical")

    database = snapshot.get("database", {})
    if database.get("dialect") == "mysql" and not database.get("available"):
        add("mysql_metrics_unavailable", "critical")
    connection_ratio = database.get("connection_utilization_ratio")
    if connection_ratio is not None and float(connection_ratio) >= MYSQL_CONNECTION_ALERT_RATIO:
        add("mysql_connections_high", "warning")
    redo_checkpoint_ratio = database.get(
        "innodb_redo_log_checkpoint_utilization_ratio"
    )
    if (
        redo_checkpoint_ratio is not None
        and float(redo_checkpoint_ratio)
        >= MYSQL_REDO_CHECKPOINT_ALERT_RATIO
    ):
        add("mysql_redo_checkpoint_pressure", "critical")

    storage = snapshot.get("storage", {})
    if not storage.get("available"):
        add("data_disk_metrics_unavailable", "critical")
    used_ratio = storage.get("used_ratio")
    if used_ratio is not None and float(used_ratio) >= DISK_ALERT_RATIO:
        add(
            "data_disk_usage_high",
            "critical" if float(used_ratio) >= DISK_CRITICAL_RATIO else "warning",
        )

    backup = snapshot.get("backup", {})
    if not backup.get("configured") or not backup.get("available"):
        add("backup_missing", "critical")
    elif int(backup.get("age_seconds") or 0) >= BACKUP_AGE_ALERT_SECONDS:
        add("backup_stale", "critical")

    restore = snapshot.get("restore", {})
    if not restore.get("configured") or not restore.get("available"):
        add("restore_drill_missing", "critical")

    billing = snapshot.get("volcengine_billing", {})
    if billing.get("sync_enabled"):
        failures = int(billing.get("consecutive_failures") or 0)
        if failures >= 3:
            add("volcengine_billing_sync_failed", "critical")
        elif failures >= 1:
            add("volcengine_billing_sync_failed", "warning")
        interval = int(billing.get("sync_interval_seconds") or 3600)
        age = billing.get("last_success_age_seconds")
        if age is None or int(age) >= interval * 3:
            add("volcengine_billing_sync_stale", "critical")
        if int(billing.get("overdue_open_days") or 0) > 0:
            add("volcengine_billing_freeze_stalled", "warning")
    if int(billing.get("frozen_mismatch_days_7d") or 0) > 0:
        add("volcengine_billing_reconcile_mismatch", "warning")

    return [
        {"code": code, "severity": alerts[code]}
        for code in sorted(alerts)
    ]


def _billing_metrics(db: Session, *, current: datetime) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from .volcengine_billing import billing_health

    try:
        return billing_health(db, now=current)
    except SQLAlchemyError:
        # 旧库尚未升级到 schema 107 时不让整个指标页失效。
        return {"configured": False, "sync_enabled": False, "endpoint_routing_enabled": False,
            "consecutive_failures": 0, "frozen_mismatch_days_7d": 0, "overdue_open_days": 0,
            "last_success_age_seconds": None, "sync_interval_seconds": None, "last_error": "", "available": False}


def operational_metrics_snapshot(
    db: Session,
    *,
    expected_workers: int,
    data_root: Path,
    backup_status_file: Path | None,
    restore_status_file: Path | None = None,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    current = _utc(now or datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    queues, outbox = _queue_and_outbox_metrics(db, current=current)
    queue_depth = sum(
        int(item["queued"]) + int(item["leased"]) for item in queues
    )
    result: dict[str, Any] = {
        "schema_version": METRICS_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "generated_at": platform_isoformat(current),
        "workers": _worker_metrics(
            db,
            expected_workers=expected_workers,
            current=current,
        ),
        "queues": queues,
        "queue_depth": queue_depth,
        "outbox": outbox,
        "provider": _provider_metrics(db, current=current),
        "human_review": _human_review_metrics(db, current=current),
        "database": _database_metrics(db),
        "storage": _storage_metrics(data_root),
        "backup": _backup_metrics(backup_status_file, current=current),
        "restore": _restore_metrics(restore_status_file, current=current),
        "volcengine_billing": _billing_metrics(db, current=current),
    }
    result["alerts"] = evaluate_operational_alerts(result)
    result["status"] = (
        "critical"
        if any(item["severity"] == "critical" for item in result["alerts"])
        else "warning"
        if result["alerts"]
        else "ready"
    )
    return result


def prometheus_metrics(snapshot: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    workers = snapshot["workers"]
    outbox = snapshot["outbox"]
    provider = snapshot["provider"]
    review = snapshot["human_review"]
    database = snapshot["database"]
    storage = snapshot["storage"]
    backup = snapshot["backup"]
    restore = snapshot.get(
        "restore",
        {"available": False, "age_seconds": None},
    )
    lines = [
        "# TYPE label_system_workers_active gauge",
        f"label_system_workers_active {int(workers['active'])}",
        "# TYPE label_system_workers_expected gauge",
        f"label_system_workers_expected {int(workers['expected'])}",
        "# TYPE label_system_workers_missing gauge",
        f"label_system_workers_missing {int(workers['missing'])}",
        "# TYPE label_system_worker_loops_stale gauge",
        f"label_system_worker_loops_stale {int(workers['loop_stale'])}",
    ]
    for queue in snapshot["queues"]:
        queue_class = str(queue["queue_class"])
        lines.extend(
            [
                f'label_system_queue_queued{{queue_class="{queue_class}"}} {int(queue["queued"])}',
                f'label_system_queue_leased{{queue_class="{queue_class}"}} {int(queue["leased"])}',
                f'label_system_queue_dead_lettered{{queue_class="{queue_class}"}} {int(queue["dead_lettered"])}',
                f'label_system_queue_blocking_dead_lettered{{queue_class="{queue_class}"}} {_dead_letter_counts(queue)[0]}',
                f'label_system_queue_validation_dead_lettered{{queue_class="{queue_class}"}} {_dead_letter_counts(queue)[1]}',
                f'label_system_queue_dead_lettered_last_15m{{queue_class="{queue_class}"}} {int(queue["dead_lettered_last_15m"])}',
                f'label_system_queue_oldest_age_seconds{{queue_class="{queue_class}"}} {int(queue["oldest_queued_age_seconds"])}',
            ]
        )
    lines.extend(
        [
            f"label_system_queue_depth {int(snapshot.get('queue_depth') or 0)}",
            f"label_system_outbox_pending {int(outbox['pending'])}",
            f"label_system_outbox_dead_lettered {int(outbox['dead_lettered'])}",
            f"label_system_outbox_unknown {int(outbox['unknown'])}",
            f"label_system_outbox_oldest_age_seconds {int(outbox['oldest_queued_age_seconds'])}",
            f"label_system_provider_429_last_15m {int(provider['http_429_last_15m'])}",
            f"label_system_provider_timeout_last_15m {int(provider.get('timeouts_last_15m') or 0)}",
            f"label_system_provider_cooldown_pools {int(provider['cooldown_pools'])}",
            f"label_system_provider_confirmed_slots {int(provider.get('confirmed_slots') or 0)}",
            f"label_system_provider_active_leases {int(provider['active_leases'])}",
            f"label_system_provider_expired_active_leases {int(provider['expired_active_leases'])}",
            f"label_system_human_review_capacity {int(review['capacity'])}",
            "label_system_human_review_quota_configured "
            f"{1 if int(review['capacity']) > 0 else 0}",
            f"label_system_human_review_selected {int(review['selected'])}",
            f"label_system_human_review_pending {int(review['pending'])}",
            f"label_system_human_review_severe_overflow {int(review['severe_overflow'])}",
            f"label_system_backup_available {1 if backup['available'] else 0}",
            f"label_system_restore_available {1 if restore['available'] else 0}",
            "label_system_mysql_metrics_available "
            f"{1 if database.get('available') else 0}",
            "label_system_data_disk_metrics_available "
            f"{1 if storage.get('available') else 0}",
            f"label_system_alerts_critical {sum(1 for item in snapshot['alerts'] if item['severity'] == 'critical')}",
            f"label_system_alerts_warning {sum(1 for item in snapshot['alerts'] if item['severity'] == 'warning')}",
        ]
    )
    if backup.get("age_seconds") is not None:
        lines.append(f"label_system_backup_age_seconds {int(backup['age_seconds'])}")
    if restore.get("age_seconds") is not None:
        lines.append(
            f"label_system_restore_age_seconds {int(restore['age_seconds'])}"
        )
    if provider.get("slot_utilization_ratio") is not None:
        lines.append(
            "label_system_provider_slot_utilization_ratio "
            f"{float(provider['slot_utilization_ratio']):.6f}"
        )
    if database.get("connection_utilization_ratio") is not None:
        lines.append(
            "label_system_mysql_connection_utilization_ratio "
            f"{float(database['connection_utilization_ratio']):.6f}"
        )
    mysql_metric_names = {
        "innodb_log_waits_total": (
            "label_system_mysql_innodb_log_waits_total",
            int,
        ),
        "innodb_buffer_pool_wait_free_total": (
            "label_system_mysql_innodb_buffer_pool_wait_free_total",
            int,
        ),
        "innodb_redo_log_written_bytes_total": (
            "label_system_mysql_innodb_redo_log_written_bytes_total",
            int,
        ),
        "innodb_redo_log_checkpoint_age_bytes": (
            "label_system_mysql_innodb_redo_log_checkpoint_age_bytes",
            int,
        ),
        "innodb_redo_log_capacity_bytes": (
            "label_system_mysql_innodb_redo_log_capacity_bytes",
            int,
        ),
        "innodb_buffer_pool_size_bytes": (
            "label_system_mysql_innodb_buffer_pool_size_bytes",
            int,
        ),
        "innodb_redo_log_checkpoint_utilization_ratio": (
            "label_system_mysql_innodb_redo_log_checkpoint_utilization_ratio",
            lambda value: f"{float(value):.6f}",
        ),
    }
    for field, (name, render) in mysql_metric_names.items():
        value = database.get(field)
        if value is not None:
            lines.append(f"{name} {render(value)}")
    if storage.get("used_ratio") is not None:
        lines.append(
            "label_system_data_disk_used_ratio "
            f"{float(storage['used_ratio']):.6f}"
        )
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"



class DeadLetterDiscardRequest(BaseModel):
    queue_class: str | None = Field(default=None, pattern=r"^[a-z_]{3,30}$")
    ids: list[int] | None = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=500)
    note: str = Field(default="", max_length=200)


class DeadLetterRetryRequest(BaseModel):
    """运营对运行时死信的「重试」：按调度条目选，实际按 run 去重后做 checkpoint 重试。"""

    ids: list[int] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=500)
    note: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)


class OutboxDeadLetterActionRequest(BaseModel):
    """下游投递死信的人工处置。

    与运行时调度死信不同，这一类**可以重放**：投递是幂等的（业务幂等键 + 修订），
    重放不会产生第二条下游记录。所以 reason 是必填的——放弃和重放都改变下游事实，
    运营得说清为什么，审计里留得下。
    """

    event_ids: list[int] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)

def build_operational_metrics_router(
    require_admin: Callable[..., Any],
    *,
    require_read: Callable[..., Any] | None = None,
    settings_provider: Callable[[], Settings] = get_settings,
    now: Callable[[], datetime] = lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc),
) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(tags=["operations-metrics"])
    read_user = require_read or require_admin

    def snapshot(db: Session) -> dict[str, Any]:
        settings = settings_provider()
        expected_workers = int(
            settings.worker_cluster_expected_count
            or settings.worker_process_count
            or 1
        )
        return operational_metrics_snapshot(
            db,
            expected_workers=expected_workers,
            data_root=settings.data_dir,
            backup_status_file=settings.backup_status_file,
            restore_status_file=settings.restore_status_file,
            now=now(),
        )

    @router.get("/api/operations/metrics")
    def admin_metrics(
        _user: User = Depends(read_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return snapshot(db)

    @router.get("/api/operations/dead-letters")
    def list_dead_letters(
        _user: User = Depends(read_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """当前仍处于死信状态的调度条目（实时口径，不做时间窗）。"""
        rows = db.execute(
            select(RuntimeDispatchItem)
            .where(RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "dead_lettered")
            .order_by(RuntimeDispatchItem.dead_lettered_at.asc(), RuntimeDispatchItem.id.asc())
        ).scalars().all()
        queues: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
        attempt_ids = [row.step_attempt_id for row in rows if row.step_attempt_id is not None]
        run_by_attempt: dict[int, tuple[int, str, str, str]] = {}
        if attempt_ids:
            for attempt_id, run_id, run_key, run_status, source_type in db.execute(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt.id, ProductionRun.id, ProductionRun.run_key, ProductionRun.status, ProductionRun.source_type)
                .join(ProductionRun, ProductionRun.id == ProductionStepAttempt.run_id)
                .where(ProductionStepAttempt.id.in_(attempt_ids))
            ):
                run_by_attempt[int(attempt_id)] = (int(run_id), str(run_key), str(run_status), str(source_type or ""))
        # 存量回归的死信是实验数据：重跑一轮新 run 就够了，没有「按条人工重试」这回事，
        # 所以它们既不进「失败待处理」，也不再把清单吐给前端（曾经 648 个运行号平铺在卡片正文里）。
        regression_rows = [
            row for row in rows
            if row.step_attempt_id is not None
            and run_by_attempt.get(int(row.step_attempt_id), ("", "", "", ""))[3] == "baseline_regression_item"
        ]
        regression_ids = {row.id for row in regression_rows}
        rows = [row for row in rows if row.id not in regression_ids
                and run_by_attempt.get(int(row.step_attempt_id or 0), ("", "", "", ""))[2] != "succeeded"]
        for row in rows:
            bucket = queues.setdefault(
                str(row.queue_class),
                {"queue_class": str(row.queue_class), "count": 0, "oldest_dead_lettered_at": None, "items": []},
            )
            bucket["count"] += 1
            if bucket["oldest_dead_lettered_at"] is None and row.dead_lettered_at is not None:
                bucket["oldest_dead_lettered_at"] = platform_isoformat(row.dead_lettered_at)
            if len(bucket["items"]) < 50:
                run_ref = run_by_attempt.get(int(row.step_attempt_id)) if row.step_attempt_id is not None else None
                bucket["items"].append(
                    {
                        "id": row.id,
                        "step_attempt_id": row.step_attempt_id,
                        "attempt_count": row.attempt_count,
                        "last_error_code": row.last_error_code,
                        "dead_lettered_at": platform_isoformat(row.dead_lettered_at) if row.dead_lettered_at else None,
                        # 运营按 run 认素材；重试也是按 run 去重（同一 run 可能有多条调度死信）
                        "run_id": run_ref[0] if run_ref else None,
                        "run_key": run_ref[1] if run_ref else None,
                        "run_status": run_ref[2] if run_ref else None,
                    }
                )
        outbox_rows = db.execute(
            select(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
            .where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.status == "dead_lettered")
            .order_by(RuntimeOutboxEvent.dead_lettered_at.asc(), RuntimeOutboxEvent.id.asc())
        ).scalars().all()
        outbox = [
            {
                "id": row.id,
                "target_contract_key": row.target_contract_key,
                "business_idempotency_key": row.business_idempotency_key,
                "revision": row.revision,
                "attempt_count": row.attempt_count,
                "last_error_code": row.last_error_code,
                "dead_lettered_at": platform_isoformat(row.dead_lettered_at) if row.dead_lettered_at else None,
            }
            for row in outbox_rows[:200]
        ]
        # runtime 的键保持原样：既有调用方（发布闸门、verify 脚本）按老形状读。
        return {
            "total": len(rows),
            "queues": list(queues.values()),
            "outbox": {"total": len(outbox_rows), "items": outbox},
        }

    @router.post("/api/operations/dead-letters/discard")
    def discard_dead_letters(
        payload: DeadLetterDiscardRequest,
        user: User = Depends(require_admin),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """把当前死信标记为已放弃（canceled），告警随之消失；写审计。

        只允许作用于仍处于 dead_lettered 的条目；不会重排、不会改写运行结果。
        """
        query = select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "dead_lettered")
        if payload.queue_class is not None:
            query = query.where(RuntimeDispatchItem.queue_class == payload.queue_class)
        if payload.ids:
            query = query.where(RuntimeDispatchItem.id.in_(payload.ids))
        rows = db.execute(query).scalars().all()
        if not rows:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="没有匹配的死信条目")
        discarded = []
        for row in rows:
            row.status = "canceled"
            discarded.append(row.id)
        db.flush()
        from .audit import append_audit_event

        append_audit_event(
            db,
            category="operations",
            action="dead_letters_discarded",
            subject_type="runtime_dispatch_items",
            subject_id=payload.queue_class or "all",
            actor=str(getattr(user, "username", "") or "admin"),
            payload={"ids": discarded, "queue_class": payload.queue_class, "note": payload.note},
        )
        db.commit()
        return {"discarded": len(discarded), "ids": discarded}

    @router.post("/api/operations/dead-letters/retry")
    def retry_dead_letters(
        payload: DeadLetterRetryRequest,
        user: User = Depends(require_admin),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """运营看完信息只有一个动作：重试。按 run 去重做 checkpoint 重试（只重跑失败节点，
        成功节点不重放不重复计费），并重开传输窗口（新一代批量机会 + 24 小时时钟，
        见 record_operator_retry）。该 run 名下仍是死信的调度条目一并标 canceled（已被新尝试取代），
        避免列表里继续显示。逐条留结果，一条失败不影响其余。
        """
        from .audit import append_audit_event
        from .workflow_runtime import WorkflowRuntimeError, retry_failed_run_from_checkpoint

        rows = db.execute(
            select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "dead_lettered", RuntimeDispatchItem.id.in_(payload.ids)
            )
        ).scalars().all()
        if not rows:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="没有匹配的死信条目")
        actor = str(getattr(user, "username", "") or "admin")
        run_for_item: dict[int, int | None] = {}
        for row in rows:
            run_id = (
                db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id).where(ProductionStepAttempt.id == row.step_attempt_id))
                if row.step_attempt_id is not None else None
            )
            run_for_item[row.id] = int(run_id) if run_id is not None else None
        results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        retried_runs: list[int] = []
        for run_id in sorted({rid for rid in run_for_item.values() if rid is not None}):
            item_ids = sorted(item_id for item_id, rid in run_for_item.items() if rid == run_id)
            run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            if run is None or run.status != "failed":
                results.append({"run_id": run_id, "ids": item_ids, "ok": False, "code": "run_retry_state_invalid",
                                "error": "只有 failed 运行可以人工重试"})
                continue
            try:
                with db.begin_nested():
                    retry_failed_run_from_checkpoint(db, run_id, actor=actor)
                    superseded = db.execute(
                        select(RuntimeDispatchItem)
                        .join(ProductionStepAttempt, ProductionStepAttempt.id == RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id)
                        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id, RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "dead_lettered")
                    ).scalars().all()
                    for item in superseded:
                        item.status = "canceled"
                    db.flush()
            except WorkflowRuntimeError as exc:
                results.append({"run_id": run_id, "ids": item_ids, "ok": False, "code": exc.code, "error": str(exc)})
                continue
            retried_runs.append(run_id)
            results.append({"run_id": run_id, "run_key": run.run_key, "ids": item_ids, "ok": True,
                            "superseded": sorted(item.id for item in superseded)})
        for item_id, rid in run_for_item.items():
            if rid is None:
                results.append({"run_id": None, "ids": [item_id], "ok": False, "code": "run_missing",
                                "error": "调度条目没有对应的运行"})
        append_audit_event(
            db,
            category="operations",
            action="dead_letters_retried",
            subject_type="runtime_dispatch_items",
            subject_id="all",
            actor=actor,
            payload={"ids": sorted(payload.ids), "runs": retried_runs, "note": payload.note,
                     "failed": [r for r in results if not r["ok"]][:100]},
        )
        db.commit()
        return {
            "ok_count": len(retried_runs),
            "failed_count": sum(1 for r in results if not r["ok"]),
            "runs": retried_runs,
            "results": results,
        }

    def _outbox_action(payload, user, db, *, replay: bool) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """逐条各自成败：一条处置不了不该把其余的一起拖住，运营要看到具体是哪几条。"""
        from .connector_delivery import (
            ConnectorDeliveryError,
            discard_dead_letter_delivery,
            replay_dead_letter_delivery,
        )

        action = replay_dead_letter_delivery if replay else discard_dead_letter_delivery
        actor = str(getattr(user, "username", "") or "admin")
        results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for event_id in payload.event_ids:
            try:
                event = action(db, event_id, actor=actor, reason=payload.reason)
                db.commit()
                results.append({"id": event_id, "ok": True, "status": event.status})
            except (ConnectorDeliveryError, ValueError) as exc:
                db.rollback()
                code = getattr(exc, "code", None)
                results.append({"id": event_id, "ok": False, "code": code, "error": str(exc)})
        ok = [item for item in results if item["ok"]]
        if not ok:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail={"results": results})
        return {"ok_count": len(ok), "failed_count": len(results) - len(ok), "results": results}

    @router.post("/api/operations/dead-letters/outbox/discard")
    def discard_outbox_dead_letters(
        payload: OutboxDeadLetterActionRequest,
        user: User = Depends(require_admin),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """放弃下游投递死信：事件转 canceled、台账记 rejected，历史与费用不改写。"""
        return _outbox_action(payload, user, db, replay=False)

    @router.post("/api/operations/dead-letters/outbox/replay")
    def replay_outbox_dead_letters(
        payload: OutboxDeadLetterActionRequest,
        user: User = Depends(require_admin),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """重放下游投递死信：按业务幂等键 + 修订重新入队，不会产生第二条下游记录。"""
        return _outbox_action(payload, user, db, replay=True)

    @router.get("/metrics", response_class=Response)
    def machine_metrics(
        authorization: str | None = Header(default=None),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> Response:
        configured = settings_provider().operations_metrics_token
        if not configured:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="运行指标机器认证尚未配置")
        prefix = "Bearer "
        supplied = (
            authorization[len(prefix) :]
            if authorization and authorization.startswith(prefix)
            else ""
        )
        if not supplied or not hmac.compare_digest(supplied.encode("utf-8"), configured.encode("utf-8")):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="运行指标机器认证失败")
        return Response(
            content=prometheus_metrics(snapshot(db)),
            media_type="text/plain; version=0.0.4",
        )

    return router

MATERIALIZE_REGRESSION_SAFETY_SECONDS = 120
# The largest supported server tier has 193 workers. Keep room for the Web

    {

        "baseline_strategy_bundle_contract_mismatch",
    }
)


class AutomationConfigurationBlocker(ValueError):
    """Stable, actionable reason why one automation cohort cannot run."""

    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.message = message


def _category_contract_definition_errors(
    db: Session,
    *,
    profile: EvaluationCategoryProfile | None,
    require_prompt_b: bool,
) -> list[str]:
    if profile is None:
        return ["category_profile_missing"]
    errors: list[str] = []
    required_refs = {
        "prompt_a": (profile.prompt_a_id, PromptVersion, "A"),
        "model_config": (profile.model_config_id, ModelConfig, None),
    }
    if require_prompt_b:
        required_refs["prompt_b"] = (profile.prompt_b_id, PromptVersion, "B")
    for name, (identity, model_type, expected_stage) in required_refs.items():
        if identity is None:
            errors.append(f"{name}_id_missing")
            continue
        entity = db.get(model_type, identity)
        if entity is None:
            errors.append(f"{name}_missing")
        elif expected_stage is not None and entity.stage != expected_stage:
            errors.append(f"{name}_stage_mismatch")
    if not (profile.rubric_version or "").strip():
        errors.append("rubric_version_missing")
    if not profile.dimension_schema_key or not profile.dimension_schema_version:
        errors.append("dimension_contract_missing")
    return list(dict.fromkeys(errors))


def _resolve_category_baseline_bundle(
    db: Session,
    *,
    profile: EvaluationCategoryProfile | None,
    base_prompt: PromptVersion,
    freeze_automatic_binding: bool,
) -> tuple[StrategyBundle, str]:
    """Resolve one explicit or uniquely matching full category contract."""
    if profile is None:
        raise AutomationConfigurationBlocker(
            "baseline_category_contract_incomplete",
            "类目配置不存在；请由管理员先创建并启用类目合同。",
        )
    definition_errors = _category_contract_definition_errors(
        db, profile=profile, require_prompt_b=True
    )
    if definition_errors:
        raise AutomationConfigurationBlocker(
            "baseline_category_contract_incomplete",
            "类目基线合同未完整配置 A/B Prompt、模型、Rubric 和维度 Schema；"
            "请在后台高级设置补齐后重试。",
        )
    configured_prompt_b = db.get(PromptVersion, profile.prompt_b_id)
    if configured_prompt_b is None or configured_prompt_b.version != base_prompt.version:
        raise AutomationConfigurationBlocker(
            "baseline_category_contract_mismatch",
            "待优化案例的 B Prompt 与当前类目合同不一致；"
            "请先归档旧案例或恢复对应类目版本。",
        )

    config = _safe_json_object(profile.automation_config_json)
    baseline_bundle_id = config.get("baseline_strategy_bundle_id")
    if baseline_bundle_id is not None:
        if (
            not isinstance(baseline_bundle_id, int)
            or isinstance(baseline_bundle_id, bool)
            or baseline_bundle_id < 1
        ):
            raise AutomationConfigurationBlocker(
                "baseline_strategy_bundle_invalid",
                "类目显式基线 ID 无效；请由管理员在后台高级设置重新选择。",
            )
        baseline = db.get(StrategyBundle, baseline_bundle_id)
        if baseline is None:
            raise AutomationConfigurationBlocker(
                "baseline_strategy_bundle_missing",
                "类目显式绑定的基线 Bundle 不存在；"
                "请由管理员在后台高级设置重新选择。",
            )
        errors = category_bundle_contract_errors(
            db,
            profile=profile,
            bundle=baseline,
            require_complete=True,
            require_prompt_b=True,
            enforce_baseline_id=True,
        )
        if errors or baseline.prompt_b_version != base_prompt.version:
            raise AutomationConfigurationBlocker(
                "baseline_strategy_bundle_contract_mismatch",
                "显式基线 Bundle 与类目 A/B Prompt、模型、Rubric 或维度合同不一致；"
                "请由管理员在后台高级设置重新选择。",
            )
        return baseline, str(config.get("baseline_binding_source") or "explicit_legacy")

    candidates = db.scalars(
        select(StrategyBundle).where(
            StrategyBundle.prompt_b_version == base_prompt.version
        )
    ).all()
    matches = [
        candidate
        for candidate in candidates
        if not category_bundle_contract_errors(
            db,
            profile=profile,
            bundle=candidate,
            require_complete=True,
            require_prompt_b=True,
            enforce_baseline_id=False,
        )
    ]
    if not matches:
        raise AutomationConfigurationBlocker(
            "baseline_strategy_bundle_not_found",
            "没有 StrategyBundle 完整匹配当前类目的 A/B Prompt、模型、Rubric 和维度合同；"
            "请先生成该组合的 Bundle，或由管理员在后台高级设置显式绑定。",
        )
    if len(matches) > 1:
        raise AutomationConfigurationBlocker(
            "baseline_strategy_bundle_ambiguous",
            f"当前类目合同匹配到 {len(matches)} 个 StrategyBundle；"
            "请由管理员在后台高级设置显式选择一个基线。",
        )
    baseline = matches[0]
    if freeze_automatic_binding:
        profile.automation_config_json = canonical_json(
            {
                **config,
                "baseline_strategy_bundle_id": baseline.id,
                "baseline_binding_source": "auto_contract",
            }
        )
        profile.automation_revision += 1
        db.flush()
    return baseline, "auto_contract"


def automation_lifecycle_status(status: str) -> str:
    """Expose the production state machine while retaining legacy DB values."""
    return {
        "planned": "pending",
        "awaiting_executor": "pending",
        "processing": "processing",
        "succeeded": "running",
        "running": "running",
        "awaiting_release_review": "awaiting_release_review",
        "failed": "failed",
        "cancelled": "cancelled",
    }.get(status, status)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AutomationAdapterResult:
    candidates: list[dict[str, Any]]
    regression: dict[str, Any]
    actual_cost_micros: int = 0
    input_tokens: int | None = None
    output_tokens: int | None = None
    total_tokens: int | None = None


class OptimizationAdapter(Protocol):
    def optimize(
        self,
        *,
        frozen_input: dict[str, Any],
        max_candidates: int,
    ) -> AutomationAdapterResult: ...

    def estimate_cost_micros(self, *, frozen_input: dict[str, Any]) -> int: ...


class RealOptimizationAdapter:
    """Production adapter backed by the configured optimizer model only."""

    def __init__(
        self, *, config: Any, base_prompt: PromptVersion | None = None
    ):
        self.config = config
        self.base_prompt = base_prompt

    def bind_base_prompt(self, db: Session, *, version: str) -> None:
        prompt = db.scalar(
            select(PromptVersion).where(
                PromptVersion.stage == "B",
                PromptVersion.version == version,
            )
        )
        if prompt is None:
            raise ValueError("优化案例对应的 B 提示词不存在")
        self.base_prompt = prompt

    def estimate_cost_micros(self, *, frozen_input: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
        if (
            self.config.max_input_tokens <= 0
            or self.config.input_micros_per_million_tokens <= 0
            or self.config.output_micros_per_million_tokens <= 0
        ):
            raise ValueError("优化模型缺少输入上限或计价配置")
        calls = OPTIMIZER_MODEL_CALLS_PER_RUN
        input_tokens = calls * self.config.max_input_tokens
        output_tokens = calls * self.config.max_tokens
        return _token_cost(
            input_tokens,
            output_tokens,
            self.config.input_micros_per_million_tokens,
            self.config.output_micros_per_million_tokens,
        )

    def optimize(
        self,
        *,
        frozen_input: dict[str, Any],
        max_candidates: int,
    ) -> AutomationAdapterResult:
        if self.base_prompt is None:
            raise ValueError("优化执行器尚未绑定基础提示词")
        generated = asyncio.run(
            generate_automation_candidates(
                config=self.config,
                base_prompt=self.base_prompt,
                frozen_input=frozen_input,
                max_candidates=max_candidates,
            )
        )
        return AutomationAdapterResult(
            candidates=generated.candidates,
            regression={
                "status": "awaiting_results",
                "roles": ["target_error", "stable_control", "blind_holdout"],
            },
            actual_cost_micros=_token_cost(
                generated.input_tokens,
                generated.output_tokens,
                self.config.input_micros_per_million_tokens,
                self.config.output_micros_per_million_tokens,
            ),
            input_tokens=generated.input_tokens,
            output_tokens=generated.output_tokens,
            total_tokens=generated.total_tokens,
        )

    def prepare_regression_binding(
        self,
        db: Session,
        *,
        base_prompt: PromptVersion,
        category_key: str = "space_image",
        freeze_automatic_binding: bool = True,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        profile = db.scalar(
            select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == category_key
            )
        )
        baseline, binding_source = _resolve_category_baseline_bundle(
            db,
            profile=profile,
            base_prompt=base_prompt,
            freeze_automatic_binding=freeze_automatic_binding,
        )
        assert profile is not None
        sample_sets = db.scalars(
            select(SampleSet)
            .where(
                SampleSet.kind == "golden",
                SampleSet.status == "locked",
                SampleSet.category_key == category_key,
            )
            .order_by(SampleSet.id.desc())
        ).all()
        for sample_set in sample_sets:
            if any(item.asset.category_key != category_key for item in sample_set.items):
                continue
            target = stable = blind = None
            for item in sample_set.items:
                review = latest_review_for_result(item.source_result)
                if review is None or review.decision not in {"approved", "corrected"}:
                    continue
                if target is None and review.decision == "corrected":
                    target = item
                elif stable is None and review.decision == "approved":
                    stable = item
                elif blind is None:
                    blind = item
            if target is not None and stable is not None and blind is not None:
                return {
                    "sample_set_id": sample_set.id,
                    "baseline_strategy_bundle_id": baseline.id,
                    "category_contract": {
                        "category_key": category_key,
                        "profile_id": profile.id,
                        "pipeline_revision": profile.pipeline_revision,
                        "automation_revision": profile.automation_revision,
                        "baseline_strategy_bundle_id": baseline.id,
                        "baseline_binding_source": binding_source,
                        "prompt_a_id": profile.prompt_a_id,
                        "prompt_b_id": profile.prompt_b_id,
                        "model_config_id": profile.model_config_id,
                        "rubric_version": profile.rubric_version,
                        "dimension_schema_key": profile.dimension_schema_key,
                        "dimension_schema_version": profile.dimension_schema_version,
                    },
                    "samples": [
                        {"sample_item_id": target.id, "role": "target_error"},
                        {"sample_item_id": stable.id, "role": "stable_control"},
                        {"sample_item_id": blind.id, "role": "blind_holdout"},
                    ],
                }
        raise AutomationConfigurationBlocker(
            "regression_binding_missing",
            f"类目 {category_key} 没有可用于三角色配对回归的锁定黄金样本；"
            "请补齐 target_error、stable_control 和 blind_holdout。",
        )

    def materialize(
        self,
        db: Session,
        *,
        run: AutomationOptimizationRun,
        result: AutomationAdapterResult,
        worker_id: str,
    ) -> dict[str, list[int]]:
        from .main import (
            PairedRegressionCreateRequest,
            PairedRegressionSampleRequest,
            _create_paired_regression,
        )

        binding = json.loads(run.frozen_input_json).get("regression_binding")
        if not isinstance(binding, dict):
            raise ValueError("自动优化缺少三角色回归绑定")
        baseline = db.get(
            StrategyBundle, int(binding["baseline_strategy_bundle_id"])
        )
        category_contract = binding.get("category_contract")
        if not isinstance(category_contract, dict):
            raise ValueError("自动优化缺少冻结类目合同")
        profile = db.scalar(
            select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == run.category_key
            )
        )
        frozen_identity = {
            "prompt_a_id": profile.prompt_a_id if profile is not None else None,
            "prompt_b_id": profile.prompt_b_id if profile is not None else None,
            "model_config_id": profile.model_config_id if profile is not None else None,
            "rubric_version": profile.rubric_version if profile is not None else None,
            "dimension_schema_key": (
                profile.dimension_schema_key if profile is not None else None
            ),
            "dimension_schema_version": (
                profile.dimension_schema_version if profile is not None else None
            ),
        }
        if (
            baseline is None
            or baseline.prompt_b_version != run.base_prompt_version
            or category_contract.get("category_key") != run.category_key
            or profile is None
            or category_contract.get("profile_id") != profile.id
            or category_contract.get("pipeline_revision") != profile.pipeline_revision
            or category_contract.get("automation_revision") != profile.automation_revision
            or category_contract.get("baseline_strategy_bundle_id") != baseline.id
            or any(
                field in category_contract
                and category_contract[field] != current_value
                for field, current_value in frozen_identity.items()
            )
            or category_bundle_contract_errors(
                db,
                profile=profile,
                bundle=baseline,
                require_complete=True,
                require_prompt_b=True,
                enforce_baseline_id=True,
            )
        ):
            raise ValueError("自动优化基线策略已失配")
        prompt_a = db.scalar(
            select(PromptVersion).where(
                PromptVersion.stage == "A",
                PromptVersion.version == baseline.prompt_a_version,
            )
        )
        model_configs = db.scalars(
            select(ModelConfig).where(
                ModelConfig.model_id == baseline.model_id,
                ModelConfig.active.is_(True),
            )
        ).all()
        model_matches = []
        for model in model_configs:
            try:
                if build_model_config_snapshot(model) == json.loads(
                    baseline.model_config_snapshot
                ):
                    model_matches.append(model)
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                break
        model_config = model_matches[0] if len(model_matches) == 1 else None
        if prompt_a is None or model_config is None:
            raise ValueError("自动优化基线配置无法解析")
        policy = (
            None
            if baseline.sampling_policy_revision is None
            else db.scalar(
                select(SamplingPolicy).where(
                    SamplingPolicy.revision
                    == baseline.sampling_policy_revision
                )
            )
        )
        prompt_ids: list[int] = []
        regression_ids: list[int] = []
        for index, generated in enumerate(result.candidates, start=1):
            version = f"auto-b-{run.id}-{index}"
            if db.scalar(
                select(PromptVersion.id).where(PromptVersion.version == version)
            ) is not None:
                raise ValueError("自动候选版本已存在")
            candidate = PromptVersion(
                category_key=run.category_key,
                stage="B",
                name=f"自动优化候选 #{run.id}.{index}",
                version=version,
                system_prompt=str(generated["system_prompt"]),
                user_prompt=str(generated["user_prompt"]),
                rubric_version=baseline.rubric_version,
                status="draft",
                source="optimizer",
                source_automation_run_id=run.id,
                change_note=str(generated["change_note"]),
                created_by=worker_id,
            )
            db.add(candidate)
            db.flush()
            candidate_bundle = get_or_create_bundle(
                db=db,
                model_config=model_config,
                prompt_a=prompt_a,
                prompt_b=candidate,
                rubric_version=baseline.rubric_version,
                engine_version=baseline.engine_version,
                risk_review_version=baseline.risk_review_version,
                sampling_policy=policy,
                agent_plan_version=baseline.agent_plan_version,
            )
            if candidate_bundle.model_config_snapshot != baseline.model_config_snapshot:
                raise ValueError("自动优化期间模型配置已漂移")
            assert_bundle_pair_category_contract(baseline, candidate_bundle)
            regression = _create_paired_regression(
                PairedRegressionCreateRequest(
                    name=f"自动优化候选 #{run.id}.{index} 配对回归",
                    sample_set_id=int(binding["sample_set_id"]),
                    baseline_strategy_bundle_id=baseline.id,
                    candidate_strategy_bundle_id=candidate_bundle.id,
                    trigger_prompt_id=candidate.id,
                    samples=[
                        PairedRegressionSampleRequest(**sample)
                        for sample in binding["samples"]
                    ],
                    metric_rules_version="automation-paired-v1",
                    aesthetic_accuracy_max_drop=0,
                    whole_image_accuracy_max_drop=0,
                    level_consistency_max_drop=0,
                ),
                user=SimpleNamespace(username=worker_id),
                db=db,
                commit=False,
            )
            prompt_ids.append(candidate.id)
            regression_ids.append(int(regression["id"]))
        return {"prompt_ids": prompt_ids, "regression_ids": regression_ids}


def _token_cost(
    input_tokens: int,
    output_tokens: int,
    input_price: int,
    output_price: int,
) -> int:
    if input_price <= 0 or output_price <= 0:
        raise ValueError("模型计价未完整配置")
    return (
        input_tokens * input_price + output_tokens * output_price + 999_999
    ) // 1_000_000


class DeterministicOptimizationAdapter:
    """A test-only adapter. It never performs network or model calls."""

    def __init__(self, result: AutomationAdapterResult):
        self.result = result

    def optimize(
        self,
        *,
        frozen_input: dict[str, Any],
        max_candidates: int,
    ) -> AutomationAdapterResult:
        del frozen_input
        if len(self.result.candidates) > max_candidates:
            raise ValueError("测试适配器返回的候选数超过策略上限")
        return self.result

    def estimate_cost_micros(self, *, frozen_input: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
        del frozen_input
        return self.result.actual_cost_micros


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)


def _aware(value: datetime | None) -> datetime | None:
    if value is None or value.tzinfo is not None:
        return value
    return value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def _policy_payload(policy: AutomationPolicy) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "enabled": policy.enabled,
        "dry_run": policy.dry_run,
        "revision": policy.revision,
        "case_threshold": policy.case_threshold,
        "immediate_severities": json.loads(
            policy.immediate_severities_json or "[]"
        ),
        "daily_budget_micros": policy.daily_budget_micros,
        "cooldown_seconds": policy.cooldown_seconds,
        "max_candidates": policy.max_candidates,
        "lease_seconds": policy.lease_seconds,
        "max_attempts": policy.max_attempts,
        "base_retry_seconds": policy.base_retry_seconds,
    }


def _safe_json_object(value: str | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(value or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return {}
    return parsed if isinstance(parsed, dict) else {}


def _safe_json_list(value: str | None) -> list[Any]:
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(value or "[]")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return []
    return parsed if isinstance(parsed, list) else []


_BUNDLE_CATEGORY_IDENTITY_FIELDS = (
    "model_id",
    "model_config_snapshot",
    "prompt_a_version",
    "rubric_version",
    "engine_version",
    "sampling_policy_revision",
    "risk_review_version",
    "agent_plan_version",
    "dimension_route_policy_id",
    "dimension_schema_set_snapshot",
    "label_field_set_snapshot",
    "resolved_schema_contract_version",
    "dimension_route_policy_snapshot",
    "evaluation_profile_set_snapshot",
)


def assert_bundle_pair_category_contract(
    baseline: StrategyBundle,
    candidate: StrategyBundle,
) -> None:
    """Require candidate and baseline to differ only by the B prompt."""
    mismatched = [
        field
        for field in _BUNDLE_CATEGORY_IDENTITY_FIELDS
        if getattr(baseline, field) != getattr(candidate, field)
    ]
    if mismatched:
        raise ValueError(
            "基线与候选 StrategyBundle 的类目合同不一致："
            + "、".join(mismatched)
        )


def category_bundle_contract_errors(
    db: Session,
    *,
    profile: EvaluationCategoryProfile | None,
    bundle: StrategyBundle,
    require_complete: bool,
    require_prompt_b: bool,
    enforce_baseline_id: bool,
) -> list[str]:
    """Validate one immutable bundle against the persisted category contract."""
    if profile is None:
        return ["category_profile_missing"]
    errors: list[str] = []
    if require_complete:
        errors.extend(
            _category_contract_definition_errors(
                db, profile=profile, require_prompt_b=require_prompt_b
            )
        )
    config = _safe_json_object(profile.automation_config_json)
    baseline_bundle_id = config.get("baseline_strategy_bundle_id")
    if enforce_baseline_id and baseline_bundle_id is not None:
        if (
            not isinstance(baseline_bundle_id, int)
            or isinstance(baseline_bundle_id, bool)
            or baseline_bundle_id != bundle.id
        ):
            errors.append("baseline_strategy_bundle_mismatch")

    required_refs = {
        "prompt_a_id": profile.prompt_a_id,
        "model_config_id": profile.model_config_id,
    }
    if require_prompt_b:
        required_refs["prompt_b_id"] = profile.prompt_b_id
    explicit_refs = any(value is not None for value in required_refs.values())
    contract_declared = (
        require_complete
        or explicit_refs
        or baseline_bundle_id is not None
        or profile.dimension_schema_key is not None
        or profile.dimension_schema_version is not None
    )
    if require_complete or explicit_refs:
        errors.extend(
            f"{name}_missing"
            for name, value in required_refs.items()
            if value is None
        )

    prompt_a = db.get(PromptVersion, profile.prompt_a_id) if profile.prompt_a_id else None
    if prompt_a is not None and (
        prompt_a.stage != "A" or prompt_a.version != bundle.prompt_a_version
    ):
        errors.append("prompt_a_mismatch")
    if require_prompt_b:
        prompt_b = db.get(PromptVersion, profile.prompt_b_id) if profile.prompt_b_id else None
        if prompt_b is not None and (
            prompt_b.stage != "B" or prompt_b.version != bundle.prompt_b_version
        ):
            errors.append("prompt_b_mismatch")

    model = db.get(ModelConfig, profile.model_config_id) if profile.model_config_id else None
    if model is not None:
        if model.model_id != bundle.model_id:
            errors.append("model_id_mismatch")
        else:
            try:
                frozen_model = json.loads(bundle.model_config_snapshot)
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                errors.append("model_snapshot_invalid")
            else:
                if frozen_model != build_model_config_snapshot(model):
                    errors.append("model_snapshot_mismatch")
    if contract_declared and profile.rubric_version != bundle.rubric_version:
        errors.append("rubric_version_mismatch")

    schema_key = profile.dimension_schema_key
    schema_version = profile.dimension_schema_version
    if bool(schema_key) != bool(schema_version):
        errors.append("dimension_contract_incomplete")
    elif require_complete and not schema_key:
        errors.append("dimension_contract_missing")
    elif schema_key and schema_version:
        try:
            schema_set = json.loads(bundle.dimension_schema_set_snapshot or "")
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            errors.append("dimension_contract_invalid")
        else:
            schemas = schema_set.get("schemas") if isinstance(schema_set, dict) else None
            if not isinstance(schemas, list) or not any(
                isinstance(item, dict)
                and item.get("schema_key") == schema_key
                and item.get("version") == schema_version
                for item in schemas
            ):
                errors.append("dimension_contract_mismatch")
    return list(dict.fromkeys(errors))


def _config_is_ready(config: Any | None) -> bool:
    return (
        config is not None
        and bool(getattr(config, "encrypted_api_key", None))
        and int(getattr(config, "input_micros_per_million_tokens", 0) or 0) > 0
        and int(getattr(config, "output_micros_per_million_tokens", 0) or 0) > 0
        and int(getattr(config, "max_input_tokens", 0) or 0) > 0
        and int(getattr(config, "max_tokens", 0) or 0) > 0
    )


def _execution_lease_seconds(
    policy: AutomationPolicy,
    adapter: OptimizationAdapter | None,
) -> int:
    return int(
        _execution_lease_details(policy, adapter)["effective_lease_seconds"]
    )


def _execution_lease_details(
    policy: AutomationPolicy,
    adapter: OptimizationAdapter | None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Explain the policy floor and optimizer worst-case execution window."""
    config = getattr(adapter, "config", None)
    try:
        timeout_seconds = int(getattr(config, "timeout_seconds", 0) or 0)
        max_retries = max(0, int(getattr(config, "max_retries", 0) or 0))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        timeout_seconds = 0
        max_retries = 0
    optimizer_timeout_budget = (
        timeout_seconds * (max_retries + 1) * OPTIMIZER_MODEL_CALLS_PER_RUN
        if config is not None and timeout_seconds > 0
        else 0
    )
    safety_seconds = (
        MATERIALIZE_REGRESSION_SAFETY_SECONDS
        if optimizer_timeout_budget > 0
        else 0
    )
    effective_seconds = max(
        policy.lease_seconds,
        optimizer_timeout_budget + safety_seconds,
    )
    return {
        "effective_lease_seconds": effective_seconds,
        # Retain the original generic key for readers of existing audit payloads.
        "effective_seconds": effective_seconds,
        "policy_seconds": policy.lease_seconds,
        "optimizer_timeout_seconds": timeout_seconds,
        "optimizer_max_retries": max_retries,
        "optimizer_call_count": (
            OPTIMIZER_MODEL_CALLS_PER_RUN if optimizer_timeout_budget > 0 else 0
        ),
        "optimizer_timeout_budget_seconds": optimizer_timeout_budget,
        "materialize_regression_safety_seconds": safety_seconds,
        "source": (
            "optimizer_timeout_budget"
            if effective_seconds > policy.lease_seconds
            else "policy_minimum"
        ),
    }
