        from .models import BaselineSet,BaselineSetItem
        require_row(db,BaselineSet,a['set_id'])
        ids=db.scalars(select(BaselineSetItem.id).where(BaselineSetItem.baseline_set_id==a['set_id']).order_by(BaselineSetItem.id).offset(a['offset']).limit(a['limit']+1)).all()
        return {'items':[stock.truth_payload(db,a['set_id'],i) for i in ids[:a['limit']]],'next_offset':a['offset']+a['limit'] if len(ids)>a['limit'] else None}
    raise HTTPException(404,'工具未实现')

===== FILE backend/app/postgres_importer.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import Engine, Table, func, inspect, select, text
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import insert as postgresql_insert
from sqlalchemy.exc import SQLAlchemyError

from . import models as _models  # noqa: F401
from .database import Base
from .snapshot_import_common import (
    ImportPlan,
    ImportReport,
    InjectedImportStop,
    SnapshotImportError,
    load_checkpoint,
    open_immutable_sqlite,
    resolve_source_snapshot,
    source_batch,
    source_schema_compatibility_version,
    validate_source_schema,
    verify_source_unchanged,
    write_checkpoint,
)


PostgresImportError = SnapshotImportError
_validate_source_schema = validate_source_schema


def _assert_target_ready(
    target: Engine,
    *,
    checkpoint: dict[str, Any],
    resumed: bool,
) -> None:
    if target.dialect.name != "postgresql":
        raise PostgresImportError("导入目标必须是 PostgreSQL")
    actual_tables = set(inspect(target).get_table_names())
    missing = set(Base.metadata.tables) - actual_tables
    if missing:
        raise PostgresImportError(
            f"PostgreSQL 目标缺少基线表：{sorted(missing)}"
        )
    progress = checkpoint["tables"]
    with target.connect() as connection:
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
            actual_count = int(
                connection.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(table)) or 0
            )
            if not resumed and actual_count:
                raise PostgresImportError(
                    f"PostgreSQL 目标表 {table.name} 非空，禁止首次合并导入"
                )
            if resumed and table.name in progress:
                recorded_count = int(progress[table.name]["imported_rows"])
                if actual_count < recorded_count:
                    raise PostgresImportError(
                        f"PostgreSQL 目标表 {table.name} 少于检查点记录"
                    )


def _insert_batch(target: Engine, table: Table, rows: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> int:
    primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
    statement = postgresql_insert(table).values(rows).on_conflict_do_nothing(
        index_elements=[primary_key.name]
    ).returning(primary_key)
    try:
        with target.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SET LOCAL session_replication_role = replica"
            )
            result = connection.execute(statement)
            inserted_primary_keys = result.scalars().all()
    except SQLAlchemyError:
        raise PostgresImportError(
            f"PostgreSQL 导入表 {table.name} 的批次失败；行内容已隐藏"
        ) from None
    return len(inserted_primary_keys)


def _reset_postgres_sequences(target: Engine) -> None:
    with target.begin() as connection:
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
            primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
            sequence_name = connection.scalar(
                text("SELECT pg_get_serial_sequence(:table_name, :column_name)"),
                {
                    "table_name": table.name,
                    "column_name": primary_key.name,
                },
            )
            if sequence_name is None:
                continue
            maximum = connection.scalar(select(func.max(primary_key)))
            connection.execute(
                text(
                    "SELECT setval(CAST(:sequence_name AS regclass), "
                    ":value, :is_called)"
                ),
                {
                    "sequence_name": sequence_name,
                    "value": 1 if maximum is None else int(maximum),
                    "is_called": maximum is not None,
                },
            )


def import_sqlite_snapshot(
    source: Path,
    target: Engine,
    checkpoint_dir: Path,
    *,
    batch_size: int = 1000,
    stop_after_batches: int | None = None,
) -> ImportReport:
    """Import a frozen SQLite snapshot in committed, resumable PK batches."""
    if batch_size <= 0:
        raise PostgresImportError("batch_size 必须大于 0")
    if stop_after_batches is not None and stop_after_batches <= 0:
        raise PostgresImportError("stop_after_batches 必须大于 0")
    resolved_source, source_stat, source_sha256 = resolve_source_snapshot(source)
    source_connection = open_immutable_sqlite(resolved_source)
    inserted_rows = 0
    duplicate_rows = 0
    committed_batches = 0
    try:
        source_columns_by_table = validate_source_schema(
            source_connection,
            Base.metadata,
        )
        source_schema_version = source_schema_compatibility_version(
            source_connection
        )
        complete_plan = ImportPlan.from_metadata(Base.metadata)
        plan = ImportPlan(
            tuple(
                table_name
                for table_name in complete_plan
                if table_name in source_columns_by_table
            )
        )
        checkpoint_path = checkpoint_dir / "sqlite-to-postgres-import.json"
        checkpoint, resumed = load_checkpoint(
            checkpoint_path,
            source=resolved_source,
            source_size=source_stat.st_size,
            source_sha256=source_sha256,
            plan=plan,
            format_name="sqlite-to-postgres-import-v1",
        )
        _assert_target_ready(target, checkpoint=checkpoint, resumed=resumed)
        table_progress: dict[str, Any] = checkpoint["tables"]
        for table_name in plan:
            table = Base.metadata.tables[table_name]
            progress = table_progress.setdefault(
                table_name,
                {
                    "completed": False,
                    "imported_rows": 0,
                    "last_primary_key": None,
                },
            )
            if progress["completed"]:
                continue
            while True:
                rows = source_batch(
                    source_connection,
                    table,
                    last_primary_key=progress["last_primary_key"],
                    batch_size=batch_size,
                    source_columns=source_columns_by_table[table_name],
                    source_schema_version=source_schema_version,
                )
                if not rows:
                    progress["completed"] = True
                    write_checkpoint(checkpoint_path, checkpoint)
                    break
                inserted = _insert_batch(target, table, rows)
                inserted_rows += inserted
                duplicate_rows += len(rows) - inserted
                primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
                progress["last_primary_key"] = rows[-1][primary_key.name]
                progress["imported_rows"] = int(
                    progress["imported_rows"]
                ) + len(rows)
                write_checkpoint(checkpoint_path, checkpoint)
                committed_batches += 1
                if (
                    stop_after_batches is not None
                    and committed_batches >= stop_after_batches
                ):
                    raise InjectedImportStop(
                        f"在第 {committed_batches} 个已提交批次后注入停止"
                    )
        _reset_postgres_sequences(target)
        checkpoint["complete"] = True
        write_checkpoint(checkpoint_path, checkpoint)
        table_rows = {
            table_name: int(progress["imported_rows"])
            for table_name, progress in checkpoint["tables"].items()
        }
        return ImportReport(
            source=resolved_source,
            source_sha256=source_sha256,
            inserted_rows=inserted_rows,
            duplicate_rows=duplicate_rows,
            table_rows=table_rows,
            completed_tables=sum(
                1
                for progress in checkpoint["tables"].values()
                if progress["completed"]
            ),
            resumed=resumed,
        )
    finally:
        source_connection.close()
        verify_source_unchanged(
            resolved_source,
            initial_stat=source_stat,
            initial_sha256=source_sha256,
        )


def resume_import(
    source: Path,
    target: Engine,
    checkpoint_dir: Path,
    *,
    batch_size: int = 1000,
) -> ImportReport:
    return import_sqlite_snapshot(
        source,
        target,
        checkpoint_dir,
        batch_size=batch_size,
    )

===== FILE backend/app/postgres_integration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import getpass
import hashlib
import json
import os
import plistlib
import re
import shlex
import shutil
import sys
import tempfile
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from subprocess import DEVNULL, PIPE, CompletedProcess, Popen, run
from typing import Any, Callable, Sequence, TextIO


INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT = Path("/Volumes/WorkSSD")
INTEGRATION_DIRECTORY_NAME = "3d66-label-system-integration"
PROJECT_LIMIT_BYTES = 120 * 1024**3
MINIMUM_FREE_BYTES = 40 * 1024**3


class IntegrationPathError(ValueError):
    """Raised when an integration path could escape the approved SSD directory."""


class IntegrationCapacityError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when the integration workspace reaches a storage safety limit."""


class PostgresIntegrationError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when the isolated PostgreSQL cluster cannot be managed safely."""


class SnapshotVerificationError(PostgresIntegrationError):
    """Raised when a migration snapshot is missing, mutable, or has the wrong digest."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SnapshotManifest:
    path: Path
    size_bytes: int
    sha256: str
    source: str

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, int | str]:
        return {
            "path": str(self.path),
            "size_bytes": self.size_bytes,
            "sha256": self.sha256,
            "source": self.source,
        }


def load_snapshot_manifest(snapshot: Path) -> SnapshotManifest:
    manifest_path = snapshot.with_name(f"{snapshot.name}.manifest.json")
    if manifest_path.is_symlink() or not manifest_path.is_file():
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("快照清单不存在或不是普通文件")
    try:
        payload = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("快照清单 JSON 无法读取") from exc
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("快照清单 JSON 必须是对象")
    try:
        manifest = SnapshotManifest(
            path=Path(str(payload["path"])),
            size_bytes=int(payload["size_bytes"]),
            sha256=str(payload["sha256"]),
            source=str(payload["source"]),
        )
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("快照清单字段不完整") from exc
    if manifest.path != snapshot:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("快照清单路径与导入文件不一致")
    verify_snapshot(manifest)
    return manifest


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PostgresBinaries:
    initdb: Path
    pg_ctl: Path
    postgres: Path
    psql: Path
    createdb: Path

    @classmethod
    def for_prefix(cls, prefix: Path) -> "PostgresBinaries":
        binary_dir = prefix.expanduser().resolve(strict=False) / "bin"
        return cls(
            initdb=binary_dir / "initdb",
            pg_ctl=binary_dir / "pg_ctl",
            postgres=binary_dir / "postgres",
            psql=binary_dir / "psql",
            createdb=binary_dir / "createdb",
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class IntegrationPaths:
    root: Path
    pgdata: Path
    socket_dir: Path
    logs: Path
    snapshots: Path
    checkpoints: Path
    reports: Path

    @classmethod
    def from_root(cls, root: Path) -> "IntegrationPaths":
        resolved_root = root.expanduser().resolve(strict=False)
        return cls(
            root=resolved_root,
            pgdata=resolved_root / "pgdata",
            socket_dir=resolved_root / "socket",
            logs=resolved_root / "logs",
            snapshots=resolved_root / "snapshots",
            checkpoints=resolved_root / "checkpoints",
            reports=resolved_root / "reports",
        )

    def assert_safe(self) -> None:
        approved_parent = INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT.resolve(strict=False)
        if (
            self.root.name != INTEGRATION_DIRECTORY_NAME
            or self.root.parent != approved_parent
        ):
            raise IntegrationPathError(
                "PostgreSQL 集成数据只能位于 WorkSSD 的项目专属目录"
            )
        for child in (
            self.pgdata,
            self.socket_dir,
            self.logs,
            self.snapshots,
            self.checkpoints,
            self.reports,
        ):
            if child.is_symlink():
                raise IntegrationPathError(
                    f"项目专属目录内不得使用符号链接：{child.name}"
                )
            if child.exists() and not child.resolve(strict=True).is_relative_to(
                self.root
            ):
                raise IntegrationPathError(
                    f"项目专属目录子路径越界：{child.name}"
                )


def _directory_size(root: Path) -> int:
    if not root.exists():
        return 0
    total = 0
    pending = [root]
    while pending:
        directory = pending.pop()
        with os.scandir(directory) as entries:
            for entry in entries:
                if entry.is_symlink():
                    raise IntegrationPathError(
                        f"项目专属目录内不得使用符号链接：{entry.name}"
                    )
                if entry.is_dir(follow_symlinks=False):
                    pending.append(Path(entry.path))
                elif entry.is_file(follow_symlinks=False):
                    total += entry.stat(follow_symlinks=False).st_size
    return total


def integration_disk_report(paths: IntegrationPaths) -> dict[str, int | str]:
    paths.assert_safe()
    project_used_bytes = _directory_size(paths.root)
    disk = shutil.disk_usage(paths.root if paths.root.exists() else paths.root.parent)
    if project_used_bytes >= PROJECT_LIMIT_BYTES:
        raise IntegrationCapacityError(
            "项目集成目录已达到 120 GiB 安全上限，停止写入"
        )
    if disk.free < MINIMUM_FREE_BYTES:
        raise IntegrationCapacityError(
            "WorkSSD 可用空间低于 40 GiB 安全下限，停止写入"
        )
    return {
        "root": str(paths.root),
        "project_used_bytes": project_used_bytes,
        "project_limit_bytes": PROJECT_LIMIT_BYTES,
        "filesystem_total_bytes": disk.total,
        "filesystem_free_bytes": disk.free,
        "minimum_free_bytes": MINIMUM_FREE_BYTES,
    }


CommandRunner = Callable[[Sequence[str]], CompletedProcess[str] | object]


def _default_run_command(command: Sequence[str]) -> CompletedProcess[str]:
    return run(
        list(command),
        check=False,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        encoding="utf-8",
        errors="replace",
    )


def _run_checked(command: Sequence[str], runner: CommandRunner) -> None:
    completed = runner(command)
    returncode = getattr(completed, "returncode", 0)
    if returncode != 0:
        stderr = str(getattr(completed, "stderr", "")).strip()
        stdout = str(getattr(completed, "stdout", "")).strip()
        detail = stderr or stdout or f"exit {returncode}"
        raise PostgresIntegrationError(
            f"PostgreSQL 命令失败（{Path(command[0]).name}）：{detail}"
        )


def _atomic_write_text(path: Path, content: str, *, mode: int) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    descriptor, temporary_name = tempfile.mkstemp(
        prefix=f".{path.name}.",
        dir=path.parent,
    )
    try:
        with os.fdopen(descriptor, "w", encoding="utf-8") as output:
            output.write(content)
            output.flush()
            os.fsync(output.fileno())
        os.chmod(temporary_name, mode)
        os.replace(temporary_name, path)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temporary_name):
            os.unlink(temporary_name)


def _fsync_directory(path: Path) -> None:
    descriptor = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY)
    try:
        os.fsync(descriptor)
    finally:
        os.close(descriptor)


def _normalized_sha256(value: str) -> str:
    normalized = value.strip().lower()
    if re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", normalized) is None:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("期望 SHA-256 必须是 64 位十六进制")
    return normalized


def _file_sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as source:
        while chunk := source.read(1024 * 1024):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def verify_snapshot(manifest: SnapshotManifest) -> None:
    """Verify the immutable local snapshot against its frozen manifest."""
    path = manifest.path
    if path.is_symlink() or not path.is_file():
        raise SnapshotVerificationError(f"快照文件不存在或不是普通文件：{path}")
    expected_sha256 = _normalized_sha256(manifest.sha256)
    actual_size = path.stat().st_size
    actual_sha256 = _file_sha256(path)
    actual_mode = path.stat().st_mode & 0o777
    failures: list[str] = []
    if actual_size != manifest.size_bytes:
        failures.append(
            f"大小期望 {manifest.size_bytes}，实际 {actual_size}"
        )
    if actual_sha256 != expected_sha256:
        failures.append(
            f"SHA-256 期望 {expected_sha256}，实际 {actual_sha256}"
        )
    if actual_mode != 0o400:
        failures.append(f"文件必须为 0400 只读，实际 {actual_mode:04o}")
    if failures:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("快照校验失败：" + "；".join(failures))


def _write_snapshot_manifest(manifest: SnapshotManifest) -> Path:
    manifest_path = manifest.path.with_name(f"{manifest.path.name}.manifest.json")
    _atomic_write_text(
        manifest_path,
        json.dumps(
            manifest.as_dict(),
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=2,
            sort_keys=True,
        )
        + "\n",
        mode=0o400,
    )
    _fsync_directory(manifest_path.parent)
    return manifest_path


def promote_verified_snapshot(
    partial: Path,
    final: Path,
    *,
    expected_sha256: str,
    expected_size: int,
    source: str,
) -> SnapshotManifest:
    """Atomically promote one complete verified partial without overwriting facts."""
    expected_digest = _normalized_sha256(expected_sha256)
    if expected_size < 0:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("期望快照大小不能为负数")
    manifest = SnapshotManifest(
        path=final,
        size_bytes=expected_size,
        sha256=expected_digest,
        source=source.strip(),
    )
    if not manifest.source:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("快照来源不能为空")
    if final.exists() or final.is_symlink():
        verify_snapshot(manifest)
        _write_snapshot_manifest(manifest)
        return manifest
    if partial.is_symlink() or not partial.is_file():
        raise SnapshotVerificationError(
            f"待校验快照不存在或不是普通文件：{partial}"
        )
    actual_size = partial.stat().st_size
    actual_sha256 = _file_sha256(partial)
    failures: list[str] = []
    if actual_size != expected_size:
        failures.append(f"大小期望 {expected_size}，实际 {actual_size}")
    if actual_sha256 != expected_digest:
        failures.append(
            f"SHA-256 期望 {expected_digest}，实际 {actual_sha256}"
        )
    if failures:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("快照校验失败：" + "；".join(failures))
    with partial.open("rb") as source_file:
        os.fsync(source_file.fileno())
    os.chmod(partial, 0o400)
    os.replace(partial, final)
    _fsync_directory(final.parent)
    verify_snapshot(manifest)
    _write_snapshot_manifest(manifest)
    return manifest


PopenFactory = Callable[..., Any]


def copy_snapshot_from_ssh(
    paths: IntegrationPaths,
    *,
    remote_host: str,
    remote_path: Path,
    expected_sha256: str,
    expected_size: int,
    ssh_binary: Path = Path("/usr/bin/ssh"),
    identity_file: Path | None = None,
    sudo_read: bool = False,
    popen_factory: PopenFactory = Popen,
) -> SnapshotManifest:
    """Stream an approved remote SQLite snapshot into the isolated workspace."""
    paths.assert_safe()
    host = remote_host.strip()
    if re.fullmatch(r"(?:[A-Za-z0-9._-]+@)?[A-Za-z0-9._:-]+", host) is None:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("远程主机格式不安全")
    if not remote_path.is_absolute() or any(
        character in str(remote_path) for character in ("\n", "\r", "\0")
    ):
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("远程快照必须是安全的绝对路径")
    if expected_size < 0:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("期望快照大小不能为负数")
    expected_digest = _normalized_sha256(expected_sha256)
    report = integration_disk_report(paths)
    if int(report["project_used_bytes"]) + expected_size >= PROJECT_LIMIT_BYTES:
        raise IntegrationCapacityError("复制快照会超过项目 120 GiB 安全上限")
    if int(report["filesystem_free_bytes"]) - expected_size < MINIMUM_FREE_BYTES:
        raise IntegrationCapacityError("复制快照会使 WorkSSD 低于 40 GiB 安全下限")
    _prepare_directories(paths)
    final = paths.snapshots / remote_path.name
    partial = final.with_name(f"{final.name}.partial")
    source = f"{host}:{remote_path}"
    manifest = SnapshotManifest(
        path=final,
        size_bytes=expected_size,
        sha256=expected_digest,
        source=source,
    )
    if final.exists() or final.is_symlink():
        verify_snapshot(manifest)
        _write_snapshot_manifest(manifest)
        return manifest
    if partial.is_symlink():
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("快照临时文件不得为符号链接")
    identity_arguments: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    if identity_file is not None:
        expanded_identity = identity_file.expanduser()
        if expanded_identity.is_symlink() or not expanded_identity.is_file():
            raise SnapshotVerificationError("SSH 身份文件不存在或不是普通文件")
        resolved_identity = expanded_identity.resolve(strict=True)
        if resolved_identity.stat().st_mode & 0o077:
            raise SnapshotVerificationError(
                "SSH 身份文件权限不得向组或其他用户开放"
            )
        identity_arguments = ("-i", str(resolved_identity))
    command = (
        str(ssh_binary),
        *identity_arguments,
        "-o",
        "BatchMode=yes",
        "--",
        host,
        ("sudo -n " if sudo_read else "")
        + f"cat -- {shlex.quote(str(remote_path))}",
    )
    process = popen_factory(command, stdout=PIPE, stderr=DEVNULL)
    stdout = getattr(process, "stdout", None)
    if stdout is None:
        raise PostgresIntegrationError("SSH 快照流缺少标准输出")
    descriptor = os.open(
        partial,
        os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0),
        0o600,
    )
    try:
        with os.fdopen(descriptor, "wb") as output:
            while chunk := stdout.read(1024 * 1024):
                output.write(chunk)
            output.flush()
            os.fsync(output.fileno())
    finally:
        stdout.close()
    returncode = int(process.wait())
    stderr = getattr(process, "stderr", None)
    if stderr is not None:
        stderr.close()
    if returncode != 0:
        raise PostgresIntegrationError(
            f"SSH 快照复制失败（exit {returncode}），未晋升临时文件"
        )
    return promote_verified_snapshot(
        partial,
        final,
        expected_sha256=expected_digest,
        expected_size=expected_size,
        source=source,
    )


def _postgres_literal(value: Path) -> str:
    rendered = str(value)
    if "\n" in rendered or "\r" in rendered:
        raise IntegrationPathError("PostgreSQL 路径不能包含换行符")
    return rendered.replace("'", "''")


def _prepare_directories(paths: IntegrationPaths) -> None:
    paths.assert_safe()
    approved_parent = INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT.resolve(strict=False)
    if not approved_parent.is_dir():
        raise IntegrationPathError("WorkSSD 未挂载，禁止创建集成目录")
    paths.root.mkdir(mode=0o700, exist_ok=True)
    os.chmod(paths.root, 0o700)
    for directory in (
        paths.socket_dir,
        paths.logs,
        paths.snapshots,
        paths.checkpoints,
        paths.reports,
    ):
        directory.mkdir(mode=0o700, exist_ok=True)
        os.chmod(directory, 0o700)
    paths.assert_safe()


def _postgres_auto_configuration(paths: IntegrationPaths) -> str:
    return "\n".join(
        (
            "# Managed by 3d66 label-system local integration tooling.",
            "listen_addresses = ''",
            f"unix_socket_directories = '{_postgres_literal(paths.socket_dir)}'",
            "unix_socket_permissions = 0700",
            "ssl = off",
            "logging_collector = on",
            f"log_directory = '{_postgres_literal(paths.logs)}'",
            "log_filename = 'postgresql-%Y-%m-%d.log'",
            "",
        )
    )


def initialize_postgres(
    paths: IntegrationPaths,
    binaries: PostgresBinaries,
    *,
    run_command: CommandRunner = _default_run_command,
) -> None:
    integration_disk_report(paths)
    _prepare_directories(paths)
    version_file = paths.pgdata / "PG_VERSION"
    if version_file.exists():
        major = version_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
        if major != "16":
            raise PostgresIntegrationError(
                f"已有 PostgreSQL 数据目录版本为 {major or '未知'}，要求版本 16"
            )
    else:
        if paths.pgdata.exists() and any(paths.pgdata.iterdir()):
            raise PostgresIntegrationError(
                "已有 pgdata 目录非空但缺少 PG_VERSION，禁止覆盖"
            )
        _run_checked(
            (
                str(binaries.initdb),
                "--pgdata",
                str(paths.pgdata),
                "--encoding=UTF8",
                "--locale=C",
                "--auth-local=trust",
                "--auth-host=reject",
            ),
            run_command,
        )
        if not version_file.is_file():
            raise PostgresIntegrationError("initdb 完成后未生成 PG_VERSION")
        major = version_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
        if major != "16":
            raise PostgresIntegrationError(
                f"initdb 生成版本为 {major or '未知'}，要求版本 16"
            )
    _atomic_write_text(
        paths.pgdata / "postgresql.auto.conf",
        _postgres_auto_configuration(paths),
        mode=0o600,
    )


def _default_filesystem_type(path: Path) -> str:
    completed = _default_run_command(
        ("/usr/sbin/diskutil", "info", "-plist", str(path))
    )
    if completed.returncode != 0:
        detail = completed.stderr.strip() or completed.stdout.strip()
        raise PostgresIntegrationError(
            f"无法识别 WorkSSD 文件系统：{detail or completed.returncode}"
        )
    try:
        payload = plistlib.loads(completed.stdout.encode("utf-8"))
    except (plistlib.InvalidFileException, ValueError) as exc:
        raise PostgresIntegrationError("无法解析 WorkSSD 文件系统信息") from exc
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise PostgresIntegrationError("WorkSSD 文件系统信息不是属性字典")
    filesystem = payload.get("FilesystemType") or payload.get("FilesystemName")
    if not isinstance(filesystem, str) or not filesystem.strip():
        raise PostgresIntegrationError("WorkSSD 文件系统信息缺少类型")
    return filesystem.strip().lower()


def _require_binaries(binaries: PostgresBinaries) -> None:
    missing = [
        str(path)
        for path in (
            binaries.initdb,
            binaries.pg_ctl,
            binaries.postgres,
            binaries.psql,
            binaries.createdb,
        )
        if not path.is_file() or not os.access(path, os.X_OK)
    ]
    if missing:
        raise PostgresIntegrationError(
            "PostgreSQL 16 可执行文件不完整：" + "、".join(missing)
        )


def preflight_postgres(
    paths: IntegrationPaths,
    binaries: PostgresBinaries,
    *,
    filesystem_type: Callable[[Path], str] = _default_filesystem_type,
    run_command: CommandRunner = _default_run_command,
    require_executables: bool = True,
) -> dict[str, int | str | bool]:
    report = integration_disk_report(paths)
    filesystem_probe_path = INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT.resolve(strict=True)
    filesystem = filesystem_type(filesystem_probe_path).strip().lower()
    if filesystem != "apfs":
        raise PostgresIntegrationError(
            f"集成数据盘必须使用 APFS，当前识别为 {filesystem or '未知'}"
        )
    if require_executables:
        _require_binaries(binaries)
    completed = run_command((str(binaries.postgres), "--version"))
    returncode = getattr(completed, "returncode", 0)
    if returncode != 0:
        raise PostgresIntegrationError("无法读取 PostgreSQL 版本")
    version_text = str(getattr(completed, "stdout", "")).strip()
    match = re.search(r"PostgreSQL\)\s+(\d+)(?:\.\d+)?", version_text)
    if match is None:
        raise PostgresIntegrationError("无法解析 PostgreSQL 版本")
    major = int(match.group(1))
    if major != 16:
        raise PostgresIntegrationError(
            f"本地集成要求 PostgreSQL 16，当前为 {major}"
        )
    return {
        **report,
        "filesystem": filesystem,
        "postgres_major": major,
        "postgres_version": version_text,
        "socket_only": True,
    }


def _require_cluster_version(paths: IntegrationPaths) -> None:
    paths.assert_safe()
    version_file = paths.pgdata / "PG_VERSION"
    if not version_file.is_file():
        raise PostgresIntegrationError("PostgreSQL 集成集群尚未初始化")
    major = version_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    if major != "16":
        raise PostgresIntegrationError(
            f"已有 PostgreSQL 数据目录版本为 {major or '未知'}，要求版本 16"
        )


def start_postgres(
    paths: IntegrationPaths,
    binaries: PostgresBinaries,
    *,
    run_command: CommandRunner = _default_run_command,
) -> None:
    integration_disk_report(paths)
    _prepare_directories(paths)
    _require_cluster_version(paths)
    _run_checked(
        (
            str(binaries.pg_ctl),
            "--pgdata",
            str(paths.pgdata),
            "--log",
            str(paths.logs / "postgres-startup.log"),
            "--wait",
            "start",
        ),
        run_command,
    )


def stop_postgres(
    paths: IntegrationPaths,
    binaries: PostgresBinaries,
    *,
    run_command: CommandRunner = _default_run_command,
) -> None:
    _require_cluster_version(paths)
    _run_checked(
        (
            str(binaries.pg_ctl),
            "--pgdata",
            str(paths.pgdata),
            "--mode=fast",
            "--wait",
            "stop",
        ),
        run_command,
    )


def postgres_status(
    paths: IntegrationPaths,
    binaries: PostgresBinaries,
    *,
    run_command: CommandRunner = _default_run_command,
) -> dict[str, bool | str]:
    paths.assert_safe()
    completed = run_command(
        (
            str(binaries.pg_ctl),
            "--pgdata",
            str(paths.pgdata),
            "status",
        )
    )
    returncode = int(getattr(completed, "returncode", 1))
    if returncode not in {0, 3}:
        stderr = str(getattr(completed, "stderr", "")).strip()
        stdout = str(getattr(completed, "stdout", "")).strip()
        raise PostgresIntegrationError(
            f"无法读取 PostgreSQL 状态：{stderr or stdout or returncode}"
        )
    return {
        "running": returncode == 0,
        "pgdata": str(paths.pgdata),
        "socket_dir": str(paths.socket_dir),
    }


def _cli_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Manage the isolated 3d66 label-system PostgreSQL 16 cluster."
    )
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    for command_name in ("preflight", "init", "start", "stop", "status"):
        command = commands.add_parser(command_name)
        command.add_argument(
            "--root",
            type=Path,
            default=INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT / INTEGRATION_DIRECTORY_NAME,
        )
        command.add_argument(
            "--postgres-prefix",
            type=Path,
            default=Path("/opt/homebrew/opt/postgresql@16"),
        )
    snapshot = commands.add_parser("copy-snapshot")
    snapshot.add_argument(
        "--root",
        type=Path,
        default=INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT / INTEGRATION_DIRECTORY_NAME,
    )
    snapshot.add_argument("--remote-host", required=True)
    snapshot.add_argument("--remote-path", type=Path, required=True)
    snapshot.add_argument("--expected-sha256", required=True)
    snapshot.add_argument("--expected-size", type=int, required=True)
    snapshot.add_argument("--identity-file", type=Path)
    snapshot.add_argument("--sudo-read", action="store_true")
    importer = commands.add_parser("import")
    importer.add_argument(
        "--root",
        type=Path,
        default=INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT / INTEGRATION_DIRECTORY_NAME,
    )
    importer.add_argument("--source", type=Path, required=True)
    importer.add_argument("--database", required=True)
    importer.add_argument("--database-user", default=getpass.getuser())
    importer.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=1000)
    importer.add_argument("--checkpoint-dir", type=Path)
    reconciler = commands.add_parser("reconcile")
    reconciler.add_argument(
        "--root",
        type=Path,
        default=INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT / INTEGRATION_DIRECTORY_NAME,
    )
    reconciler.add_argument("--source", type=Path, required=True)
    reconciler.add_argument("--database", required=True)
    reconciler.add_argument("--database-user", default=getpass.getuser())
    reconciler.add_argument("--report-name")
    return parser


def cli_main(
    argv: Sequence[str] | None = None,
    *,
    filesystem_type: Callable[[Path], str] = _default_filesystem_type,
    run_command: CommandRunner = _default_run_command,
    popen_factory: PopenFactory = Popen,
    output: TextIO = sys.stdout,
) -> int:
    args = _cli_parser().parse_args(argv)
    paths = IntegrationPaths.from_root(args.root)

    if args.command == "copy-snapshot":
        manifest = copy_snapshot_from_ssh(
            paths,
            remote_host=args.remote_host,
            remote_path=args.remote_path,
            expected_sha256=args.expected_sha256,
            expected_size=args.expected_size,
            identity_file=args.identity_file,
            sudo_read=args.sudo_read,
            popen_factory=popen_factory,
        )
        payload: dict[str, int | str | bool] = manifest.as_dict()
    elif args.command == "import":
        from sqlalchemy import URL, create_engine

        from .migrations.postgres_baseline import create_postgres_baseline
        from .postgres_importer import import_sqlite_snapshot

        paths.assert_safe()
        integration_disk_report(paths)
        resolved_source = args.source.expanduser().resolve(strict=True)
        if resolved_source.parent != paths.snapshots:
            raise SnapshotVerificationError(
                "导入源必须位于项目 snapshots 目录"
            )
        load_snapshot_manifest(resolved_source)
        database_name = str(args.database).strip()
        if re.fullmatch(r"labelsystem_integration_[a-z0-9_]+", database_name) is None:
            raise PostgresIntegrationError(
                "本地导入数据库名必须使用 labelsystem_integration_ 前缀"
            )
        checkpoint_dir = (
            args.checkpoint_dir.expanduser().resolve(strict=False)
            if args.checkpoint_dir is not None
            else paths.checkpoints / database_name
        )
        if not checkpoint_dir.is_relative_to(paths.checkpoints):
            raise IntegrationPathError("导入检查点必须位于项目 checkpoints 目录")
        target = create_engine(
            URL.create(
                "postgresql+psycopg",
                username=str(args.database_user),
                database=database_name,
                query={"host": str(paths.socket_dir)},
            ),
            pool_pre_ping=True,
            hide_parameters=True,
        )
        try:
            with target.begin() as connection:
                create_postgres_baseline(connection)
            report = import_sqlite_snapshot(
                resolved_source,
                target,
                checkpoint_dir,
                batch_size=args.batch_size,
            )
        finally:
            target.dispose()
        payload = {
            "database": database_name,
            "source": str(report.source),
            "source_sha256": report.source_sha256,
            "inserted_rows": report.inserted_rows,
            "duplicate_rows": report.duplicate_rows,
            "completed_tables": report.completed_tables,
            "resumed": report.resumed,
            "checkpoint": str(
                checkpoint_dir / "sqlite-to-postgres-import.json"
            ),
        }
    elif args.command == "reconcile":
        from sqlalchemy import URL, create_engine

        from .postgres_reconciliation import (
            ReconciliationError,
            assert_critical_invariants,
            reconcile_databases,
        )

        paths.assert_safe()
        integration_disk_report(paths)
        resolved_source = args.source.expanduser().resolve(strict=True)
        if resolved_source.parent != paths.snapshots:
            raise SnapshotVerificationError(
                "对账源必须位于项目 snapshots 目录"
            )
        load_snapshot_manifest(resolved_source)
        database_name = str(args.database).strip()
        if re.fullmatch(r"labelsystem_integration_[a-z0-9_]+", database_name) is None:
            raise PostgresIntegrationError(
                "本地对账数据库名必须使用 labelsystem_integration_ 前缀"
            )
        report_name = (
            str(args.report_name).strip()
            if args.report_name
            else f"postgresql-reconciliation-{database_name}"
        )
        if re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9._-]+", report_name) is None:
            raise IntegrationPathError("对账报告名包含不安全字符")
        target = create_engine(
            URL.create(
                "postgresql+psycopg",
                username=str(args.database_user),
                database=database_name,
                query={"host": str(paths.socket_dir)},
            ),
            pool_pre_ping=True,
            hide_parameters=True,
        )
        try:
            report = reconcile_databases(resolved_source, target)
        finally:
            target.dispose()
        accepted = True
        reconciliation_error = ""
        try:
            assert_critical_invariants(report)
        except ReconciliationError as exc:
            accepted = False
            reconciliation_error = str(exc)
        json_report = paths.reports / f"{report_name}.json"
        markdown_report = paths.reports / f"{report_name}.md"
        _atomic_write_text(
            json_report,
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "accepted": accepted,
                    "error": reconciliation_error,
                    **report.as_dict(),
                },
                ensure_ascii=False,
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
            + "\n",
            mode=0o600,
        )
        _atomic_write_text(
            markdown_report,
            report.to_markdown(
                accepted=accepted,
                error=reconciliation_error,
            ),
            mode=0o600,
        )
        _fsync_directory(paths.reports)
        if not accepted:
            raise PostgresIntegrationError(
                f"PostgreSQL 对账未通过；报告：{json_report}"
            )
        payload = {
            "database": database_name,
            "accepted": True,
            "tables": len(report.tables),
            "rows": sum(
                table.target_row_count for table in report.tables.values()
            ),
            "json_report": str(json_report),
            "markdown_report": str(markdown_report),
        }
    else:
        binaries = PostgresBinaries.for_prefix(args.postgres_prefix)
        payload = {}

    if args.command == "preflight":
        payload: dict[str, int | str | bool] = preflight_postgres(
            paths,
            binaries,
            filesystem_type=filesystem_type,
            run_command=run_command,
        )
    elif args.command == "init":
        payload = preflight_postgres(
            paths,
            binaries,
            filesystem_type=filesystem_type,
            run_command=run_command,
        )
        initialize_postgres(paths, binaries, run_command=run_command)
        payload = {**payload, **postgres_status(paths, binaries, run_command=run_command)}
    elif args.command == "start":
        preflight_postgres(
            paths,
            binaries,
            filesystem_type=filesystem_type,
            run_command=run_command,
        )
        start_postgres(paths, binaries, run_command=run_command)
        payload = postgres_status(paths, binaries, run_command=run_command)
    elif args.command == "stop":
        stop_postgres(paths, binaries, run_command=run_command)
        payload = postgres_status(paths, binaries, run_command=run_command)
    elif args.command == "status":
        payload = postgres_status(paths, binaries, run_command=run_command)

    print(
        json.dumps(
            {"command": args.command, "status": "ok", **payload},
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
        ),
        file=output,
    )
    return 0

===== FILE backend/app/postgres_reconciliation.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any
from urllib.parse import quote

from sqlalchemy import Engine, Integer, func, inspect, select, text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection

from .database import Base
from .migrations.postgres_invariants import INVARIANT_OWNERS
from .reconciliation_common import (
    CRITICAL_TABLES,
    ReconciliationError,
    TableReconciliation,
    canonical_database_value,
    schema_contract_compatible,
    table_digest as _table_digest,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SequenceState:
    sequence_name: str
    primary_key_max: int | None
    last_value: int | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReconciliationReport:
    source: Path
    source_quick_check: str
    source_schema_version: int | None
    target_schema_version: int | None
    target_postgres_version: str
    source_foreign_key_violations: int
    target_foreign_key_violations: dict[str, int]
    schema_facts: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]
    tables: dict[str, TableReconciliation]
    sequences: dict[str, SequenceState]
    installed_invariant_triggers: tuple[str, ...]

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "source": str(self.source),
            "source_quick_check": self.source_quick_check,
            "source_schema_version": self.source_schema_version,
            "target_schema_version": self.target_schema_version,
            "target_postgres_version": self.target_postgres_version,
            "source_foreign_key_violations": (
                self.source_foreign_key_violations
            ),
            "target_foreign_key_violations": (
                self.target_foreign_key_violations
            ),
            "schema_facts": self.schema_facts,
            "tables": {
                name: {**asdict(table), "matches": table.matches}
                for name, table in self.tables.items()
            },
            "sequences": {
                name: asdict(state) for name, state in self.sequences.items()
            },
            "installed_invariant_triggers": list(
                self.installed_invariant_triggers
            ),
        }

    def to_markdown(self, *, accepted: bool, error: str = "") -> str:
        mismatches = [
            name for name, table in self.tables.items() if not table.matches
        ]
        target_fk_total = sum(self.target_foreign_key_violations.values())
        lines = [
            "# SQLite → PostgreSQL 对账报告",
            "",
            f"- 结论：{'通过' if accepted else '阻断'}",
            f"- 源快照：`{self.source}`",
            f"- 表：{len(self.tables)}",
            "- 源/目标业务行："
            f"{sum(item.source_row_count for item in self.tables.values())} / "
            f"{sum(item.target_row_count for item in self.tables.values())}",
            f"- 行级不一致表：{len(mismatches)}",
            f"- SQLite 外键违规：{self.source_foreign_key_violations}",
            f"- PostgreSQL 外键违规：{target_fk_total}",
            f"- PostgreSQL 不变量触发器：{len(self.installed_invariant_triggers)}",
            f"- Schema 版本：{self.source_schema_version} → {self.target_schema_version}",
        ]
        if mismatches:
            lines.extend(("", "## 不一致表", ""))
            lines.extend(f"- `{name}`" for name in mismatches)
        if error:
            lines.extend(("", "## 阻断原因", "", error))
        lines.append("")
        return "\n".join(lines)


def _sqlite_identifier(name: str) -> str:
    if not name.replace("_", "a").isalnum() or name[0].isdigit():
        raise ReconciliationError(f"不安全的 SQLite 标识符：{name}")
    return f'"{name}"'


def _source_rows(
    connection: sqlite3.Connection,
    table_name: str,
    columns: Sequence[Column[Any]],
    primary_key_name: str,
) -> Iterable[Mapping[str, Any]]:
    selected = ", ".join(_sqlite_identifier(column.name) for column in columns)
    cursor = connection.execute(
        f"SELECT {selected} FROM {_sqlite_identifier(table_name)} "
        f"ORDER BY {_sqlite_identifier(primary_key_name)}"
    )
    while rows := cursor.fetchmany(500):
        yield from rows


def _source_schema_version(connection: sqlite3.Connection) -> int | None:
    exists = connection.execute(
        "SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master "
        "WHERE type='table' AND name='schema_migrations'"
    ).fetchone()
    if exists is None:
        return None
    value = connection.execute(
        "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
    ).fetchone()[0]
    return None if value is None else int(value)


def _schema_facts(
    source: sqlite3.Connection,
    target: Connection,
) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    target_inspector = inspect(target)
    facts: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
        source_rows = source.execute(
            f"PRAGMA table_info({_sqlite_identifier(table.name)})"
        ).fetchall()
        source_columns = [str(row[1]) for row in source_rows]
        source_primary_key = [
            str(row[1]) for row in sorted(source_rows, key=lambda row: row[5]) if row[5]
        ]
        target_columns_info = target_inspector.get_columns(table.name)
        target_columns = [str(column["name"]) for column in target_columns_info]
        target_primary_key = list(
            target_inspector.get_pk_constraint(table.name).get(
                "constrained_columns"
            )
            or []
        )
        facts[table.name] = {
            "source_columns": source_columns,
            "target_columns": target_columns,
            "source_primary_key": source_primary_key,
            "target_primary_key": target_primary_key,
            "source_declared_types": {
                str(row[1]): str(row[2]) for row in source_rows
            },
            "target_declared_types": {
                str(column["name"]): str(column["type"])
                for column in target_columns_info
            },
            "compatible": schema_contract_compatible(
                source_columns=source_columns,
                target_columns=target_columns,
                source_primary_key=source_primary_key,
                target_primary_key=target_primary_key,
            ),
        }
    return facts


def _target_foreign_key_violations(
    connection: Connection,
) -> dict[str, int]:
    violations: dict[str, int] = {}
    for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
        for constraint in sorted(
            table.foreign_key_constraints,
            key=lambda item: item.name or "",
        ):
            element = next(iter(constraint.elements))
            child = table.alias("child")
            parent = element.column.table.alias("parent")
            child_column = child.c[element.parent.name]
            parent_column = parent.c[element.column.name]
            key = (
                f"{table.name}.{element.parent.name}->"
                f"{element.column.table.name}.{element.column.name}"
            )
            count = connection.scalar(
                select(func.count())
                .select_from(
                    child.outerjoin(
                        parent,
                        child_column == parent_column,
                    )
                )
                .where(
                    child_column.is_not(None),
                    parent_column.is_(None),
                )
            )
            violations[key] = int(count or 0)
    return violations


def _sequence_states(
    connection: Connection,
    tables: dict[str, TableReconciliation],
) -> dict[str, SequenceState]:
    states: dict[str, SequenceState] = {}
    for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
        primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
        if not isinstance(primary_key.type, Integer):
            continue
        sequence_name = connection.scalar(
            text("SELECT pg_get_serial_sequence(:table_name, :column_name)"),
            {"table_name": table.name, "column_name": primary_key.name},
        )
        if sequence_name is None:
            continue
        sequence_short_name = str(sequence_name).rsplit(".", 1)[-1].strip('"')
        last_value = connection.scalar(
            text(
                "SELECT last_value FROM pg_sequences "
                "WHERE schemaname=current_schema() AND sequencename=:name"
            ),
            {"name": sequence_short_name},
        )
        maximum = tables[table.name].target_primary_key_max
        states[table.name] = SequenceState(
            sequence_name=str(sequence_name),
            primary_key_max=None if maximum is None else int(maximum),
            last_value=None if last_value is None else int(last_value),
        )
    return states


def reconcile_databases(source: Path, target: Engine) -> ReconciliationReport:
    """Stream and compare every mapped row without mutating either database."""
    from . import models as _models  # noqa: F401

    resolved_source = source.expanduser().resolve(strict=True)
    if source.expanduser().is_symlink() or not resolved_source.is_file():
        raise ReconciliationError("SQLite 对账源必须是普通文件且不得为符号链接")
    if target.dialect.name != "postgresql":
        raise ReconciliationError("对账目标必须是 PostgreSQL")
    source_connection = sqlite3.connect(
        f"file:{quote(str(resolved_source), safe='/')}?mode=ro&immutable=1",
        uri=True,
    )
    source_connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    source_connection.execute("PRAGMA query_only = ON")
    try:
        quick_check_rows = source_connection.execute(
            "PRAGMA quick_check"
        ).fetchall()
        source_quick_check = ",".join(str(row[0]) for row in quick_check_rows)
        source_fk_violations = sum(
            1 for _row in source_connection.execute("PRAGMA foreign_key_check")
        )
        source_version = _source_schema_version(source_connection)
        reconciled_tables: dict[str, TableReconciliation] = {}
        with target.connect() as target_connection:
            schema_facts = _schema_facts(
                source_connection,
                target_connection,
            )
            for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
                columns = tuple(table.columns)
                primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
                source_values = _table_digest(
                    _source_rows(
                        source_connection,
                        table.name,
                        columns,
                        primary_key.name,
                    ),
                    table_name=table.name,
                    columns=columns,
                    primary_key_name=primary_key.name,
                )
                target_result = target_connection.execution_options(
                    stream_results=True,
                    yield_per=500,
                ).execute(select(table).order_by(primary_key))
                try:
                    target_values = _table_digest(
                        target_result.mappings(),
                        table_name=table.name,
                        columns=columns,
                        primary_key_name=primary_key.name,
                    )
                finally:
                    target_result.close()
                reconciled_tables[table.name] = TableReconciliation(
                    table_name=table.name,
                    source_row_count=source_values[0],
                    target_row_count=target_values[0],
                    source_primary_key_min=source_values[1],
                    source_primary_key_max=source_values[2],
                    target_primary_key_min=target_values[1],
                    target_primary_key_max=target_values[2],
                    source_null_counts=source_values[3],
                    target_null_counts=target_values[3],
                    source_hash=source_values[4],
                    target_hash=target_values[4],
                )
            target_fk_violations = _target_foreign_key_violations(
                target_connection
            )
            sequences = _sequence_states(
                target_connection,
                reconciled_tables,
            )
            target_schema_version = target_connection.scalar(
                text("SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations")
            )
            postgres_version = str(
                target_connection.scalar(text("SELECT version()"))
            )
            triggers = tuple(
                str(row[0])
                for row in target_connection.execute(
                    text(
                        "SELECT tgname FROM pg_trigger "
                        "WHERE NOT tgisinternal AND tgname LIKE 'trg_%' "
                        "ORDER BY tgname"
                    )
                )
            )
        return ReconciliationReport(
            source=resolved_source,
            source_quick_check=source_quick_check,
            source_schema_version=source_version,
            target_schema_version=(
                None
                if target_schema_version is None
                else int(target_schema_version)
            ),
            target_postgres_version=postgres_version,
            source_foreign_key_violations=source_fk_violations,
            target_foreign_key_violations=target_fk_violations,
            schema_facts=schema_facts,
            tables=reconciled_tables,
            sequences=sequences,
            installed_invariant_triggers=triggers,
        )
    finally:
        source_connection.close()


_CRITICAL_TABLES = CRITICAL_TABLES


def assert_critical_invariants(report: ReconciliationReport) -> None:
    failures: list[str] = []
    if report.source_quick_check != "ok":
        failures.append(
            f"SQLite quick_check={report.source_quick_check or 'empty'}"
        )
    if report.source_foreign_key_violations:
        failures.append(
            "SQLite foreign_key_check="
            f"{report.source_foreign_key_violations}"
        )
    target_fk_total = sum(report.target_foreign_key_violations.values())
    if target_fk_total:
        failures.append(f"PostgreSQL foreign_key_violations={target_fk_total}")
    incompatible_schema = [
        name
        for name, fact in report.schema_facts.items()
        if not fact["compatible"]
    ]
    if incompatible_schema:
        failures.append(
            "schema=" + ",".join(sorted(incompatible_schema)[:10])
        )
    mismatched_tables = [
        name for name, table in report.tables.items() if not table.matches
    ]
    if mismatched_tables:
        failures.append(
            "tables=" + ",".join(sorted(mismatched_tables)[:10])
        )
    missing_critical = _CRITICAL_TABLES - set(report.tables)
    if missing_critical:
        failures.append(
            "critical_missing=" + ",".join(sorted(missing_critical))
        )
    critical_mismatches = [
        name
        for name in _CRITICAL_TABLES & set(report.tables)
        if not report.tables[name].matches
    ]
    if critical_mismatches:
        failures.append(
            "critical=" + ",".join(sorted(critical_mismatches))
        )
    if (
        report.source_schema_version is not None
        and report.target_schema_version != report.source_schema_version
    ):
        failures.append(
            "schema_version="
            f"{report.source_schema_version}->{report.target_schema_version}"
        )
    expected_triggers = {
        name
        for name, owner in INVARIANT_OWNERS.items()
        if owner.kind == "postgres_trigger"
    }
    actual_triggers = set(report.installed_invariant_triggers)
    if actual_triggers != expected_triggers:
        failures.append(
            f"triggers={len(actual_triggers)}/{len(expected_triggers)}"
        )
    stale_sequences = [
        table_name
        for table_name, state in report.sequences.items()
        if state.primary_key_max is not None
        and state.last_value != state.primary_key_max
    ]
    if stale_sequences:
        failures.append(
            "sequences=" + ",".join(sorted(stale_sequences)[:10])
        )
    if failures:
        raise ReconciliationError("数据库对账未通过：" + "；".join(failures))

===== FILE backend/app/prepared_media_memo.py =====
"""Process-local memo for already-verified media preparation results.

2026-09-19 run 366: with 60 admitted slots the longest local phase reached
52 s while the host CPU stayed near 40 %. Every prompt call re-read each
frozen image (target plus up to 15 anchors) from the NAS three to four times
for sha256 checks and fully decoded it four times (geometry probe, geometry
cache validation, lossless pixel hash, lossless cache validation), even when
every derived file was already cached. Sixty slots doing that at once
saturated the NAS link, the admission controller read the slow phases as
local pressure and halved itself down to 8.

The memo remembers, per worker process, the verified outcome for one exact
input identity (path, mtime_ns, size, declared sha256, policy key) together
with the derived file's identity. A later call with the same identities
returns the same outcome without touching the pixels again; any change to
either file (mtime or size) drops the entry and the full verification runs
again. Evidence produced from a memo hit is byte-identical to a cache hit.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import threading
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Hashable


def file_identity(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int, int] | None:
    """Return (path, mtime_ns, size) for a regular file, else None."""

    try:
        stat_result = path.stat()
    except OSError:
        return None
    return (str(path), stat_result.st_mtime_ns, stat_result.st_size)


class BoundedMemo:
    """Small thread-safe FIFO memo; a full memo drops its oldest quarter."""

    def __init__(self, max_entries: int) -> None:
        if max_entries < 4:
            raise ValueError("memo needs at least four entries")
        self._max_entries = max_entries
        self._entries: dict[Hashable, Any] = {}
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

    def get(self, key: Hashable) -> Any:
        with self._lock:
            return self._entries.get(key)

    def put(self, key: Hashable, value: Any) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            if key not in self._entries and len(self._entries) >= self._max_entries:
                for stale in list(self._entries)[: self._max_entries // 4]:
                    self._entries.pop(stale, None)
            self._entries[key] = value

    def discard(self, key: Hashable) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            self._entries.pop(key, None)

    def clear(self) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            self._entries.clear()

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        with self._lock:
            return len(self._entries)


_DIGEST_MEMO = BoundedMemo(8192)


def sha256_file_memo(path: Path, compute) -> str:
    """Digest a file once per (path, mtime_ns, size); recompute on any change."""

    identity = file_identity(path)
    if identity is None:
        return compute(path)
    cached = _DIGEST_MEMO.get(identity)
    if cached is not None:
        return cached
    digest = compute(path)
    _DIGEST_MEMO.put(identity, digest)
    return digest

===== FILE backend/app/production_dimension_contract.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .dimension_schema_registry import canonical_hash
from .models import DimensionSchema, StrategyBundle
from .scoring import (
    DimensionScoringContractError,
    validate_dimension_scoring_contract,
)


class ProductionDimensionContractError(ValueError):
    """A category dimension contract is unsafe for production execution."""

    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.technical_error_type = code
        self.retryable = False


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProductionDimensionContract:
    schema_id: int
    schema_key: str
    version: str
    canonical_hash: str
    definition: dict[str, Any]


def resolve_published_dimension_contract(
    db: Session,
    *,
    schema_key: str | None,
    version: str | None,
    bundle: StrategyBundle | None = None,
    require_configured: bool = False,
) -> ProductionDimensionContract | None:
    """Resolve one immutable, published schema and optionally verify its bundle copy."""
    if schema_key is None and version is None:
        if not require_configured:
            return None
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_incomplete",
            "类目尚未配置生产维度方案，请选择已发布版本。",
        )
    if not schema_key or not version:
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_incomplete",
            "类目维度合同不完整，请同时配置维度方案和版本。",
        )

    matches = db.scalars(
        select(DimensionSchema).where(
            DimensionSchema.schema_key == schema_key,
            DimensionSchema.version == version,
        )
    ).all()
    if not matches:
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_missing",
            "类目绑定的维度方案不存在，请重新选择已发布版本。",
        )
    if len(matches) != 1:
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_ambiguous",
            "类目绑定的维度方案存在重复版本，已停止生产执行。",
        )
    schema = matches[0]
    if schema.status != "published":
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_not_published",
            "类目绑定的维度方案尚未发布或已停用，请选择现役发布版本。",
        )
    try:
        definition = json.loads(schema.definition_json)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_invalid",
            "类目绑定的维度方案内容损坏，已停止生产执行。",
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(definition, dict) or canonical_hash(definition) != schema.canonical_hash:
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_invalid",
            "类目绑定的维度方案校验失败，已停止生产执行。",
        )

    try:
        validate_dimension_scoring_contract(definition)
    except (DimensionScoringContractError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_not_executable",
            "类目绑定的维度方案与当前评分引擎不兼容，已停止生产执行。",
        ) from exc

    if bundle is not None:
        try:
            frozen_set = json.loads(bundle.dimension_schema_set_snapshot or "")
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
                "dimension_contract_not_executable",
                "StrategyBundle 冻结维度合同损坏，已停止生产执行。",
            ) from exc
        entries = frozen_set.get("schemas") if isinstance(frozen_set, dict) else None
        frozen_matches = [
            entry
            for entry in entries or []
            if isinstance(entry, dict)
            and entry.get("schema_key") == schema_key
            and entry.get("version") == version
        ]
        if len(frozen_matches) != 1:
            raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
                "dimension_contract_not_executable",
                "StrategyBundle 未唯一冻结类目现役维度方案，已停止生产执行。",
            )
        frozen = frozen_matches[0]
        if (
            frozen.get("canonical_hash") != schema.canonical_hash
            or frozen.get("definition") != definition
        ):
            raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
                "dimension_contract_not_executable",
                "StrategyBundle 冻结维度方案与注册表不一致，已停止生产执行。",
            )
    return ProductionDimensionContract(
        schema_id=schema.id,
        schema_key=schema.schema_key,
        version=schema.version,
        canonical_hash=schema.canonical_hash,
        definition=definition,
    )


def resolve_frozen_dimension_contract(
    snapshot: dict[str, Any],
    *,
    bundle: StrategyBundle | None = None,
) -> ProductionDimensionContract | None:
    """Validate a dimension contract frozen when a job was accepted."""
    frozen = snapshot.get("dimension_contract")
    if frozen is None:
        return None
    if not isinstance(frozen, dict):
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_invalid",
            "任务冻结维度合同损坏，已停止生产执行。",
        )
    definition = frozen.get("definition")
    schema_key = frozen.get("schema_key")
    version = frozen.get("version")
    schema_hash = frozen.get("canonical_hash")
    schema_id = frozen.get("schema_id")
    if (
        not isinstance(schema_id, int)
        or not isinstance(schema_key, str)
        or not isinstance(version, str)
        or not isinstance(schema_hash, str)
        or not isinstance(definition, dict)
        or canonical_hash(definition) != schema_hash
    ):
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_invalid",
            "任务冻结维度合同校验失败，已停止生产执行。",
        )
    try:
        validate_dimension_scoring_contract(definition)
    except (DimensionScoringContractError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_not_executable",
            "任务冻结维度合同与当前评分引擎不兼容，已停止生产执行。",
        ) from exc
    if bundle is not None:
        try:
            frozen_set = json.loads(bundle.dimension_schema_set_snapshot or "")
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
                "dimension_contract_not_executable",
                "StrategyBundle 冻结维度合同损坏，已停止生产执行。",
            ) from exc
        entries = frozen_set.get("schemas") if isinstance(frozen_set, dict) else None
        matches = [
            entry for entry in entries or []
            if isinstance(entry, dict)
            and entry.get("schema_key") == schema_key
            and entry.get("version") == version
            and entry.get("canonical_hash") == schema_hash
            and entry.get("definition") == definition
        ]
        if len(matches) != 1:
            raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
                "dimension_contract_not_executable",
                "StrategyBundle 与任务冻结维度合同不一致，已停止生产执行。",
            )
    return ProductionDimensionContract(
        schema_id=schema_id,
        schema_key=schema_key,
        version=version,
        canonical_hash=schema_hash,
        definition=definition,
    )

===== FILE backend/app/production_feedback.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import append_audit_event, canonical_json
from .models import EvaluationCategoryProfile, OptimizationCaseQueue, ProductionFeedbackEvent


SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION = "production-feedback-v1"
SUPPORTED_EVENT_TYPE = "human_correction_finalized"


class FeedbackConflict(ValueError):
    pass


def ingest_production_feedback(
    db: Session,
    *,
    event_id: str,
    schema_version: str,
    event_type: str,
    source_system: str,
    occurred_at: datetime,
    payload: dict[str, Any],
    received_by: str,
) -> tuple[ProductionFeedbackEvent, OptimizationCaseQueue, bool]:
    if schema_version != SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION:
        raise ValueError("不支持的生产反馈 schema_version")
    if event_type != SUPPORTED_EVENT_TYPE:
        raise ValueError("不支持的生产反馈 event_type")
    required = {
        "production_case_id",
        "category_key",
        "prompt_version",
        "severity",
        "model_output",
        "human_truth",
    }
    if not required.issubset(payload):
        raise ValueError("生产反馈 payload 缺少必填字段")
    if payload["severity"] not in {"P0", "P1", "P2", "P3"}:
        raise ValueError("生产反馈 severity 非法")
    if not isinstance(payload["model_output"], dict) or not isinstance(
        payload["human_truth"], dict
    ):
        raise ValueError("生产反馈模型结果与人工真值必须为对象")
    prompt_version = str(payload["prompt_version"]).strip()
    if not prompt_version or len(prompt_version) > 40:
        raise ValueError("生产反馈 prompt_version 非法")
    projected_label_version = payload.get("projected_label_version")
    if projected_label_version is not None and (
        isinstance(projected_label_version, bool)
        or not isinstance(projected_label_version, int)
        or projected_label_version < 1
    ):
        raise ValueError("生产反馈 projected_label_version 必须为正整数")
    shadow_batches = payload.get("shadow_batches")
    normalized_shadow_batches: list[str] | None = None
    if shadow_batches is not None:
        if not isinstance(shadow_batches, list) or len(shadow_batches) > 20:
            raise ValueError("生产反馈 shadow_batches 必须是不超过 20 项的数组")
        normalized_shadow_batches = []
        for batch_id in shadow_batches:
            if not isinstance(batch_id, str):
                raise ValueError("生产反馈 shadow_batches 只能包含字符串")
            normalized_batch_id = batch_id.strip()
            if not normalized_batch_id or len(normalized_batch_id) > 160:
                raise ValueError("生产反馈 shadow_batches 包含非法批次标识")
            if normalized_batch_id in normalized_shadow_batches:
                raise ValueError("生产反馈 shadow_batches 不能包含重复批次")
            normalized_shadow_batches.append(normalized_batch_id)
    category_key = str(payload["category_key"]).strip()
    profile = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
            EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == category_key,
            EvaluationCategoryProfile.status == "active",
        )
    )
    if profile is None:
        raise ValueError("生产反馈 category_key 非法")

    payload_json = canonical_json(payload)
    payload_hash = hashlib.sha256(payload_json.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionFeedbackEvent).where(
            ProductionFeedbackEvent.event_id == event_id
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        existing_occurred = existing.occurred_at
        if existing_occurred.tzinfo is None:
            existing_occurred = existing_occurred.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        incoming_occurred = occurred_at
        if incoming_occurred.tzinfo is None:
            incoming_occurred = incoming_occurred.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        if (
            existing.payload_hash != payload_hash
            or existing.schema_version != schema_version
            or existing.event_type != event_type
            or existing.source_system != source_system
            or existing_occurred != incoming_occurred
        ):
            raise FeedbackConflict("同一 event_id 的事件载荷不一致")
        existing_case = db.scalar(
            select(OptimizationCaseQueue).where(
                OptimizationCaseQueue.source_event_id == existing.id
            )
        )
        if existing_case is None:
            raise RuntimeError("生产反馈事件缺少优化队列映射")
        return existing, existing_case, True

    event = ProductionFeedbackEvent(
        event_id=event_id,
        schema_version=schema_version,
        event_type=event_type,
        source_system=source_system,
        occurred_at=occurred_at,
        payload_hash=payload_hash,
        payload_json=payload_json,
        status="mapped",
        received_by=received_by,
    )
    db.add(event)
    db.flush()
    case_payload = {
        "schema_version": "optimization-case-v1",
        "source": "production_feedback",
        "source_event_id": event_id,
        "production_case_id": payload["production_case_id"],
        "model_output": payload["model_output"],
        "human_truth": payload["human_truth"],
        "reason_codes": payload.get("reason_codes", []),
        "production_applied": bool(payload.get("production_applied", False)),
        "writes_production_database": False,
    }
    if projected_label_version is not None:
        case_payload["projected_label_version"] = projected_label_version
    if normalized_shadow_batches is not None:
        case_payload["shadow_batches"] = normalized_shadow_batches
    case = OptimizationCaseQueue(
        category_key=category_key,
        idempotency_key=f"production:{event_id}",
        evaluation_id=None,
        final_review_id=None,
        source_type="production_feedback",
        source_event_id=event.id,
        prompt_version=prompt_version,
        severity=str(payload["severity"]),
        case_json=canonical_json(case_payload),
        status="pending",
    )
    db.add(case)
    db.flush()
    append_audit_event(
        db,
        category="production_feedback",
        action="accepted",
        subject_type="production_feedback_event",
        subject_id=event.event_id,
        actor=received_by,
        payload={
            "payload_hash": payload_hash,
            "optimization_case_id": case.id,
            "writes_production_database": False,
        },
        event_key=f"production-feedback:{event_id}",
    )
    return event, case, False

===== FILE backend/app/production_title_normalization.py =====
"""Versioned 3DSU title formatting; never rewrite visual facts or scores."""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Any

PROFILE = "3dsu-chinese-title-preserve-latin-v1"
CONFIG_KEY = "production_title_normalization_profile"
# Keep complete Latin phrases (including brand spelling, spaces and digits).
_LATIN = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9]*(?:[ '\u2019&.-]+[A-Za-z0-9]+)*")
_CHINESE = re.compile(r"[\u3400-\u4dbf\u4e00-\u9fff]+")


def frozen_title_profile(node: Mapping[str, Any], category_key: str) -> str | None:
    config = node.get("config") or {}
    profile = config.get(CONFIG_KEY)
    if profile is None:
        return None
    if (profile != PROFILE or category_key != "model_3d_su"
            or node.get("node_instance_id") != "prompt_a"
            or (config.get("output_contract") or {}).get("name") != "model_3d_su_call_a"):
        raise ValueError("标题规范策略只允许冻结到 3DSU 调用 A 的标准合同")
    return profile


def normalize_production_title(contract, parsed: dict[str, Any], profile: str | None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Apply an atomic, idempotent format repair and return bounded audit facts.

    Only remove Latin text when the remaining title is a complete Chinese
    string of 1..10 characters. Never truncate, translate, invent a subject,
    drop numbers/punctuation, replace tags or relax the frozen contract.
    Raw provider text/payload remain untouched in DoubaoResponse.
    """
    if profile is None:
        return []
    if profile != PROFILE or contract.name != "model_3d_su_call_a":
        raise ValueError("Unsupported production title normalization profile")
    fields = parsed.get("production_fields")
    if not isinstance(fields, dict):
        return []
    title, seo, tags = (fields.get(key) for key in ("title", "seotitle", "tags"))
    title_schema = contract.schema.get("properties", {}).get("production_fields", {}).get("properties", {}).get("title", {})
    if (title_schema.get("maxLength") != 10 or not isinstance(title, str)
            or not 10 < len(title) <= 80 or not isinstance(seo, str)
            or not isinstance(tags, list) or not all(isinstance(tag, str) for tag in tags)):
        return []
    phrases = list(dict.fromkeys(match.group() for match in _LATIN.finditer(title)))
    shorter = _LATIN.sub("", title).strip()
    if not phrases or not 1 <= len(shorter) <= 10 or not _CHINESE.fullmatch(shorter):
        return []
    updated_tags = list(tags)
    preserved = []
    for phrase in phrases:
        if phrase in seo:
            location = "production_fields.seotitle"
        else:
            index = next((i for i, tag in enumerate(updated_tags) if phrase in tag), None)
            if index is None:
                index = len(updated_tags)
                updated_tags.append(phrase)
            location = f"production_fields.tags[{index}]"
        preserved.append({"text": phrase, "field": location})
    trace = [{"profile": PROFILE, "field": "production_fields.title",
              "before": title, "after": shorter, "preserved_text": preserved}]
    if updated_tags != tags:
        trace.append({"profile": PROFILE, "field": "production_fields.tags",
                      "before": list(tags), "after": updated_tags})
    fields["title"] = shorter
    fields["tags"] = updated_tags
    return trace

===== FILE backend/app/projection_contracts.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Mapping

from sqlalchemy import delete, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .models import (
    LocalProjectionRow,
    ProjectionContract,
    ProjectionManifest,
    ProjectionReconciliation,
    PublishedLabel,
)


BUILTIN_LOCAL_TARGETS = {
    "unified_dimension": "unified_dimension_table",
    "search_labels": "search_labels_small_table",
    "quality_governance": "quality_governance_small_table",
}

_ALLOWED_ROOTS = {
    "content_key",
    "category_key",
    "level",
    "score",
    "classification",
    "dimensions",
    "production_fields",
    "image_quality",
    "media_form",
    "semantic",
    "quality",
    "governance",
}
_ALLOWED_PROVENANCE = {
    "asset_version_id",
    "asset_id",
    "asset_sha256",
    "asset_scope",
    "is_single",
    "evaluation_id",
    "final_review_id",
    "strategy_bundle_id",
    "model_id",
    "prompt_a_version",
    "prompt_b_version",
    "rubric_version",
    "engine_version",
    "tag_contract_version",
    "normalization_version",
    "mapping_version",
}
_ALLOWED_LABEL_META = {
    "$label.id",
    "$label.release_id",
    "$label.version",
    "$label.schema_version",
    "$label.payload_hash",
    "$label.published_at",
}
_FORBIDDEN_TOKENS = {
    "raw_response",
    "candidate",
    "human_review",
    "review",
    "credential",
    "api_key",
    "password",
    "token",
    "secret",
    "manual_process",
}


class ProjectionContractError(ValueError):
    pass
