def _hash(value: Any) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(value).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def validate_projection_contract(
    *,
    target_role: str,
    table_name: str,
    environment: str,
    primary_key: list[str],
    field_mappings: Mapping[str, str],
    write_policy: str = "local_only",
    target_key: str | None = None,
    category_key: str | None = None,
    field_contract_id: int | None = None,
    max_batch_size: int = 500,
    adapter_key: str = "local-sqlite",
) -> None:
    if environment == "shadow":
        if (
            write_policy != "shadow_only"
            or not target_key
            or not category_key
            or not field_contract_id
            or field_contract_id < 1
            or not adapter_key.strip()
        ):
            raise ProjectionContractError(
                "影子投影必须绑定目标、类目、字段合同和 shadow_only 策略"
            )
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*", table_name):
            raise ProjectionContractError("影子表名必须是已校验标识符")
    else:
        expected_table = BUILTIN_LOCAL_TARGETS.get(target_role)
        if expected_table is None or table_name != expected_table:
            raise ProjectionContractError("仅支持已登记的本地大维表和小表目标")
        if environment not in {"local", "test"} or write_policy != "local_only":
            raise ProjectionContractError("本地投影只允许 local/test 与 local_only")
    if not 1 <= max_batch_size <= 500:
        raise ProjectionContractError("投影批次必须在 1 到 500 条之间")
    if primary_key != ["content_key"]:
        raise ProjectionContractError("本地投影主键必须为 content_key")
    if not field_mappings or field_mappings.get("content_key") != "content_key":
        raise ProjectionContractError("字段映射必须包含 content_key")
    for target_field, source_path in field_mappings.items():
        if not target_field.strip() or not source_path.strip():
            raise ProjectionContractError("字段映射不能为空")
        if source_path in _ALLOWED_LABEL_META:
            continue
        root, _, suffix = source_path.partition(".")
        if root == "provenance" and suffix in _ALLOWED_PROVENANCE:
            continue
        normalized = f"{target_field}.{source_path}".lower()
        if any(token in normalized for token in _FORBIDDEN_TOKENS):
            raise ProjectionContractError(
                f"禁止将候选、凭据、模型原始响应或人工过程字段投影：{source_path}"
            )
        if root in _ALLOWED_ROOTS:
            continue
        raise ProjectionContractError(
            f"禁止将候选、凭据、模型原始响应或人工过程字段投影：{source_path}"
        )


def contract_payload(contract: ProjectionContract) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": contract.id,
        "contract_key": contract.contract_key,
        "version": contract.version,
        "target_role": contract.target_role,
        "table_name": contract.table_name,
        "environment": contract.environment,
        "adapter_key": contract.adapter_key,
        "target_key": contract.target_key,
        "write_policy": contract.write_policy,
        "category_key": contract.category_key,
        "field_contract_id": contract.field_contract_id,
        "max_batch_size": contract.max_batch_size,
        "primary_key": json.loads(contract.primary_key_json),
        "field_mappings": json.loads(contract.field_mappings_json),
        "input_versions": json.loads(contract.input_versions_json),
        "mode": contract.mode,
        "idempotency_key_template": contract.idempotency_key_template,
        "checkpoint": json.loads(contract.checkpoint_json),
        "reconciliation": json.loads(contract.reconciliation_json),
        "rollback": json.loads(contract.rollback_json),
        "owner": contract.owner,
        "status": contract.status,
        "contract_hash": contract.contract_hash,
        "created_by": contract.created_by,
        "created_at": contract.created_at,
    }


def create_contract_version(
    db: Session,
    *,
    contract_key: str,
    target_role: str,
    table_name: str,
    environment: str,
    primary_key: list[str],
    field_mappings: Mapping[str, str],
    input_versions: Mapping[str, Any],
    mode: str,
    idempotency_key_template: str,
    checkpoint: Mapping[str, Any],
    reconciliation: Mapping[str, Any],
    rollback: Mapping[str, Any],
    owner: str,
    status: str,
    created_by: str,
    adapter_key: str = "local-sqlite",
    target_key: str | None = None,
    write_policy: str = "local_only",
    category_key: str | None = None,
    field_contract_id: int | None = None,
    max_batch_size: int = 500,
) -> ProjectionContract:
    validate_projection_contract(
        target_role=target_role,
        table_name=table_name,
        environment=environment,
        primary_key=primary_key,
        field_mappings=field_mappings,
        write_policy=write_policy,
        target_key=target_key,
        category_key=category_key,
        field_contract_id=field_contract_id,
        max_batch_size=max_batch_size,
        adapter_key=adapter_key,
    )
    latest = db.scalar(
        select(ProjectionContract)
        .where(ProjectionContract.contract_key == contract_key)
        .order_by(ProjectionContract.version.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )
    version = (latest.version + 1) if latest else 1
    definition = {
        "contract_key": contract_key,
        "version": version,
        "target_role": target_role,
        "table_name": table_name,
        "environment": environment,
        "adapter_key": adapter_key,
        "target_key": target_key,
        "write_policy": write_policy,
        "category_key": category_key,
        "field_contract_id": field_contract_id,
        "max_batch_size": max_batch_size,
        "primary_key": primary_key,
        "field_mappings": dict(field_mappings),
        "input_versions": dict(input_versions),
        "mode": mode,
        "idempotency_key_template": idempotency_key_template,
        "checkpoint": dict(checkpoint),
        "reconciliation": dict(reconciliation),
        "rollback": dict(rollback),
        "owner": owner,
        "status": status,
    }
    contract = ProjectionContract(
        contract_key=contract_key,
        version=version,
        target_role=target_role,
        table_name=table_name,
        environment=environment,
        adapter_key=adapter_key,
        target_key=target_key,
        write_policy=write_policy,
        category_key=category_key,
        field_contract_id=field_contract_id,
        max_batch_size=max_batch_size,
        primary_key_json=canonical_json(primary_key),
        field_mappings_json=canonical_json(dict(field_mappings)),
        input_versions_json=canonical_json(dict(input_versions)),
        mode=mode,
        idempotency_key_template=idempotency_key_template,
        checkpoint_json=canonical_json(dict(checkpoint)),
        reconciliation_json=canonical_json(dict(reconciliation)),
        rollback_json=canonical_json(dict(rollback)),
        owner=owner,
        status=status,
        contract_hash=_hash(definition),
        created_by=created_by,
    )
    db.add(contract)
    db.flush()
    return contract


def _lookup(payload: Mapping[str, Any], path: str) -> Any:
    parts = path.split(".")
    if len(parts) == 4 and parts[0] == "semantic" and parts[2] in {"primary_name", "weighted_names"}:
        field = payload.get("semantic")
        field_payload = field.get(parts[1]) if isinstance(field, Mapping) else None
        values = field_payload.get("values") if isinstance(field_payload, Mapping) else None
        if not isinstance(values, list):
            return "" if parts[2] == "primary_name" else ""
        locale = parts[3]
        rendered: list[tuple[str, float | None]] = []
        for item in values:
            if not isinstance(item, Mapping):
                continue
            names = item.get("localized_names") or item.get("names") or {}
            name = names.get(locale) if isinstance(names, Mapping) else None
            name = str(name or item.get("value") or "").strip()
            if name:
                weight = item.get("weight")
                rendered.append((name, float(weight) if isinstance(weight, (int, float)) and not isinstance(weight, bool) else None))
        if parts[2] == "primary_name":
            return rendered[0][0] if rendered else ""
        return ",".join(
            f"{name}_{weight:g}" if weight is not None else name
            for name, weight in rendered
        )
    if path == "provenance.is_single":
        provenance = payload.get("provenance")
        if isinstance(provenance, Mapping) and provenance.get("is_single") is not None:
            return int(bool(provenance["is_single"]))
        return 1 if isinstance(provenance, Mapping) and provenance.get("asset_scope") == "single" else 0
    value: Any = payload
    for part in path.split("."):
        if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
            return None
        value = value.get(part)
    return value


def _label_meta(label: PublishedLabel, source_path: str) -> Any:
    return {
        "$label.id": label.id,
        "$label.release_id": label.release_id,
        "$label.version": label.version,
        "$label.schema_version": label.label_schema_version,
        "$label.payload_hash": label.payload_hash,
        "$label.published_at": label.published_at.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat()
        if label.published_at.tzinfo
        else label.published_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc).isoformat(),
    }[source_path]


def _projection_row(
    label: PublishedLabel,
    *,
    field_mappings: Mapping[str, str],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    payload = json.loads(label.label_payload_json or "{}")
    row = {
        target: (
            _label_meta(label, source)
            if source.startswith("$label.")
            else _lookup(payload, source)
        )
        for target, source in sorted(field_mappings.items())
    }
    row["_projection_version"] = {
        "published_label_id": label.id,
        "label_version": label.version,
    }
    return row


def _manifest_payload(
    contract: ProjectionContract,
    *,
    rows: list[dict[str, Any]],
    labels: list[PublishedLabel],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    payload_hash = _hash(rows)
    provenance = [json.loads(label.label_payload_json or "{}").get("provenance") or {} for label in labels]
    input_versions = {
        "label_schema_versions": sorted({label.label_schema_version for label in labels}),
        "label_release_versions": sorted({label.version for label in labels}),
        "asset_versions": sorted(
            {str(item.get("asset_version_id") or item.get("asset_sha256") or item.get("asset_id")) for item in provenance if item.get("asset_version_id") or item.get("asset_sha256") or item.get("asset_id")}
        ),
        "mechanism_versions": sorted(
            {f"strategy-bundle:{item['strategy_bundle_id']}" for item in provenance if item.get("strategy_bundle_id") is not None}
        ),
        "model_versions": sorted(
            {str(item["model_id"]) for item in provenance if item.get("model_id")}
        ),
        "tag_contract_versions": sorted(
            {str(item["tag_contract_version"]) for item in provenance if item.get("tag_contract_version")}
        ),
        "mapping_versions": sorted(
            {str(item["mapping_version"]) for item in provenance if item.get("mapping_version")}
        ),
    }
    base = {
        "schema_version": "projection-manifest-v1",
        "contract_id": contract.id,
        "contract_key": contract.contract_key,
        "contract_version": contract.version,
        "contract_hash": contract.contract_hash,
        "target_role": contract.target_role,
        "target_table": contract.table_name,
        "environment": contract.environment,
        "mode": contract.mode,
        "row_count": len(rows),
        "content_keys": [str(row["content_key"]) for row in rows],
        "payload_hash": payload_hash,
        "input_versions": input_versions,
        "rows": rows,
    }
    return {**base, "manifest_hash": _hash(base)}


def build_projection_manifest(
    db: Session,
    *,
    contract: ProjectionContract,
) -> tuple[ProjectionManifest, dict[str, Any]]:
    mappings = json.loads(contract.field_mappings_json)
    labels = list(
        db.scalars(
            select(PublishedLabel)
            .where(PublishedLabel.status == "published")
            .order_by(PublishedLabel.content_key.asc(), PublishedLabel.id.asc())
        ).all()
    )
    rows = [_projection_row(label, field_mappings=mappings) for label in labels]
    payload = _manifest_payload(contract, rows=rows, labels=labels)
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(ProjectionManifest).where(
            ProjectionManifest.contract_id == contract.id,
            ProjectionManifest.manifest_hash == payload["manifest_hash"],
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        return existing, payload
    manifest = ProjectionManifest(
        contract_id=contract.id,
        manifest_hash=payload["manifest_hash"],
        payload_hash=payload["payload_hash"],
        row_count=payload["row_count"],
        content_keys_json=canonical_json(payload["content_keys"]),
        input_versions_json=canonical_json(payload["input_versions"]),
        rows_json=canonical_json(rows),
    )
    db.add(manifest)
    db.flush()
    return manifest, payload


def manifest_payload(manifest: ProjectionManifest, contract: ProjectionContract) -> dict[str, Any]:
    rows = json.loads(manifest.rows_json)
    return {
        "id": manifest.id,
        "schema_version": "projection-manifest-v1",
        "contract_id": contract.id,
        "contract_key": contract.contract_key,
        "contract_version": contract.version,
        "contract_hash": contract.contract_hash,
        "target_role": contract.target_role,
        "target_table": contract.table_name,
        "environment": contract.environment,
        "mode": contract.mode,
        "row_count": manifest.row_count,
        "content_keys": json.loads(manifest.content_keys_json),
        "payload_hash": manifest.payload_hash,
        "manifest_hash": manifest.manifest_hash,
        "input_versions": json.loads(manifest.input_versions_json),
        "rows": rows,
        "created_at": manifest.created_at,
    }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReconciliationResult:
    status: str
    reason: str
    row_count: int
    missing_count: int
    unexpected_count: int
    payload_hash: str
    version_match: bool


class LocalProjectionAdapter:
    def apply(
        self,
        db: Session,
        *,
        contract: ProjectionContract,
        manifest: ProjectionManifest,
    ) -> None:
        rows = json.loads(manifest.rows_json)
        expected_keys = {str(row["content_key"]) for row in rows}
        existing = {
            row.content_key: row
            for row in db.scalars(
                select(LocalProjectionRow).where(
                    LocalProjectionRow.table_name == contract.table_name
                )
            ).all()
        }
        for row in rows:
            content_key = str(row["content_key"])
            version = row.get("_projection_version") or {}
            raw = canonical_json(row)
            stored = existing.get(content_key)
            if stored is None:
                stored = LocalProjectionRow(
                    table_name=contract.table_name,
                    content_key=content_key,
                    contract_id=contract.id,
                    contract_version=contract.version,
                    published_label_id=int(version["published_label_id"]),
                    label_version=int(version["label_version"]),
                    payload_json=raw,
                    payload_hash=_hash(row),
                )
                db.add(stored)
            else:
                stored.contract_id = contract.id
                stored.contract_version = contract.version
                stored.published_label_id = int(version["published_label_id"])
                stored.label_version = int(version["label_version"])
                stored.payload_json = raw
                stored.payload_hash = _hash(row)
                stored.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if contract.mode == "snapshot":
            stale_keys = set(existing) - expected_keys
            if stale_keys:
                db.execute(
                    delete(LocalProjectionRow).where(
                        LocalProjectionRow.table_name == contract.table_name,
                        LocalProjectionRow.content_key.in_(stale_keys),
                    )
                )
        db.flush()

    def reconcile(
        self,
        db: Session,
        *,
        contract: ProjectionContract,
        manifest: ProjectionManifest,
    ) -> ReconciliationResult:
        expected_rows = json.loads(manifest.rows_json)
        actual_models = list(
            db.scalars(
                select(LocalProjectionRow)
                .where(LocalProjectionRow.table_name == contract.table_name)
                .order_by(LocalProjectionRow.content_key.asc())
            ).all()
        )
        actual_rows = [json.loads(row.payload_json) for row in actual_models]
        expected_keys = {str(row["content_key"]) for row in expected_rows}
        actual_keys = {row.content_key for row in actual_models}
        missing = expected_keys - actual_keys
        unexpected = actual_keys - expected_keys
        actual_hash = _hash(actual_rows)
        version_match = all(
            row.contract_id == contract.id and row.contract_version == contract.version
            for row in actual_models
        )
        reason = ""
        if missing:
            reason = "missing_rows"
        elif unexpected:
            reason = "unexpected_rows"
        elif actual_hash != manifest.payload_hash:
            reason = "payload_hash_mismatch"
        elif not version_match:
            reason = "version_mismatch"
        return ReconciliationResult(
            status="matched" if not reason else "drift",
            reason=reason,
            row_count=len(actual_rows),
            missing_count=len(missing),
            unexpected_count=len(unexpected),
            payload_hash=actual_hash,
            version_match=version_match,
        )


def persist_reconciliation(
    db: Session,
    *,
    contract: ProjectionContract,
    manifest: ProjectionManifest,
    result: ReconciliationResult,
) -> ProjectionReconciliation:
    record = ProjectionReconciliation(
        contract_id=contract.id,
        manifest_id=manifest.id,
        target_table=contract.table_name,
        status=result.status,
        reason=result.reason,
        row_count=result.row_count,
        missing_count=result.missing_count,
        unexpected_count=result.unexpected_count,
        expected_payload_hash=manifest.payload_hash,
        actual_payload_hash=result.payload_hash,
        version_match=result.version_match,
        checkpoint_json=contract.checkpoint_json,
        compensation_json=canonical_json(
            {
                "retryable": result.status != "matched",
                "strategy": "rebuild_from_published_labels",
                "canonical_rows_mutated": False,
            }
        ),
    )
    db.add(record)
    db.flush()
    return record


def reconciliation_payload(record: ProjectionReconciliation) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": record.id,
        "contract_id": record.contract_id,
        "manifest_id": record.manifest_id,
        "target_table": record.target_table,
        "status": record.status,
        "reason": record.reason,
        "row_count": record.row_count,
        "missing_count": record.missing_count,
        "unexpected_count": record.unexpected_count,
        "expected_payload_hash": record.expected_payload_hash,
        "payload_hash": record.actual_payload_hash,
        "version_match": record.version_match,
        "checkpoint": json.loads(record.checkpoint_json),
        "compensation": json.loads(record.compensation_json),
        "created_at": record.created_at,
    }

===== FILE backend/app/prompt_contract_merge.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from copy import deepcopy
import hashlib
from typing import Any

from .audit import canonical_json


class PromptContractMergeError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, path: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.path = path


def _invalid(message: str, *, path: str = "$") -> None:
    raise PromptContractMergeError(
        "prompt_contract_invalid",
        message,
        path=path,
    )


def _conflict(path: str) -> None:
    raise PromptContractMergeError(
        "prompt_contract_field_conflict",
        f"严格输出合同在 {path} 的字段语义冲突",
        path=path,
    )


def _canonical_mapping(value: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    normalized: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for key in sorted(value):
        child = value[key]
        if isinstance(child, Mapping):
            normalized[key] = _canonical_mapping(child)
        elif isinstance(child, list):
            normalized[key] = [
                _canonical_mapping(item) if isinstance(item, Mapping) else deepcopy(item)
                for item in child
            ]
        else:
            normalized[key] = deepcopy(child)
    return normalized


def _normalize_schema(schema: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if schema.get("type") == "object":
        meta = {
            key: value
            for key, value in schema.items()
            if key not in {"properties", "required", "additionalProperties"}
        }
        properties = {
            key: _normalize_schema(schema["properties"][key])
            for key in sorted(schema["properties"])
        }
        return {
            **_canonical_mapping(meta),
            "properties": properties,
            "required": sorted(properties),
            "additionalProperties": False,
        }
    if schema.get("type") == "array":
        meta = {key: value for key, value in schema.items() if key != "items"}
        return {
            **_canonical_mapping(meta),
            "items": _normalize_schema(schema["items"]),
        }
    return _canonical_mapping(schema)


def _validate_schema(schema: Any, *, path: str) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(schema, Mapping):
        _invalid("严格输出 schema 节点必须是对象", path=path)
    schema_type = schema.get("type")
    if schema_type == "object":
        properties = schema.get("properties")
        required = schema.get("required")
        if not isinstance(properties, Mapping):
            _invalid("严格对象必须声明 properties", path=path)
        if schema.get("additionalProperties") is not False:
            _invalid("严格对象必须关闭 additionalProperties", path=path)
        if (
            not isinstance(required, list)
            or any(not isinstance(item, str) for item in required)
            or len(required) != len(set(required))
            or set(required) != set(properties)
        ):
            _invalid("严格对象的所有固定字段都必须 required", path=path)
        for key, child in properties.items():
            if not isinstance(key, str) or not key:
                _invalid("严格对象字段名不能为空", path=path)
            _validate_schema(child, path=f"{path}.{key}")
    elif schema_type == "array":
        if "items" not in schema:
            _invalid("严格数组必须声明 items", path=path)
        _validate_schema(schema["items"], path=f"{path}[]")
    elif schema_type is None:
        _invalid("严格输出 schema 节点必须声明 type", path=path)
    return schema


def _merge_schema(
    left: Mapping[str, Any],
    right: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    path: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if left.get("type") != right.get("type"):
        _conflict(path)
    schema_type = left.get("type")
    if schema_type == "object":
        left_meta = {
            key: value
            for key, value in left.items()
            if key not in {"properties", "required", "additionalProperties"}
        }
        right_meta = {
            key: value
            for key, value in right.items()
            if key not in {"properties", "required", "additionalProperties"}
        }
        if canonical_json(left_meta) != canonical_json(right_meta):
            _conflict(path)
        left_properties = left["properties"]
        right_properties = right["properties"]
        merged_properties: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for key in sorted(set(left_properties) | set(right_properties)):
            if key in left_properties and key in right_properties:
                merged_properties[key] = _merge_schema(
                    left_properties[key],
                    right_properties[key],
                    path=f"{path}.{key}",
                )
            elif key in left_properties:
                merged_properties[key] = _normalize_schema(left_properties[key])
            else:
                merged_properties[key] = _normalize_schema(right_properties[key])
        return {
            **_canonical_mapping(left_meta),
            "properties": merged_properties,
            "required": sorted(merged_properties),
            "additionalProperties": False,
        }
    if schema_type == "array":
        left_meta = {key: value for key, value in left.items() if key != "items"}
        right_meta = {key: value for key, value in right.items() if key != "items"}
        if canonical_json(left_meta) != canonical_json(right_meta):
            _conflict(path)
        return {
            **_canonical_mapping(left_meta),
            "items": _merge_schema(left["items"], right["items"], path=f"{path}[]"),
        }
    if canonical_json(left) != canonical_json(right):
        _conflict(path)
    return _canonical_mapping(left)


def merge_strict_prompt_contracts(
    contracts: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    *,
    name: str,
    description: str = "",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if (
        not isinstance(contracts, Sequence)
        or isinstance(contracts, (str, bytes))
        or not contracts
    ):
        _invalid("至少需要一个严格输出合同")
    if not isinstance(name, str) or not name.strip():
        _invalid("合并合同必须有稳定名称")
    if not isinstance(description, str):
        _invalid("合并合同描述必须是文本")

    schemas: list[Mapping[str, Any]] = []
    for index, contract in enumerate(contracts):
        if not isinstance(contract, Mapping):
            _invalid("严格输出合同必须是对象", path=f"$[{index}]")
        if contract.get("mode") != "json_schema" or contract.get("strict") is not True:
            _invalid("只能合并 mode=json_schema 且 strict=true 的合同", path=f"$[{index}]")
        schema = _validate_schema(contract.get("schema"), path="$")
        if schema.get("type") != "object":
            _invalid("严格输出合同根节点必须是对象", path="$")
        schemas.append(schema)

    ordered = sorted(schemas, key=canonical_json)
    merged = _normalize_schema(ordered[0])
    for schema in ordered[1:]:
        merged = _merge_schema(merged, schema, path="$")
    return {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": name.strip(),
        "description": description,
        "strict": True,
        "schema": merged,
    }


def prompt_contract_hash(contract: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(contract).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

"""存量实验 API 基础服务。

提示词/字段便捷候选保持模型与路由不变；stock_lab 提供独立完整草稿、
组件、样本和确认后参考答案修订。只开放已登记未退役类目。
不改增量绑定、共享配置或正式下游；写入串行、幂等和审计。
旧 cancel 仅取消自身回归；新 control 逐项校验原生存量身份。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence
import uuid

from sqlalchemy import and_, func, select, text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, selectinload

from .audit import append_audit_event
from .models import (
    Asset,
    AuditEvent,
    BaselineRegressionItem,
    BaselineRegressionRun,
    BaselineSet,
    BaselineSetItem,
    BaselineTruthRevision,
    PromptVersion,
    WorkflowNodeAsset,
    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding,
    WorkflowVersion,
)

def allowed_categories(db: Session) -> frozenset[str]:
    from .models import EvaluationCategoryProfile
    return frozenset(db.scalars(select(EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key).where(
        EvaluationCategoryProfile.status != "retired")))
LAB_ACTOR = "prompt-lab:codex"
LAB_VERSION_PREFIX = "lab-"
MAX_PROMPT_CHARS = 30_000
MAX_NOTE_CHARS = 2_000
MAX_ACTIVE_RUNS = 1
DEFAULT_DAILY_ITEM_CAP = 12_000
MAX_ITEMS_PAGE = 500
#: 实验室只跑真实模型；测试里换成 simulated 走夹具执行器。
MODEL_EXECUTION_MODE = "real"
_PLACEHOLDER = re.compile(r"\{\{[^{}]+\}\}")


class PromptLabError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int = 422) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


LAB_LOCK_NAME = "label-system:prompt-lab"
LAB_LOCK_WAIT_SECONDS = 30
_LOCK_ENGINES: dict[str, Any] = {}


def _lock_engine(business_engine: Any) -> Any:
    """命名锁专用连接通道（ls-review-r24 F1）。

    锁连接若从业务连接池借，持锁者做事务还要再借一条，等锁的请求也各占一条等着，
    池一小（合法配置 pool_size=1/2、无溢出）就互相卡死。这里用同一个库地址另建不入池的
    NullPool 引擎：等锁只占自己的物理连接，关闭即断开，MySQL 随之释放会话级命名锁。
    """
    key = business_engine.url.render_as_string(hide_password=False)
    engine = _LOCK_ENGINES.get(key)
    if engine is None:
        from sqlalchemy import create_engine
        from sqlalchemy.pool import NullPool

        engine = create_engine(business_engine.url, poolclass=NullPool, connect_args={"connect_timeout": 10})
        _LOCK_ENGINES[key] = engine
    return engine


class LabWriteLock:
    """实验室写操作（建候选、开回归、取消）的全局串行锁。

    ls-review-r23 R1/S1：并发请求各自读到"没有在跑、额度够"，就能越过一轮上限与日额度；
    同一候选首次并发创建会撞唯一键成 500。所以必须在本请求第一次读库之前拿锁、提交之后才放：
    MySQL 用独立连接上的命名锁（会话连接提交后会还回连接池，锁不能挂在它上面），
    锁前没有读过库也就没有旧的可重复读视图；SQLite 在本会话 BEGIN IMMEDIATE。
    """

    def __init__(self, db: Session) -> None:
        self._db = db
        self._conn = None

    def __enter__(self) -> "LabWriteLock":
        if self._db.in_transaction():
            raise RuntimeError("prompt lab lock must be taken before the first read of this session")
        engine = self._db.get_bind()
        if engine.dialect.name == "mysql":
            conn = _lock_engine(engine).connect()
            try:
                got = conn.execute(
                    text("SELECT GET_LOCK(:name, :wait)"), {"name": LAB_LOCK_NAME, "wait": LAB_LOCK_WAIT_SECONDS}
                ).scalar()
            except BaseException:
                # 服务端可能已拿到锁而客户端没读到结果（ls-review-r24 F3）：作废并物理断开，锁随会话释放。
                conn.invalidate()
                conn.close()
                raise
            if got != 1:
                conn.close()
                raise PromptLabError("lab_busy", "实验室正在处理另一个写请求，请稍后重试", status_code=409)
            self._conn = conn
        else:
            from .database_capabilities import DatabaseCapabilities

            DatabaseCapabilities.for_dialect(engine.dialect.name).reserve_claim_transaction(self._db)
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *exc: Any) -> None:
        if self._conn is None:
            return
        conn, self._conn = self._conn, None
        try:
            conn.execute(text("SELECT RELEASE_LOCK(:name)"), {"name": LAB_LOCK_NAME})
        finally:
            conn.close()  # NullPool：物理断开，即使 RELEASE_LOCK 失败锁也随会话释放


def daily_item_cap() -> int:
    raw = os.getenv("PROMPT_LAB_DAILY_ITEM_CAP", "").strip()
    if raw.isdigit() and 0 < int(raw) <= 100_000:
        return int(raw)
    return DEFAULT_DAILY_ITEM_CAP


def _digest(value: Any) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), default=str).encode()
    ).hexdigest()


def _json(value: str | None) -> Any:
    try:
        return json.loads(value or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return None


def _iso(value: datetime | None) -> str | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    if value.tzinfo is None:
        value = value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return value.astimezone(timezone(timedelta(hours=8))).isoformat()


def _manifest(version: WorkflowVersion) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from .workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest

    return persisted_workflow_manifest(version)


def _require_version(db: Session, version_id: int) -> tuple[WorkflowVersion, dict[str, Any]]:
    version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, version_id)
    if version is None or version.workflow_schema_version != "workflow-v3":
        raise PromptLabError("mechanism_not_found", "评测机制版本不存在", status_code=404)
    manifest = _manifest(version)
    if manifest.get("category_key") not in allowed_categories(db):
        raise PromptLabError("category_not_allowed", "这个评测机制不在实验室开放的类目内", status_code=403)
    return version, manifest


def _prompt_nodes(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[Mapping[str, Any]]:
    return [
        node
        for node in manifest.get("nodes") or []
        if isinstance(node, Mapping)
        and isinstance(node.get("config"), Mapping)
        and "prompt_version_id" in node["config"]
    ]


def _prompt_content(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    snapshot = node["config"].get("component_snapshot") or {}
    content = snapshot.get("content") if isinstance(snapshot, Mapping) else None
    if not isinstance(content, Mapping):
        raise PromptLabError("mechanism_prompt_snapshot_missing", "机制里的提示词节点缺少冻结内容", status_code=409)
    return content


def _comparable(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """比对"候选只差提示词"时去掉模型计费价目表：调价只影响预算，不改模型行为。

    .137 实测：价目表 v2→v3 之后重新冻结老机制，模型快照只有 pricing_contract 与随之变化的
    snapshot_hash 不同，原来会把所有老机制都判成"除提示词外不一致"。模型身份、参数、输出合同照样严格比对。
    """
    copy = deepcopy(dict(manifest))
    for node in copy.get("nodes") or []:
        snapshot = (node.get("config") or {}).get("model_snapshot") if isinstance(node, dict) else None
        if isinstance(snapshot, dict):
            snapshot.pop("snapshot_hash", None)
            runtime = snapshot.get("runtime_config")
            if isinstance(runtime, dict):
                runtime.pop("pricing_contract", None)
    return copy


def is_lab_version(version: WorkflowVersion) -> bool:
    return version.created_by == LAB_ACTOR and version.version.startswith((LAB_VERSION_PREFIX, "stock-"))


def _routes(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    return [
        {"branch": edge.get("branch"), "to": edge.get("to"), "condition": edge.get("condition")}
        for edge in manifest.get("edges") or []
        if isinstance(edge, Mapping) and edge.get("branch")
    ]


def describe_mechanism(db: Session, version_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    version, manifest = _require_version(db, version_id)
    nodes = []
    for node in _prompt_nodes(manifest):
        cfg = node["config"]
        content = _prompt_content(node)
        system_prompt = str(content.get("system_prompt") or "")
        user_prompt = str(content.get("user_prompt") or "")
        nodes.append({
            "node_id": node.get("node_instance_id"),
            "display_name": node.get("display_name"),
            "prompt_version_id": cfg.get("prompt_version_id"),
            "system_prompt": system_prompt,
            "user_prompt": user_prompt,
            "placeholders": sorted(set(_PLACEHOLDER.findall(system_prompt + "\n" + user_prompt))),
            "output_contract_key": cfg.get("output_contract_key"),
            "output_contract": cfg.get("output_contract_snapshot") or cfg.get("output_contract"),
            "model": {key: (cfg.get("model_snapshot") or {}).get(key) for key in ("provider", "model_id", "name")}
            if isinstance(cfg.get("model_snapshot"), Mapping) else None,
            "temperature": cfg.get("temperature"),
        })
    return {
        "id": version.id,
        "version": version.version,
        "canonical_hash": version.canonical_hash,
        "category_key": manifest.get("category_key"),
        "status": version.status,
        "created_by": version.created_by,
        "created_at": _iso(version.created_at),
        "is_lab_candidate": is_lab_version(version),
        "routes": _routes(manifest),
        "prompt_nodes": nodes,
    }


MAX_MECHANISMS_SCANNED = 500


def list_mechanisms_page(
    db: Session,
    *,
    limit: int = 30,
    before_id: int | None = None,
    scan_cap: int = MAX_MECHANISMS_SCANNED,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """有界分页列出实验室可用的评测机制版本（按 id 倒序）。

    Astra 全仓复审 F1：原实现不带 LIMIT 把全部 workflow-v3 版本连同大正文载入再在 Python 里过滤。
    现在先用流程定义的 allowed_categories_json 在 SQL 里缩小范围，按 id 分页只取 id，
    每条再单独载入核对冻结类目并立即移出会话，内存上界按页大小而不是机制总量。

    复审 N1：一个流程定义可能同时允许多个类目，冻结类目不在开放范围的版本会被跳过；一次调用最多检查
    ``scan_cap`` 个版本（每页取 min(limit, 剩余额度)，上限在页内检查），扫满还没凑够 ``limit`` 条时
    返回 ``scan_capped=True``，不再静默返回空列表或残缺列表。还有更早的版本时给出 ``next_before_id``，
    调用方带上 ``before_id`` 接着翻。
    """
    from .models import WorkflowDefinition

    categories = allowed_categories(db)
    definition_ids = [
        definition_id
        for definition_id, allowed in db.execute(
            select(WorkflowDefinition.id, WorkflowDefinition.allowed_categories_json)
        )
        if isinstance(parsed := _json(allowed), list) and set(parsed) & categories
    ]
    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    page_info: dict[str, Any] = {"items": out, "next_before_id": None, "scan_capped": False, "scanned": 0}
    if not definition_ids:
        return page_info
    base = select(WorkflowVersion.id).where(
        WorkflowVersion.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3",
        WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id.in_(definition_ids),
    )
    last_id = before_id
    scanned = 0
    exhausted = False
    while len(out) < limit and scanned < scan_cap:
        query = base if last_id is None else base.where(WorkflowVersion.id < last_id)
        page = db.scalars(query.order_by(WorkflowVersion.id.desc()).limit(min(limit, scan_cap - scanned))).all()
        if not page:
            exhausted = True
            break
        for version_id in page:
            last_id = version_id
            scanned += 1
            version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, version_id)
            category = _manifest(version).get("category_key")
            if category in categories:
                out.append({
                    "id": version.id,
                    "version": version.version,
                    "canonical_hash": version.canonical_hash,
                    "category_key": category,
                    "status": version.status,
                    "created_by": version.created_by,
                    "created_at": _iso(version.created_at),
                    "is_lab_candidate": is_lab_version(version),
                })
            db.expunge(version)
            if len(out) >= limit:
                break
    if not exhausted and last_id is not None:
        # 只查一个 id 判断后面还有没有，避免"刚好扫完"时误报不完整。
        exhausted = db.scalar(base.where(WorkflowVersion.id < last_id).limit(1)) is None
    page_info["scanned"] = scanned
    if not exhausted:
        page_info["next_before_id"] = last_id
        page_info["scan_capped"] = len(out) < limit
    return page_info


def list_mechanisms(db: Session, *, limit: int = 30) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """兼容旧调用：只要第一页的条目。需要判断是否扫满上限、继续翻页时用 list_mechanisms_page。"""
    return list_mechanisms_page(db, limit=limit)["items"]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PromptEdit:
    system_prompt: str | None = None
    user_prompt: str | None = None


def _checked_text(value: str, *, field: str, node_id: str, allowed: set[str], allow_empty: bool = False) -> str:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or (not value.strip() and not allow_empty):
        raise PromptLabError("prompt_text_empty", f"{node_id}.{field} 不能为空")
    if len(value) > MAX_PROMPT_CHARS:
        raise PromptLabError("prompt_text_too_long", f"{node_id}.{field} 超过 {MAX_PROMPT_CHARS} 字")
    unknown = sorted(set(_PLACEHOLDER.findall(value)) - allowed)
    if unknown:
        raise PromptLabError(
            "prompt_placeholder_not_allowed",
            f"{node_id}.{field} 出现源提示词里没有的占位符：" + "、".join(unknown),
        )
    return value


def create_candidate(
    db: Session,
    *,
    source_version_id: int,
    expected_source_hash: str,
    edits: Mapping[str, PromptEdit],
    output_schemas: Mapping[str, dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
    note: str = "",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """在源机制上修改提示词和输出字段定义，冻结成一个新的评测机制版本（同样的输入得到同一个版本）。"""

    from .workflow_working_copies import WorkflowWorkingCopyError, create_immutable_evaluation_mechanism_version

    source, before = _require_version(db, source_version_id)
    if source.canonical_hash != expected_source_hash:
        raise PromptLabError("source_hash_mismatch", "源机制的 canonical_hash 与请求不一致，请重新读取机制", status_code=409)
    if len(note) > MAX_NOTE_CHARS:
        raise PromptLabError("note_too_long", f"说明不能超过 {MAX_NOTE_CHARS} 字")
    nodes = {str(node.get("node_instance_id")): node for node in _prompt_nodes(before)}
    output_schemas = output_schemas or {}
    unknown = sorted((set(edits) | set(output_schemas)) - set(nodes))
    if unknown:
        raise PromptLabError("prompt_node_unknown", "源机制里没有这些提示词节点：" + "、".join(unknown))
    planned: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for node_id, edit in sorted(edits.items()):
        content = _prompt_content(nodes[node_id])
        old_system = str(content.get("system_prompt") or "")
        old_user = str(content.get("user_prompt") or "")
        allowed = set(_PLACEHOLDER.findall(old_system + "\n" + old_user))
        system = old_system if edit.system_prompt is None else _checked_text(edit.system_prompt, field="system_prompt", node_id=node_id, allowed=allowed)
        user = old_user if edit.user_prompt is None else _checked_text(edit.user_prompt, field="user_prompt", node_id=node_id, allowed=allowed, allow_empty=True)
        if system == old_system and user == old_user:
            continue
        planned[node_id] = {"system": system, "user": user, "content": content, "cfg": nodes[node_id]["config"]}
    from .knowledge_graph_contracts import extend_knowledge_graph_contract, KnowledgeGraphContractError, knowledge_graph_strict_output_contract
    schemas = {}
    for node_id, schema in sorted(output_schemas.items()):
        try:
            contract = extend_knowledge_graph_contract(nodes[node_id]["config"].get("output_contract_key"), schema)
        except KnowledgeGraphContractError as exc:
            raise PromptLabError(exc.code, str(exc)) from exc
        if contract.snapshot() != nodes[node_id]["config"].get("output_contract_snapshot"):
            schemas[node_id] = contract
    if not planned and not schemas:
        raise PromptLabError("prompt_unchanged", "提示词没有任何变化")
    patch_hash = _digest({
        "source": source.canonical_hash,
        "prompts": {node_id: [plan["system"], plan["user"]] for node_id, plan in planned.items()},
        **({"output_schemas": {k: v.snapshot() for k, v in schemas.items()}} if schemas else {}),
    })
    version_name = f"{LAB_VERSION_PREFIX}{source.id}-{patch_hash[:16]}"
    candidate = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowVersion).where(
            WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == source.workflow_definition_id,
            WorkflowVersion.version == version_name,
        )
    )
    created = candidate is None
    expected_manifest = deepcopy(before)
    schema_nodes = set()
    for node_id, contract in schemas.items():
        old_key = nodes[node_id]["config"]["output_contract_key"]
        # A route's prompts and projections must share one field definition.
        peers = [n for n in _prompt_nodes(before) if n["config"].get("output_contract_key") == old_key]
        if len(peers) != 1:
            raise PromptLabError("schema_route_ambiguous", "同一路线有多个提示词节点，无法自动确定投影字段定义")
        for node in expected_manifest["nodes"]:
            cfg = node.get("config") or {}
            if node.get("node_instance_id") == node_id:
                cfg["output_contract_snapshot"] = contract.snapshot()
                cfg["output_contract"] = knowledge_graph_strict_output_contract(old_key, contract.snapshot())
                node["output_schema"] = contract.output_schema
                schema_nodes.add(node_id)
            elif node.get("kind") == "downstream_projection" and cfg.get("field_contract_key") == old_key:
                cfg["field_contract_snapshot"] = contract.snapshot()
                schema_nodes.add(node["node_instance_id"])
    if candidate is None:
        category = before["category_key"]
        manifest = deepcopy(expected_manifest)
        for node_id, plan in planned.items():
            cfg = plan["cfg"]
            old = db.get(PromptVersion, cfg["prompt_version_id"])
            if old is None or old.category_key != category:
                raise PromptLabError("mechanism_prompt_missing", f"{node_id} 引用的提示词版本已不可用", status_code=409)
            prompt_version = f"{version_name}-{node_id}"[:120]
            new_prompt = db.scalar(
                select(PromptVersion).where(
                    PromptVersion.category_key == category,
                    PromptVersion.stage == old.stage,
                    PromptVersion.version == prompt_version,
                )
            )
            if new_prompt is None:
                new_prompt = PromptVersion(
                    category_key=category,
                    pipeline_scope=old.pipeline_scope,
                    stage=old.stage,
                    name=f"{old.name[:100]} · 实验室",
                    version=prompt_version,
                    system_prompt=plan["system"],
                    user_prompt=plan["user"],
                    rubric_version=str(plan["content"].get("rubric_version") or old.rubric_version),
                    status="draft",
                    source="manual",
                    created_by=LAB_ACTOR,
                    change_note=("提示词实验室候选（仅评测，不会自动上线）。" + (note[:500] if note else "")),
                )
                db.add(new_prompt)
                db.flush()
            if new_prompt.system_prompt != plan["system"] or new_prompt.user_prompt != plan["user"]:
                raise PromptLabError("prompt_version_conflict", f"{node_id} 同名实验提示词内容不一致", status_code=409)
            asset = db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
                    WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == category,
                    WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key == cfg["asset_key"],
                    WorkflowNodeAsset.kind == "prompt",
                )
            )
            if asset is None:
                raise PromptLabError("prompt_asset_missing", f"{node_id} 的提示词组件不存在", status_code=409)
            if db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.id).where(
                    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.asset_id == asset.id,
                    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id == new_prompt.id,
                )
            ) is None:
                db.add(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding(asset_id=asset.id, prompt_version_id=new_prompt.id, created_by=LAB_ACTOR))
                db.flush()
            node_cfg = next(n for n in _prompt_nodes(manifest) if n.get("node_instance_id") == node_id)["config"]
            node_cfg["prompt_version_id"] = new_prompt.id
            node_cfg.pop("component_snapshot", None)
        try:
            candidate = create_immutable_evaluation_mechanism_version(
                db,
                definition=source.definition,
                version=version_name,
                manifest=manifest,
                created_by=LAB_ACTOR,
            )
        except WorkflowWorkingCopyError as exc:
            # 源机制引用的模型/组件现在冻结不了（例如模型配置被改成未登记能力档案的型号）。
            raise PromptLabError(exc.code, f"候选机制无法冻结：{exc}", status_code=409) from exc
    elif not is_lab_version(candidate):
        raise PromptLabError("candidate_name_conflict", "候选版本名已被非实验室版本占用", status_code=409)
    # 逐项核对：只允许请求中的提示词/字段定义和同路线投影同步，其他内容不变。
    frozen = _manifest(candidate)
    restored = deepcopy(frozen)
    before_nodes = {str(n.get("node_instance_id")): n for n in _prompt_nodes(before)}
    for node in _prompt_nodes(restored):
        node_id = str(node.get("node_instance_id"))
        content = _prompt_content(node)
        expected = planned.get(node_id)
        if expected is not None:
            if content.get("system_prompt") != expected["system"] or (content.get("user_prompt") or "") != expected["user"]:
                raise PromptLabError("candidate_differs_beyond_prompts", f"{node_id} 冻结后的提示词与请求不一致", status_code=409)
            for key in ("prompt_version_id", "component_snapshot"):
                node["config"][key] = deepcopy(before_nodes[node_id]["config"][key])
    if _comparable(restored) != _comparable(expected_manifest):
        # 源机制冻结之后平台组件（模型登记、输出合同等）又变过，重新冻结会带进别的差异，
        # 候选与源机制就不再只差提示词，对比失去意义。
        raise PromptLabError(
            "candidate_differs_beyond_prompts",
            "候选机制存在请求之外的差异（源机制冻结后平台组件有变化），请换用最新的源机制",
            status_code=409,
        )
    receipt = {
        "source_version_id": source.id,
        "source_hash": source.canonical_hash,
        "candidate_version_id": candidate.id,
        "candidate_hash": candidate.canonical_hash,
        "candidate_version": candidate.version,
        "changed_nodes": sorted(planned),
        "schema_changed_nodes": sorted(schema_nodes),
        "output_schema_changes": {node_id: {
            "previous_contract_hash": nodes[node_id]["config"].get("output_contract_snapshot", {}).get("contract_hash"),
            "contract_hash": contract.snapshot()["contract_hash"],
            "fields": list(contract.field_order),
            "added_fields": sorted(set(contract.field_order) - set(nodes[node_id]["config"].get("output_contract_snapshot", {}).get("field_order", []))),
            "removed_fields": sorted(set(nodes[node_id]["config"].get("output_contract_snapshot", {}).get("field_order", [])) - set(contract.field_order)),
        } for node_id, contract in schemas.items()},
        "patch_hash": patch_hash,
        "note": note,
        "created": created,
    }
    if created:
        append_audit_event(
            db,
            category="prompt_lab",
            action="candidate_created",
            subject_type="workflow_version",
            subject_id=candidate.id,
            actor=LAB_ACTOR,
            payload=receipt,
            event_key=f"prompt-lab:candidate:{candidate.id}",
        )
    return receipt


def _lab_runs_query():
    return select(BaselineRegressionRun).where(BaselineRegressionRun.created_by == LAB_ACTOR)


def start_run(
    db: Session,
    *,
    workflow_version_id: int,
    baseline_set_id: int,
    baseline_item_ids: Sequence[int] | None = None,
    client_request_id: str | None = None,
    note: str = "",
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """在已有基准集上用冻结机制跑真实回归，验证队列、业务写入隔离。"""

    from .native_baseline_regression import (
        NativeBaselineRegressionError,
        create_native_baseline_run,
    )

    version, manifest = _require_version(db, workflow_version_id)
    if version.status != "active":
        raise PromptLabError("mechanism_not_active", "只能用可执行（active）的评测机制版本跑回归", status_code=409)
    baseline_set = db.get(BaselineSet, baseline_set_id)
    if baseline_set is None:
        raise PromptLabError("baseline_set_not_found", "基准集不存在", status_code=404)
    if baseline_set.category_key != manifest.get("category_key"):
        raise PromptLabError("baseline_set_category_mismatch", "基准集与评测机制的类目不一致")
    if len(note) > MAX_NOTE_CHARS:
        raise PromptLabError("note_too_long", f"说明不能超过 {MAX_NOTE_CHARS} 字")
    items = list(baseline_set.items)
    if baseline_item_ids is not None:
        wanted = list(dict.fromkeys(int(i) for i in baseline_item_ids))
        by_id = {item.id: item for item in items}
        missing = [i for i in wanted if i not in by_id]
        if missing:
            raise PromptLabError("baseline_items_invalid", f"有 {len(missing)} 个条目不属于这个基准集：{missing[:20]}")
        items = [by_id[i] for i in wanted]
    if not items:
        raise PromptLabError("baseline_items_empty", "没有可跑的条目")
    # ls-review-r23 R2：同一个 client_request_id 只认同一份请求（机制、集合、实际条目），
    # 请求记录落在唯一键的审计事件上；内容不同一律 409，不能把没跑过的样本说成已跑。
    request_digest = _digest({
        "workflow_version_id": version.id,
        "workflow_hash": version.canonical_hash,
        "baseline_set_id": baseline_set.id,
        "baseline_item_ids": sorted(item.id for item in items),
    })
    request_key = f"prompt-lab:request:{client_request_id}" if client_request_id is not None else None
    if request_key is not None:
        prior = db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.event_key == request_key))
        if prior is not None:
            recorded = _json(prior.payload_json)
            recorded = recorded if isinstance(recorded, dict) else {}
            existing = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, recorded.get("run_id")) if recorded.get("run_id") else None
            if recorded.get("request_digest") != request_digest or existing is None:
                raise PromptLabError("client_request_id_reused", "client_request_id 已用于另一份请求（机制、基准集或条目不同）", status_code=409)
            return {**run_summary(existing), "created": False}
    key = f"prompt-lab:{client_request_id or uuid.uuid4().hex}:{request_digest[:16]}"
    active = db.scalar(
        select(func.count()).select_from(BaselineRegressionRun).where(
            BaselineRegressionRun.created_by == LAB_ACTOR,
            BaselineRegressionRun.status == "running",
        )
    ) or 0
    if active >= MAX_ACTIVE_RUNS:
        raise PromptLabError("lab_run_in_progress", "实验室已有一轮回归在跑，等它结束或先取消", status_code=409)
    now = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    day_start = now.astimezone(timezone(timedelta(hours=8))).replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
    used = db.scalar(
        select(func.coalesce(func.sum(BaselineRegressionRun.total), 0)).where(
            BaselineRegressionRun.created_by == LAB_ACTOR,
            BaselineRegressionRun.created_at >= day_start.astimezone(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None),
        )
    ) or 0
    cap = daily_item_cap()
    if used + len(items) > cap:
        raise PromptLabError(
            "lab_daily_cap_reached",
            f"今天（东八区）实验室已跑 {used} 条，本轮 {len(items)} 条会超过每日上限 {cap} 条",
            status_code=429,
        )
    try:
        run = create_native_baseline_run(
            db,
            baseline_set=baseline_set,
            workflow_version=version,
            actor=LAB_ACTOR,
            idempotency_key=key,
            items=items,
            model_execution_mode=MODEL_EXECUTION_MODE,
            real_execution_confirmed=MODEL_EXECUTION_MODE == "real",
            inference_mode="online",
            evaluation_purpose="golden_calibration",
        )
    except NativeBaselineRegressionError as exc:
        raise PromptLabError(exc.code, str(exc), status_code=exc.status_code) from exc
    append_audit_event(
        db,
        category="prompt_lab",
        action="run_created",
        subject_type="baseline_regression_run",
        subject_id=run.id,
        actor=LAB_ACTOR,
        payload={
            "baseline_set_id": baseline_set.id,
            "workflow_version_id": version.id,
            "workflow_canonical_hash": version.canonical_hash,
            "total": run.total,
            "partial": baseline_item_ids is not None,
            "note": note,
            "daily_used_before": int(used),
            "daily_cap": cap,
        },
        event_key=f"prompt-lab:run:{run.id}",
    )
    if request_key is not None:
        append_audit_event(
            db,
            category="prompt_lab",
            action="run_request",
            subject_type="baseline_regression_run",
            subject_id=run.id,
            actor=LAB_ACTOR,
            payload={"request_digest": request_digest, "run_id": run.id, "client_request_id": client_request_id},
            event_key=request_key,
        )
    return {**run_summary(run), "created": True}


def _require_run(db: Session, run_id: int) -> BaselineRegressionRun:
    run = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, run_id)
    if run is None:
        raise PromptLabError("run_not_found", "回归不存在", status_code=404)
    if run.category_key not in allowed_categories(db):
        raise PromptLabError("category_not_allowed", "这个回归不在实验室开放的类目内", status_code=403)
    return run


def run_summary(run: BaselineRegressionRun) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": run.id,
        "baseline_set_id": run.baseline_set_id,
        "workflow_version_id": run.workflow_version_id,
        "category_key": run.category_key,
        "status": run.status,
        "total": run.total,
        "completed": run.completed,
        "failed": run.failed,
        "valid_predictions": run.valid_predictions,
        "created_by": run.created_by,
        "is_lab_run": run.created_by == LAB_ACTOR,
        "created_at": _iso(run.created_at),
        "finished_at": _iso(run.finished_at),
    }


def get_run(db: Session, run_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return run_summary(_require_run(db, run_id))


def list_runs(db: Session, *, limit: int = 30, lab_only: bool = False) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    query = select(BaselineRegressionRun).where(BaselineRegressionRun.category_key.in_(sorted(allowed_categories(db))))
    if lab_only:
        query = query.where(BaselineRegressionRun.created_by == LAB_ACTOR)
    rows = db.scalars(query.order_by(BaselineRegressionRun.id.desc()).limit(max(1, min(limit, 200))))
    return [run_summary(run) for run in rows]


def list_baseline_sets(db: Session) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    rows = db.execute(
        select(BaselineSet, func.count(BaselineSetItem.id))
        .join(BaselineSetItem, BaselineSetItem.baseline_set_id == BaselineSet.id, isouter=True)
        .where(BaselineSet.category_key.in_(sorted(allowed_categories(db))))
        .group_by(BaselineSet.id)
        .order_by(BaselineSet.id)
    ).all()
    return [
        {"id": row.id, "name": row.name, "category_key": row.category_key, "items": int(count)}
        for row, count in rows
    ]


def run_items(db: Session, run_id: int, *, offset: int = 0, limit: int = 200) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """逐条：模型输出、原始输出、跑时冻结的真值、当前最新真值。怎么算准确率由调用方自己定。"""

    from .baseline_truth import BaselineTruthError, effective_truth_from_latest

    run = _require_run(db, run_id)
    limit = max(1, min(limit, MAX_ITEMS_PAGE))
    offset = max(0, offset)
    total = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(BaselineRegressionItem).where(BaselineRegressionItem.run_id == run.id)) or 0
    # ls-review-r23 S2：按页批量取基准条目、图片与最新真值修订，不逐条懒加载。
    rows = db.scalars(
        select(BaselineRegressionItem)
        .where(BaselineRegressionItem.run_id == run.id)
        .options(selectinload(BaselineRegressionItem.baseline_set_item), selectinload(BaselineRegressionItem.asset))
        .order_by(BaselineRegressionItem.id)
        .offset(offset)
        .limit(limit)
    ).all()
    set_item_ids = sorted({row.baseline_set_item_id for row in rows})
    latest_by_item: dict[int, BaselineTruthRevision] = {}
    if set_item_ids:
        newest = (
            select(
                BaselineTruthRevision.baseline_set_item_id.label("item_id"),
                func.max(BaselineTruthRevision.revision).label("revision"),
            )
            .where(BaselineTruthRevision.baseline_set_item_id.in_(set_item_ids))
            .group_by(BaselineTruthRevision.baseline_set_item_id)
            .subquery()
        )
        for revision in db.scalars(
            select(BaselineTruthRevision).join(
                newest,
                and_(
                    BaselineTruthRevision.baseline_set_item_id == newest.c.item_id,
                    BaselineTruthRevision.revision == newest.c.revision,
                ),
            )
        ):
            latest_by_item[revision.baseline_set_item_id] = revision
    items = []
    for row in rows:
        snapshot = _json(row.result_snapshot_json)
        snapshot = snapshot if isinstance(snapshot, dict) else {}
        set_item = row.baseline_set_item
        try:
            truth = effective_truth_from_latest(set_item, latest_by_item.get(set_item.id))
            current_truth = {
                "revision": truth.revision,
                "source": truth.source,
                "truth_contract_key": truth.truth_contract_key,
                "expected_result": truth.expected_result,
            }
        except BaselineTruthError as exc:
            current_truth = {"error": exc.code}
        items.append({
            "item_id": row.id,
            "baseline_set_item_id": row.baseline_set_item_id,
            "asset_id": row.asset_id,
            "file_name": row.asset.original_name if row.asset is not None else None,
            "truth_contract_key": row.truth_contract_key,
            "status": row.status,
            "error_message": row.error_message or None,
            "prediction": snapshot.get("normalized_result"),
            "raw_output": snapshot.get("stage_a"),
            "output_contract_versions": [
                {"node_id": node_id, "contract_key": result.get("output_contract_key"),
                 "contract_hash": result.get("output_contract_hash")}
                for node_id, result in (snapshot.get("workflow_node_outputs") or {}).items()
                if isinstance(result, dict) and result.get("output_contract_key")
            ],
            "truth_at_run": _json(row.expected_result_json),
            "current_truth": current_truth,
        })
    return {"run": run_summary(run), "offset": offset, "limit": limit, "total": int(total), "items": items}


def cancel_run(db: Session, run_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from .native_baseline_regression import cancel_native_baseline_runs

    run = _require_run(db, run_id)
    if run.created_by != LAB_ACTOR:
        raise PromptLabError("run_not_owned", "只能取消实验室自己发起的回归", status_code=403)
    if run.status != "running":
        return {**run_summary(run), "cancelled_items": 0}
    affected = cancel_native_baseline_runs(db, baseline_run_ids=[run.id])
    append_audit_event(
        db,
        category="prompt_lab",
        action="run_cancelled",
        subject_type="baseline_regression_run",
        subject_id=run.id,
        actor=LAB_ACTOR,
        payload={"cancelled_items": affected},
        event_key=f"prompt-lab:run:{run.id}:cancel",
    )
    db.flush()
    db.refresh(run)
    return {**run_summary(run), "cancelled_items": affected}


def require_asset(db: Session, asset_id: int) -> Asset:
    """只放出开放类目基准集里的图。"""

    asset = db.get(Asset, asset_id)
    if asset is None:
        raise PromptLabError("asset_not_found", "图片不存在", status_code=404)
    in_allowed_set = db.scalar(
        select(BaselineSetItem.id)
        .join(BaselineSet, BaselineSet.id == BaselineSetItem.baseline_set_id)
        .where(
            BaselineSetItem.asset_id == asset_id,
            BaselineSet.category_key.in_(sorted(allowed_categories(db))),
        )
        .limit(1)
    )
    if in_allowed_set is None:
        raise PromptLabError("asset_not_allowed", "这张图不在实验室开放类目的基准集里", status_code=403)
    return asset

===== FILE backend/app/prompt_lab_api.py =====
"""提示词实验室接口：只认独立的机器令牌（``secrets/prompt-lab.token``），不认页面登录。

令牌只能访问本前缀；本前缀也只做 ``prompt_lab`` 里那几件事。见 ``GET /api/prompt-lab/guide``。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hmac
from typing import Any, Callable

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Header, HTTPException, Query
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse, PlainTextResponse
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, ConfigDict
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .database import get_db
from .prompt_lab import (
    DEFAULT_DAILY_ITEM_CAP,
    LabWriteLock,
    MAX_ACTIVE_RUNS,
    MAX_ITEMS_PAGE,
    MAX_PROMPT_CHARS,
    PromptEdit,
    PromptLabError,
    cancel_run,
    create_candidate,
    daily_item_cap,
    describe_mechanism,
    get_run,
    list_baseline_sets,
    list_mechanisms_page,
    list_runs,
    require_asset,
    run_items,
    start_run,
)

MIN_TOKEN_BYTES = 32

GUIDE = f"""# 提示词实验室（给 Codex 用）

认证：每个请求带 `Authorization: Bearer <令牌>`。令牌只能用在 /api/prompt-lab/ 下。
开放类目：平台所有已登记且未退役的类目，见 capabilities。所有时间是东八区。

## 能做的事
1. GET  /api/prompt-lab/mechanisms?limit=30       列出可用评测机制版本（按 id 倒序）
   - 返回 next_before_id 时说明还有更早的版本，带上 before_id=<它> 接着翻；为空表示已经到头。
   - scan_capped=true：这次检查的版本数到了上限，还没凑够 limit 条，列表不完整，要接着翻，不能当成"没有了"。
2. GET  /api/prompt-lab/mechanisms/{{id}}            读四路提示词原文、路由条件、输出合同、canonical_hash
3. POST /api/prompt-lab/candidates                 修改提示词及输出字段定义，生成候选机制（同样的内容返回同一个版本）
   body: {{"source_version_id": 475, "expected_source_hash": "<读到的 canonical_hash>",
          "prompts": {{"prompt_overseas_whole": {{"system_prompt": "...", "user_prompt": "..."}}}},
          "note": "改了什么、为什么"}}
   - 只传要改的节点；system_prompt / user_prompt 不传就沿用原文。
   - 每段最多 {MAX_PROMPT_CHARS} 字；不能出现源提示词里没有的 {{{{占位符}}}}。
   - 可选 output_schemas：以提示词 node_id 为键、完整 JSON Schema 为值；也可只改字段而不改提示词。
   - 从 GET mechanisms/id 的 prompt_nodes[].output_contract.output_schema 复制后修改，不能只传新增字段。
   - 可增加字段（含嵌套对象、数组、布尔值）、修改扩展字段的枚举；基础字段不能删除或改变规则。
   - 所有对象须 required 覆盖全部属性、additionalProperties=false。支持 type/title/description/enum/properties/required/additionalProperties/items；不支持 $ref、代码或自定义校验器。
   - 每份 Schema 最多 32 KB、6 层、128 字段；同一路线的下游预览定义自动同步。返回 schema_changed_nodes 和 output_schema_changes。
   - 字段定义随候选冻结，以 contract_hash 标识；contract_key 仍标识基础路线，不能仅凭 key 判断字段版本。
   - 新字段保存在测试结果/写入预览；不会修改业务表、人工参考答案或自动改变选路。新增字段的正确性需另行验收。
   - 此便捷接口保持模型与路由不变；需要修改结构、模型或路由时使用下面的独立草稿接口。正式绑定始终不变。
4. GET  /api/prompt-lab/baseline-sets              可用的基准集（样本）
5. POST /api/prompt-lab/runs                       用冻结机制及其模型在基准集上跑一轮真实回归
   body: {{"workflow_version_id": 1234, "baseline_set_id": 31, "baseline_item_ids": [可选，只跑子集],
          "client_request_id": "可选，重试时带同一个就不会重复建", "note": "..."}}
   - 候选和源机制（对照）都可以跑；同一时间实验室只能有 {MAX_ACTIVE_RUNS} 轮在跑，同一基准集也只能有一轮在跑。
   - 每天（东八区）实验室最多跑 {DEFAULT_DAILY_ITEM_CAP} 条（以服务端配置为准）。耗时与所选模型和工作流有关，不保证固定时长。
6. GET  /api/prompt-lab/runs?lab_only=true         列出回归（也能看到运营在页面上跑的知识图谱回归，比如纠偏轮）
7. GET  /api/prompt-lab/runs/{{id}}                  进度：status=running/completed/partial_failed/failed
8. GET  /api/prompt-lab/runs/{{id}}/items?offset=0&limit={MAX_ITEMS_PAGE}   逐条结果（每页最多 {MAX_ITEMS_PAGE} 条）
9. POST /api/prompt-lab/runs/{{id}}/cancel           取消实验室自己发起、还在跑的回归
10. GET /api/prompt-lab/assets/{{asset_id}}/file      看原图（只限可用类目基准集里的图）

## 存量配置、样本和复核
- GET /catalog：可选类目、已登记模型、提示词与规则版本；不返回凭据。组件目录最多500条。
- GET /mechanisms/{{id}}/manifest：完整工作流、source_version_id、source_hash。
- PUT /drafts/{{key}}：传 source_version_id、expected_source_hash、expected_revision（首次0）、manifest、note。key为1–80位字母数字点横线。存入独立草稿，不覆盖页面共用工作副本；相同请求可重放，冲突409。
- GET /drafts/{{key}}：读取最新修订。可修改节点、边、模型登记ID、规则版本和输出结构；不能改变源类目。
- POST /drafts/{{key}}/freeze：传 revision。完整校验后冻结新机制，返回 workflow_version_id；不会切换生产绑定。历史机制不变。
- POST /components：传 source_asset_id、content（完整组件内容）、request_id。基于现有组件类型创建独立提示词或规则候选，返回新asset_key和版本引用；在草稿中使用新引用，绝不覆盖源组件。
- POST /baseline-sets/clone：传 source_set_id、item_ids、name、request_id。复制选定已有素材形成新样本集，冻结复制时参考答案；原样本集不改。每次最多10000条。
- POST /baseline-sets/import：传 category_key、name、request_id、items。每条含 asset_id 和 expected_level，或 expected_result＋truth_contract_key，可附 workflow_input。只引用已有素材；整批校验失败回滚，不改素材或原集。
- GET /baseline-sets/{{id}}/items?offset=0&limit=100：分页读取样本和有效参考答案，next_offset为空表示结束。
- GET /baseline-sets/{{id}}/items/{{item_id}}/truth：读取当前参考答案和canonical_hash。
- POST 同一truth地址：传 expected_hash、expected_result或expected_level、reason、request_id、confirmed=true。先展示差异，明确确认后提交；并发变化409，旧版本保留。该确认记为API调用方声明，不冒称某位员工亲自复核。仅模型建议不得调用此接口当作人工确认。
- POST /runs/{{id}}/control：传 item_ids（本轮条目ID，最多200）、action（pause/resume/retry/cancel）、request_id。只控制原生存量子运行；拒绝混入增量任务。仍有在途调用时整批409，等待完成再重试，不强停供应商调用。成功请求重放不重复执行。
- 结果导出：分页读取 /runs/{{id}}/items，保存完整JSON；不要只保存第一页。补跑可用control的retry保留成功节点，也可用原机制及失败条目在 /runs 发起独立子集回归。
- 所有request_id为8–80位字母数字点横线，同键不同内容409。存量运行固定dry_run，只保存测试结果与写入预览，不能向业务下游投递。

## 逐条结果里的字段
- prediction：这一轮的结果行。注意：运营在页面上纠偏会直接改这一行，所以纠偏轮（例如 run 512）里 prediction 已经是纠偏后的值。
- raw_output：模型原始输出（stage_a），纠偏不会改它。实验室自己的轮次没人纠偏，两者相同。
- truth_at_run：跑这一轮时冻结的真值。
- current_truth：此刻的最新真值（运营可能还在纠偏，会变）。
- truth_contract_key：链路，kg.knowledge_graph.<domestic|overseas>.<whole|single>.v1。
准确率怎么算（逐字段/整张、大小写、中英文、多值顺序、要不要排除某些字段）由你们自己定，平台不替你们算。
比较两个机制时，建议在同一批条目上同时跑候选和源机制，用同一份真值比。

## 不能做的事
改增量开关、生产绑定、线上共用配置、凭据；生产投递、任意SQL和代码执行。
不能覆盖已冻结版本、硬删历史或绕过工作流校验。新类目的实际执行能力以工作流校验为准。
正式增量采纳上线仍走独立发布流程。API提供已有引擎能力，不会因添加字段自动增加引擎尚不支持的分流语义。
"""


def _http(exc: PromptLabError) -> HTTPException:
    return HTTPException(status_code=exc.status_code, detail={"code": exc.code, "message": str(exc)})


class PromptTextEdit(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    system_prompt: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=MAX_PROMPT_CHARS)
    user_prompt: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=MAX_PROMPT_CHARS)


class CandidateRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    source_version_id: int = Field(gt=0)
    expected_source_hash: str = Field(min_length=64, max_length=64, pattern="^[0-9a-f]{64}$")
    prompts: dict[str, PromptTextEdit] = Field(default_factory=dict, max_length=20)
    output_schemas: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = Field(default_factory=dict, max_length=20)
    note: str = Field(default="", max_length=2000)


class RunRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    workflow_version_id: int = Field(gt=0)
    baseline_set_id: int = Field(gt=0)
    baseline_item_ids: list[int] | None = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=5000)
    client_request_id: str | None = Field(default=None, min_length=8, max_length=80, pattern="^[A-Za-z0-9._:-]+$")
    note: str = Field(default="", max_length=2000)


def build_prompt_lab_router(settings_provider: Callable[[], Any]) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/prompt-lab", tags=["prompt-lab"])

    def lab_client(authorization: str | None = Header(default=None)) -> str:
        configured = settings_provider().prompt_lab_token
        if not configured or len(configured.encode("utf-8")) < MIN_TOKEN_BYTES:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="提示词实验室未开通")
        prefix = "Bearer "
        supplied = authorization[len(prefix):] if authorization and authorization.startswith(prefix) else ""
        if not supplied or not hmac.compare_digest(supplied.encode("utf-8"), configured.encode("utf-8")):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="提示词实验室认证失败", headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"})
        return "prompt-lab"

    def call(db: Session, action: Callable[[], Any], *, write: bool = False) -> Any:
        if not write:
            try:
                return action()
            except PromptLabError as exc:
                raise _http(exc) from exc
        try:
            # 写操作全局串行：锁在本请求第一次读库之前拿，提交之后才放（见 LabWriteLock）。
            with LabWriteLock(db):
                try:
                    result = action()
                    db.commit()
                except BaseException:
                    db.rollback()
                    raise
        except PromptLabError as exc:
            raise _http(exc) from exc
        return result

    @router.get("/capabilities")
    def capabilities(_client: str = Depends(lab_client), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        from .prompt_lab import allowed_categories
        return {"categories": sorted(allowed_categories(db)),
                "permissions": ["mechanisms:read", "candidates:prompt_write", "candidates:output_schema_write", "drafts:write", "drafts:freeze", "components:create_isolated", "baseline_sets:clone", "baseline_sets:import", "truth:write_confirmed", "regressions:run", "regressions:read", "regressions:cancel_own", "regressions:control_stock"],
                "production_activation": False, "truth_write": True,
                "production_write": False, "shared_config_write": False,
                "truth_confirmation_source": "api_client_assertion",
                "schema_limits": {"max_bytes": 32768, "max_depth": 6, "max_fields": 128},
                "schema_version_identity": "contract_hash"}

    @router.get("/guide", response_class=PlainTextResponse)
    def guide(_client: str = Depends(lab_client)) -> str:
        return GUIDE.replace(str(DEFAULT_DAILY_ITEM_CAP), str(daily_item_cap()), 1)

    @router.get("/mechanisms")
    def mechanisms(
        limit: int = Query(default=30, ge=1, le=100),
        before_id: int | None = Query(default=None, ge=1),
        _client: str = Depends(lab_client),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return call(db, lambda: list_mechanisms_page(db, limit=limit, before_id=before_id))

    @router.get("/mechanisms/{version_id}")
    def mechanism(version_id: int, _client: str = Depends(lab_client), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return call(db, lambda: describe_mechanism(db, version_id))

    @router.post("/candidates", status_code=201)
    def candidate(payload: CandidateRequest, _client: str = Depends(lab_client), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        edits = {
            node_id: PromptEdit(system_prompt=edit.system_prompt, user_prompt=edit.user_prompt)
            for node_id, edit in payload.prompts.items()
        }
        receipt = call(
            db,
            lambda: create_candidate(
                db,
                source_version_id=payload.source_version_id,
                expected_source_hash=payload.expected_source_hash,
                edits=edits,
                output_schemas=payload.output_schemas,
                note=payload.note,
            ),
            write=True,
        )
        return {**receipt, "mechanism": call(db, lambda: describe_mechanism(db, receipt["candidate_version_id"]))}

    @router.get("/baseline-sets")
    def baseline_sets(_client: str = Depends(lab_client), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"items": call(db, lambda: list_baseline_sets(db))}

    @router.post("/runs", status_code=201)
    def create_run(payload: RunRequest, _client: str = Depends(lab_client), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return call(
            db,
            lambda: start_run(
                db,
                workflow_version_id=payload.workflow_version_id,
                baseline_set_id=payload.baseline_set_id,
                baseline_item_ids=payload.baseline_item_ids,
                client_request_id=payload.client_request_id,
                note=payload.note,
            ),
            write=True,
        )

    @router.get("/runs")
    def runs(
        limit: int = Query(default=30, ge=1, le=200),
        lab_only: bool = Query(default=False),
        _client: str = Depends(lab_client),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"items": call(db, lambda: list_runs(db, limit=limit, lab_only=lab_only))}

    @router.get("/runs/{run_id}")
    def run(run_id: int, _client: str = Depends(lab_client), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return call(db, lambda: get_run(db, run_id))

    @router.get("/runs/{run_id}/items")
    def items(
        run_id: int,
        offset: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
        limit: int = Query(default=200, ge=1, le=MAX_ITEMS_PAGE),
        _client: str = Depends(lab_client),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return call(db, lambda: run_items(db, run_id, offset=offset, limit=limit))

    @router.post("/runs/{run_id}/cancel")
    def cancel(run_id: int, _client: str = Depends(lab_client), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return call(db, lambda: cancel_run(db, run_id), write=True)

    @router.get("/assets/{asset_id}/file")
    def asset_file(asset_id: int, _client: str = Depends(lab_client), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> FileResponse:
        from .nas_storage import NasStorageError, resolve_asset_path

        asset = call(db, lambda: require_asset(db, asset_id))
        try:
            path = resolve_asset_path(asset, settings_provider())
        except NasStorageError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="图片文件不可用") from exc
        if not path.is_file():
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="图片文件缺失")
        return FileResponse(path, media_type=asset.mime_type, filename=asset.original_name)

    from .stock_lab_api import install_stock_routes
    install_stock_routes(router, lab_client, call)
    return router

===== FILE backend/app/prompt_loader.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PromptPair:
    system: str
    user: str


def _extract(source: str, start_marker: str, end_marker: str) -> str:
    try:
        start = source.index(start_marker) + len(start_marker)
        end = source.index(end_marker, start)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise RuntimeError(f"提示词文件缺少段落：{start_marker.strip()}") from exc
    return source[start:end].strip()


def load_prompt_pairs(path: Path) -> dict[str, PromptPair]:
    source = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    system_a = _extract(source, "### System Prompt A\n", "### User Prompt A")
    user_a = _extract(source, "### User Prompt A\n", "\n---")
    system_b = _extract(source, "### System Prompt B\n", "### User Prompt B")
    user_b = _extract(source, "### User Prompt B\n", "\n---\n\n## 三、外部评分引擎规则")
    return {
        "A": PromptPair(system=system_a, user=user_a),
        "B": PromptPair(system=system_b, user=user_b),
    }


def load_standalone_prompt(path: Path) -> PromptPair:
    source = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    system = _extract(source, "### System Prompt\n", "### User Prompt")
    try:
        user = source.split("### User Prompt\n", 1)[1].strip()
    except IndexError as exc:
        raise RuntimeError("提示词文件缺少 User Prompt") from exc
    return PromptPair(system=system, user=user)

===== FILE backend/app/prompt_metrics.py =====
"""Immutable prompt-version accuracy calculations over frozen evaluations."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from typing import Any

from .dimension_deduction_bridge import dimension_result_map
from .models import EvaluationResult, HumanReview, ReviewPanel


def frozen_task_set_hash(evaluation_ids: list[int]) -> str:
    canonical = json.dumps(
        sorted(evaluation_ids),
        separators=(",", ":"),
    )
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def final_review(
    result: EvaluationResult,
    panels_by_evaluation: dict[int, ReviewPanel],
    reviews_by_id: dict[int, HumanReview],
) -> HumanReview | None:
    panel = panels_by_evaluation.get(result.id)
    if panel is not None:
        if panel.status != "completed" or panel.final_review_id is None:
            return None
        return reviews_by_id.get(panel.final_review_id)
    if result.review_stage != "completed":
        return None
    legacy = [
        review
        for review in result.reviews
        if review.panel_id is None
    ]
    return legacy[-1] if legacy else None


def calculate_prompt_metrics(
    results: list[EvaluationResult],
    *,
    panels_by_evaluation: dict[int, ReviewPanel],
    reviews_by_id: dict[int, HumanReview],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not results:
        raise ValueError("冻结任务集至少需要一条评测结果")
    reviewed: list[tuple[EvaluationResult, HumanReview]] = []
    for result in results:
        review = final_review(
            result,
            panels_by_evaluation,
            reviews_by_id,
        )
        if review is not None:
            reviewed.append((result, review))

    corrected_sample_count = sum(
        1 for _result, review in reviewed
